GLOBAL OUTBREAK

CURRENT ARCHIVE • EPISODE 7 — PART 9
# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 1 — THE BEGINNING

### PART 1 — A NORMAL WORLD

---

### **[SCENE 1 — BLACK SCREEN]**

*Complete darkness.*

*No music.*

*No voices.*

For a few seconds, there is nothing.

Then—

**SFX: DISTANT CITY TRAFFIC**

*Cars passing.*

*Distant horns.*

*People talking.*

*A faint breeze.*

The screen remains black.

Then—

**FADE IN.**

---

### **[SCENE 2 — NEW YORK CITY | MORNING]**

*Aerial shot of New York City.*

Tall skyscrapers rise above the streets.

Cars move through busy roads.

People walk along sidewalks, carrying coffee, bags and phones.

The city is alive.

Everything is normal.

The camera moves between the buildings before slowly rising toward the sky.

A commercial aircraft passes overhead.

But behind it—

**another aircraft appears.**

A sleek private jet.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — PRIVATE JET | ABOVE NEW YORK]**

*Inside the private jet.*

The cabin is quiet.

Large windows reveal New York far below.

Sitting beside the window is **Michael Carter**.

He looks outside calmly.

Across the cabin sits his driver.

The driver checks the aircraft's systems.

**DRIVER**
Everything's going smoothly, Mr. Carter.

**Michael Carter**
Good.

Michael looks toward the cockpit.

He seems completely comfortable inside the aircraft.

The driver notices.

**DRIVER**
You really never get tired of flying this thing?

Michael turns toward him.

**Michael Carter**
It's better than sitting in traffic.

The driver laughs.

Michael smiles slightly and looks back through the window.

The jet continues through the clouds.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — CALIFORNIA | HARRY'S HOUSE | MORNING]**

*A peaceful suburban neighbourhood.*

Cars are parked along the street.

Trees move gently in the wind.

One house stands quietly among the others.

Inside—

**LOUD NOISES.**

**Harry Wilson (O.S.)**
THAT DOESN'T COUNT!

**David Anderson (O.S.)**
It absolutely counts!

**Harry Wilson (O.S.)**
NO, IT DOESN'T!

---

### **[SCENE 5 — HARRY'S HOUSE | LIVING ROOM]**

**Harry Wilson** and **David Anderson** sit together, playing a game.

Harry points toward the screen like he's presenting evidence in court.

**Harry Wilson**
Bro, you literally changed the rules!

David looks at him with complete disbelief.

**David Anderson**
I changed nothing.

**Harry Wilson**
You did!

**David Anderson**
Then explain exactly what I changed.

Harry pauses.

He looks at the screen.

Then at David.

**Harry Wilson**
...I don't know.

David smirks.

**David Anderson**
Exactly.

**Harry Wilson**
That proves nothing!

David laughs.

Harry grabs a cushion and throws it at him.

**THUD!**

David catches it.

**David Anderson**
Very mature.

**Harry Wilson**
Thank you.

David throws the cushion back.

Harry ducks.

**THUMP!**

The cushion hits the sofa.

Both start laughing.

The house is otherwise quiet.

Harry's parents have gone out.

Just the two best friends remain.

Harry looks at the clock.

**Harry Wilson**
Your turn.

**David Anderson**
Nope.

**Harry Wilson**
Why?

**David Anderson**
Because you lost.

Harry stares at him.

**Harry Wilson**
We're debating this.

**David Anderson**
We've been debating it for ten minutes.

**Harry Wilson**
Then we're not done.

David shakes his head.

---

### **[SCENE 6 — HAWAII | SCHOOL | MORNING]**

*A bright Hawaiian morning.*

Palm trees sway outside a school building.

Students move through the halls.

Teachers walk between classrooms.

Lockers open and close.

Then—

**SFX: SCHOOL BELL**

Students begin moving toward their classrooms.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — SCHOOL HALLWAY]**

**Chris Bennett** walks through the hallway beside his younger sister, **Emma Bennett**.

Emma suddenly looks toward something on the other side of the hallway.

Chris notices.

**Chris Bennett**
Emma.

She doesn't respond.

**Chris Bennett**
Emma.

She finally turns.

**Emma Bennett**
What?

**Chris Bennett**
We're going this way.

Chris points down the hallway.

Emma looks in the opposite direction.

**Emma Bennett**
I know.

Chris raises an eyebrow.

**Chris Bennett**
Then why were you going the other way?

Emma smiles.

**Emma Bennett**
I wasn't.

Chris sighs.

Emma walks ahead.

Chris follows.

The school continues normally around them.

Students laugh.

Someone runs down the hallway.

A teacher immediately tells them to slow down.

Everything is ordinary.

---

### **[SCENE 8 — SEOUL, SOUTH KOREA | PARK | MORNING]**

*A peaceful park in Seoul.*

Trees sway gently.

People walk along the paths.

Children play nearby.

Cyclists pass.

The sounds of the city are distant.

**Do Do-Hee** sits quietly on a bench.

She looks around.

Nothing unusual.

A bird lands nearby.

Do Do-Hee watches it for a moment.

Then she looks toward the city.

Seoul stretches beyond the trees.

Traffic moves normally.

People continue with their day.

Do Do-Hee simply sits there.

Peacefully.

---

### **[SCENE 9 — DAEGU, SOUTH KOREA | OLYMPIAD TEST HALL | MORNING]**

*Silence.*

Rows of students sit at desks.

Every student is focused on their test.

Pens move across paper.

**SFX: SCRATCH... SCRATCH... SCRATCH...**

**Kim Wong-jun** sits among the students.

He reads a question carefully.

He thinks.

Then begins writing.

His eyes move between the question and his answer sheet.

The room remains completely silent.

A supervisor walks slowly between the rows.

Kim Wong-jun continues working.

No distractions.

No conversation.

Just the test.

---

### **[SCENE 10 — MUMBAI, INDIA | STREET | MORNING]**

*The atmosphere changes.*

Busy streets.

Autos.

Cars.

Motorcycles.

People walking in every direction.

Street vendors call out to customers.

The smell of food fills the air.

At a street-side food stall sits **Kartik Sharma**.

In front of him—

A full plate of **chhole bhature**.

Kartik takes a bite.

He stops.

His face immediately shows satisfaction.

**Kartik Sharma**
Mmm.

He takes another bite.

A man walking past looks at the plate.

**MAN**
Good?

Kartik looks at him.

**Kartik Sharma**
Very.

He takes another bite.

The man smiles and continues walking.

Kartik returns to his food.

Mumbai continues around him.

Traffic.

Voices.

Food stalls.

People.

Normal life.

---

### **[SCENE 11 — MONTAGE | DIFFERENT PARTS OF THE WORLD]**

**NEW YORK.**

Michael Carter's private jet moves through the sky.

---

**CALIFORNIA.**

Harry Wilson and David Anderson continue their argument.

**Harry Wilson**
I'm telling you, I won.

**David Anderson**
You lost.

**Harry Wilson**
I won.

---

**HAWAII.**

Chris Bennett and Emma Bennett walk toward their classroom.

---

**SEOUL.**

Do Do-Hee remains peacefully in the park.

---

**DAEGU.**

Kim Wong-jun continues his Olympiad test.

---

**MUMBAI.**

Kartik Sharma takes another bite of his chhole bhature.

---

*Six locations.*

*Eight people.*

*Completely different lives.*

*One ordinary day.*

---

### **[SCENE 12 — ABOVE THE CLOUDS]**

*Back inside Michael Carter's private jet.*

Michael looks through the window.

Clouds stretch endlessly below.

Sunlight reflects across the glass.

He leans back.

The driver looks ahead.

Everything is calm.

No danger.

No panic.

Nothing unusual.

Just another day.

---

### **[SCENE 13 — EARTH | SPACE]**

The camera slowly pulls away from the jet.

Farther.

And farther.

The aircraft becomes smaller.

The clouds become smaller.

The atmosphere comes into view.

Then—

**Earth.**

Blue.

Beautiful.

Quiet.

The camera continues pulling back.

The entire planet fills the screen.

For a moment—

**silence.**

Then the title appears.

# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

*The title remains on screen.*

**FADE TO BLACK.**

## **END OF PART 1**




# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 1 — THE BEGINNING

### PART 2 — THE RESEARCH

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | RESEARCH FACILITY | MORNING]**

*Black screen.*

**SFX: LOW ELECTRONIC HUM**

*The sound of machinery slowly becomes louder.*

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

*Fade in.*

A massive high-security research facility stands in the German countryside.

Tall fences surround the property.

Security cameras monitor every entrance.

A large sign is fixed beside the main gate.*

> **ADVANCED BIOLOGICAL RESEARCH FACILITY**

*A security vehicle passes through the gate.*

The camera follows it toward the enormous laboratory building.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — RESEARCH LABORATORY | MORNING]**

*Inside the facility.*

Scientists in white laboratory coats move between workstations.

Computers display graphs, biological data and microscopic images.

Glass chambers contain samples under controlled conditions.

The atmosphere is professional and quiet.

At one workstation stands **Mr. William Brown**.

He carefully examines a sample through a microscope.

He adjusts the focus.

Looks at the monitor.

Then smiles slightly.

A voice comes from behind him.

**John Wright (O.S.)**
You've been staring at that thing for almost an hour.

William doesn't turn around.

**William Brown**
Because it keeps changing.

**John Wright**
And that's supposed to be good?

William finally turns.

Standing behind him is **Mr. John Wright**.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
For this experiment?

Yes.

John looks at the equipment.

**John Wright**
And for the rest of humanity?

William laughs.

**William Brown**
Hopefully even better.

John pulls up a chair.

**John Wright**
So?

William points toward the monitor.

**William Brown**
The virus is responding exactly the way we predicted.

John looks at the screen.

**John Wright**
Meaning?

**William Brown**
It targets mosquito cells.

John raises an eyebrow.

**John Wright**
Only mosquito cells?

William nods.

**William Brown**
That's the goal.

John looks impressed.

**John Wright**
So if this works...

William finishes his sentence.

**William Brown**
Fewer mosquitoes.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
Fewer mosquito-borne diseases.

William nods.

**William Brown**
Exactly.

John looks at him.

**John Wright**
You might actually pull this off.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
That's the plan.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — LABORATORY CORRIDOR]**

William and John walk through the facility together.

John looks through the glass windows of several laboratories.

**John Wright**
You know, when you told me you were working on a mosquito virus, I thought you had finally lost it.

William laughs.

**William Brown**
And yet you're still here.

**John Wright**
Someone has to make sure you don't blow up the laboratory.

William stops walking.

He looks at John.

**William Brown**
You'd miss me.

John immediately responds.

**John Wright**
I'd miss the free coffee.

William laughs.

The two continue walking.

Their friendship is obvious.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — RESEARCH ROOM]**

*William enters another laboratory.*

Several scientists are working on the virus.

A scientist approaches him with a tablet.

**SCIENTIST**
Mr. Brown.

William turns.

**William Brown**
Yes?

**SCIENTIST**
We've completed another test.

He hands William the tablet.

William reads the results.

His expression becomes slightly more serious.

**William Brown**
The mosquito cells?

**SCIENTIST**
Destroyed.

William looks at the data.

**William Brown**
And the other samples?

**SCIENTIST**
Unaffected.

William nods.

John looks over William's shoulder.

**John Wright**
So it really is selective.

**William Brown**
That's what we've been trying to achieve.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
Congratulations, Doctor.

William gives him a look.

**William Brown**
I'm not a doctor.

**John Wright**
You work in a laboratory.

**William Brown**
That's not how that works.

**John Wright**
Sounds like something a doctor would say.

William shakes his head.

The scientist quietly walks away.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — LABORATORY OBSERVATION ROOM]**

*William stands behind a glass wall.*

Inside is a controlled testing chamber.

A mosquito moves around inside.

William watches carefully.

John stands beside him.

**John Wright**
How long until the next stage?

**William Brown**
If everything goes according to plan, we'll begin larger-scale testing soon.

John nods.

**John Wright**
And after that?

William looks at the mosquito.

**William Brown**
If the results remain stable...

He pauses.

**William Brown**
...this could change a lot.

John looks at him.

**John Wright**
For the better.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
For the better.

---

### **[SCENE 6 — LABORATORY | DIFFERENT CORRIDOR]**

*Footsteps.*

Someone walks through the corridor.

**Sarah Smith.**

She carries a tablet and several documents.

She passes a glass window.

Inside, William and John are still working.

Sarah stops.

She looks through the glass.

Her expression is calm.

She watches them for several seconds.

William notices her.

He gives a polite nod.

Sarah returns the gesture.

Then continues walking.

The camera remains on Sarah for a moment.

Her expression gives nothing away.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — CAFETERIA | AFTERNOON]**

William and John sit across from each other at a table.

Two cups of coffee sit between them.

John takes a sip.

**John Wright**
This coffee is terrible.

William takes a sip.

He pauses.

**William Brown**
You're right.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
Finally. Something we agree on.

William laughs.

For a moment, they're simply two best friends having an ordinary conversation.

No experiments.

No danger.

No outbreak.

Just friendship.

---

### **[SCENE 8 — LABORATORY | AFTERNOON]**

*William returns to his workstation.*

He puts on his gloves.

Looks at the virus sample.

A small label is visible on the container.

**MOSQUITO-SPECIFIC VIRAL RESEARCH**

William carefully places the sample into a secure containment unit.

The machine locks.

**BEEP.**

**LOCKED.**

William looks satisfied.

He turns away.

The camera stays on the containment unit.

Inside—

The tiny sample remains completely still.

---

### **[SCENE 9 — CORRIDOR | CONTINUOUS]**

Sarah walks alone through the corridor.

She stops near a security door.

She looks toward the laboratory.

Her face remains expressionless.

She quietly enters her access code.

**BEEP.**

The door unlocks.

Sarah enters.

The door closes behind her.

**CLICK.**

---

### **[SCENE 10 — RESEARCH FACILITY | EVENING]**

*The sun begins to set outside the facility.*

The research building remains brightly lit.

Inside, scientists continue working.

William and John walk toward the exit.

**John Wright**
Coming back tomorrow?

William looks at him.

**William Brown**
Of course.

**John Wright**
Good.

John pats him on the shoulder.

**John Wright**
Don't stay up all night again.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
No promises.

John laughs.

They walk away together.

The camera slowly moves upward.

Outside, the research facility stands quietly beneath the evening sky.

Everything appears normal.

---

### **[SCENE 11 — INSIDE THE LABORATORY]**

*The facility is quieter now.*

Most of the scientists have left.

Lights remain on in several rooms.

A security camera slowly rotates.

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

The camera moves toward one particular corridor.

A figure stands at the end of it.

**Sarah Smith.**

She looks toward the secured laboratory.

Her expression is completely calm.

She doesn't move.

She simply watches.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

### **END OF PART 2**



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 1 — THE BEGINNING

### PART 3 — THE FINAL TEST

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | RESEARCH FACILITY | MORNING]**

*The laboratory is already active.*

Scientists move between workstations.

Computers display biological data.

Machines hum quietly.

**SFX: BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

Inside the main laboratory, **Mr. William Brown** stands beside a large observation chamber.

Beside him is **Mr. John Wright**.

Several scientists stand behind the glass.

Everyone is watching the same thing.

A small mosquito flies around inside the chamber.

William looks at the controls.

**SCIENTIST**
Everything is ready, Mr. Brown.

William nods.

**William Brown**
Begin the test.

The scientist presses a button.

**BEEP.**

A small amount of the virus is released into the controlled chamber.

The mosquito continues flying.

For several seconds—

Nothing happens.

John looks at William.

**John Wright**
Well...

William keeps watching.

**John Wright**
Maybe it decided it likes the virus.

William gives him a look.

**William Brown**
Give it a moment.

John smiles.

They continue watching.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — OBSERVATION CHAMBER]**

The mosquito begins flying slower.

It lands on the side of the chamber.

Its movements become weaker.

William watches carefully.

**William Brown**
There.

The mosquito moves again.

Then stops.

Silence.

John looks through the glass.

**John Wright**
Is it...?

The mosquito doesn't move.

A scientist checks the monitoring equipment.

**SCIENTIST**
No movement detected.

William looks at the results.

The data appears on the screen.

**TARGET SPECIES: MOSQUITO**

**RESULT: SUCCESS**

William slowly smiles.

**William Brown**
It worked.

John grins.

**John Wright**
You actually did it.

William looks at him.

**William Brown**
We did it.

John looks surprised.

**John Wright**
We?

**William Brown**
You helped.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
I'll accept that.

They laugh.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — LABORATORY | MOMENTS LATER]**

The scientists begin recording the results.

William looks at the dead mosquito inside the chamber.

His expression is satisfied.

**William Brown**
The virus targeted the mosquito exactly as designed.

A scientist nods.

**SCIENTIST**
No reaction in the control samples.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
Excellent.

John looks through the glass one last time.

**John Wright**
Well, that's one less mosquito in the world.

William laughs.

**William Brown**
Only one?

**John Wright**
You've got a lot of work left.

William shakes his head.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — LABORATORY CORRIDOR | AFTERNOON]**

The laboratory gradually becomes quieter.

Scientists move between rooms.

William and John walk down the corridor.

John is holding a cup of coffee.

**John Wright**
So what's next?

**William Brown**
More testing.

**John Wright**
Of course.

William looks toward the laboratory behind them.

**William Brown**
If everything remains stable, this could eventually be used on a much larger scale.

John nods.

**John Wright**
Sounds like good news.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
It is.

They continue walking.

Neither notices someone standing farther down the corridor.

**Sarah Smith.**

She watches them.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — SECURE LABORATORY | NIGHT]**

*The facility is almost empty.*

Most of the lights have been turned off.

The corridors are quiet.

**SFX: FOOTSTEPS**

Sarah walks through the laboratory.

She reaches a secured door.

She enters a code.

**BEEP.**

The door unlocks.

**CLICK.**

Sarah enters.

---

### **[SCENE 6 — SECURED RESEARCH ROOM]**

The room is dark except for the light coming from the computer screens.

Sarah walks toward the main workstation.

She looks at the research files.

She opens William's virus data.

The screen displays:

> **MOSQUITO-SPECIFIC VIRAL RESEARCH**

Sarah studies the information.

Her expression remains completely calm.

She opens another file.

Then another.

She examines the virus data carefully.

She reaches toward a secured sample container.

She stops.

Looks at it.

Then takes it.

**CLICK.**

Sarah places the container into a small case.

She then accesses the research system.

Her fingers move across the keyboard.

Something changes on the screen.

We don't see exactly what.

**BEEP.**

Sarah closes the file.

She returns everything to its original position.

She looks around.

Nothing appears disturbed.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — SECURED RESEARCH ROOM]**

Sarah places the case inside her bag.

She turns toward the door.

Before leaving, she looks back at the laboratory.

Her face remains emotionless.

She quietly says—

**Sarah Smith**
This is it.

She leaves.

The door closes behind her.

**CLICK.**

---

### **[SCENE 8 — RESEARCH FACILITY | MORNING]**

The next morning.

The facility is busy again.

Scientists arrive.

Computers turn on.

Machines begin operating.

William enters the laboratory carrying his coffee.

John follows behind him.

**John Wright**
Morning.

**William Brown**
Morning.

William places his coffee down.

He checks the research system.

Everything appears normal.

He looks at the data.

Nothing seems wrong.

John looks over his shoulder.

**John Wright**
Everything okay?

William nods.

**William Brown**
Everything's fine.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
Good.

William returns to his work.

Neither of them notices what Sarah changed.

---

### **[SCENE 9 — MOSQUITO MONTAGE | DIFFERENT LOCATIONS]**

*A series of quick shots.*

A mosquito lands on a window.

It becomes weak.

---

Another mosquito flies through a garden.

It suddenly drops.

---

Another mosquito rests on a wall.

It stops moving.

---

*The mosquito population begins falling.*

People barely notice.

To them—

It's good news.

---

### **[SCENE 10 — SOMEWHERE IN GERMANY | DAY]**

Sarah walks alone.

Her bag is with her.

Inside—

The stolen sample.

She stops.

Looks toward the road.

Cars pass normally.

People walk past her.

Nobody knows what she has done.

Sarah watches the world around her.

Her expression remains calm.

She turns and walks away.

---

### **[SCENE 11 — GERMANY | JOHN'S LOCATION | EVENING]**

**Mr. John Wright** walks down a quiet street.

He checks his phone.

Nothing unusual.

He puts the phone away.

Continues walking.

A normal evening.

A normal person.

A normal world.

He has absolutely no idea what is coming.

---

### **[SCENE 12 — RESEARCH FACILITY | NIGHT]**

Back at the laboratory.

William remains at his workstation.

He looks at the mosquito research.

The results still appear successful.

He smiles.

**William Brown**
Finally.

He turns off the monitor.

The screen goes dark.

---

### **[SCENE 13 — UNKNOWN LOCATION]**

*Darkness.*

Sarah places the small sample container onto a table.

She looks at it.

The camera slowly moves closer.

The container remains sealed.

No explanation.

No motive.

No answers.

Just the sample.

Sarah looks away.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

### **END OF PART 3**




# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 1 — THE BEGINNING

### PART 4 — THEIR LIVES

---

### **[SCENE 1 — NEW YORK CITY | PRIVATE JET | AFTERNOON]**

*The private jet continues flying above the clouds.*

Inside, **Michael Carter** sits beside the window.

The driver is still in the cockpit.

Michael looks at his phone.

A notification appears.

He checks it.

Nothing important.

He locks the phone.

Michael looks out of the window again.

The clouds slowly begin to disappear beneath the aircraft.

The city comes into view.

**NEW YORK CITY.**

The driver looks back.

**DRIVER**
We're almost there, Mr. Carter.

Michael looks outside.

**Michael Carter**
Finally.

The jet begins its descent.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — NEW YORK CITY | AIRPORT | AFTERNOON]**

*The private jet lands smoothly.*

**SFX: TIRES SCREECHING SOFTLY**

The aircraft slows down and reaches its designated area.

Michael steps outside.

The driver follows with Michael's luggage.

Michael looks around the airport.

People rush past.

Announcements echo through the terminal.

**AIRPORT ANNOUNCEMENT (O.S.)**
Flight 726 to Chicago is now boarding at Gate 18.

Michael checks his watch.

The driver hands him his bag.

**DRIVER**
Here you go.

**Michael Carter**
Thanks.

Michael starts walking.

The driver follows.

For Michael—

It's just another trip.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — CALIFORNIA | HARRY'S HOUSE | AFTERNOON]**

*Back at Harry's house.*

The game is still paused.

**Harry Wilson** and **David Anderson** are sitting on the sofa.

Harry looks at the screen.

Then at David.

Then back at the screen.

**Harry Wilson**
You know what?

**David Anderson**
What?

**Harry Wilson**
I think we should settle this properly.

David looks suspicious.

**David Anderson**
What does "properly" mean?

Harry stands up.

**Harry Wilson**
Rematch.

David laughs.

**David Anderson**
You've already lost twice.

Harry points at him.

**Harry Wilson**
That was practice.

**David Anderson**
Practice?

**Harry Wilson**
Yeah.

**David Anderson**
You practiced losing?

Harry stares at him.

A beat.

**Harry Wilson**
You're lucky you're my best friend.

David laughs.

**David Anderson**
Come on.

Harry picks up the controller.

The game starts again.

**SFX: GAME START**

Both immediately become serious.

**Harry Wilson**
This time I'm winning.

**David Anderson**
You said that last time.

**Harry Wilson**
And this time I mean it.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — HAWAII | SCHOOL | AFTERNOON]**

*Students begin leaving their classrooms.*

The school day is coming to an end.

**Emma Bennett** walks through the hallway carrying her bag.

**Chris Bennett** catches up with her.

**Chris Bennett**
Emma.

Emma keeps walking.

**Chris Bennett**
Wait.

She stops.

Turns around.

**Emma Bennett**
What?

Chris holds up something.

**Chris Bennett**
You forgot this.

Emma looks at it.

Her notebook.

**Emma Bennett**
Oh.

She takes it.

**Emma Bennett**
Thanks.

Chris starts walking again.

Emma follows.

**Emma Bennett**
You would've forgotten it too.

Chris looks at her.

**Chris Bennett**
I wouldn't.

Emma smiles.

**Emma Bennett**
Sure.

They continue walking together.

Outside, students gather around the school entrance.

The Hawaiian sun shines brightly over the campus.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — SEOUL | PARK | AFTERNOON]**

*The park is busier now.*

People walk along the paths.

Children play.

Cyclists pass.

Do Do-Hee remains in the park.

She gets up from the bench.

She walks slowly along the path.

A little girl runs past her.

A ball rolls across the ground.

Do Do-Hee stops it with her foot.

The girl runs over.

**GIRL**
Thank you!

Do Do-Hee smiles and hands the ball back.

**Do Do-Hee**
You're welcome.

The girl runs away.

Do Do-Hee continues walking.

The city remains peaceful around her.

---

## EPISODE 1 — THE BEGINNING

### PART 5 — KIM WONG-JUN

### **[SCENE 1 — DAEGU, SOUTH KOREA | MORNING]**

*Morning sunlight enters through the curtains.*

*A quiet bedroom.*

**SFX: ALARM**

**BEEP. BEEP. BEEP.**

**CLICK.**

The alarm stops.

**Kim Wong-jun** opens his eyes.

He looks at the clock.

**7:00 AM.**

He sits up.

His room is neat and organised.

His books are arranged on a shelf.

His desk is clean.

His school bag is already prepared.

Kim gets out of bed.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — KIM'S ROOM | MOMENTS LATER]**

Kim gets dressed.

He checks his bag.

Notebook.

Pens.

Water bottle.

Everything is there.

He picks up his phone.

A message appears.

> **“Are you nervous about the Olympiad results?”**

Kim reads it.

He replies:

> **“No.”**

Another message immediately appears.

> **“How are you not nervous?”**

Kim thinks for a second.

Then types:

> **“Being nervous won't change the result.”**

He puts his phone away.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — DAEGU | STREET | MORNING]**

Kim walks through the streets of Daegu.

The city is already busy.

Cars move through the roads.

Students walk toward school.

Shops open for the day.

Kim walks calmly through the crowd.

He passes a convenience store.

He notices a poster advertising a mathematics competition.

He stops for a moment.

Looks at it.

Then continues walking.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — OLYMPIAD RESULTS CENTRE]**

*A large group of students waits outside.*

Some are talking nervously.

Others repeatedly check their phones.

Kim stands quietly among them.

A student approaches him.

**STUDENT**
Kim Wong-jun!

Kim turns.

**STUDENT**
Are you nervous?

**Kim Wong-jun**
Not really.

**STUDENT**
Seriously?

Kim nods.

**Kim Wong-jun**
We already took the test.

The student looks confused.

**STUDENT**
So?

**Kim Wong-jun**
So the result is already decided.

The student laughs.

**STUDENT**
You really don't get nervous, do you?

Kim smiles slightly.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — RESULTS BOARD]**

A staff member walks toward the board.

The crowd becomes silent.

The results sheet is placed onto the board.

**THUD.**

Immediately, students rush forward.

Kim waits.

After the crowd settles slightly, he walks toward the board.

His eyes move down the list.

He finds his name.

> **RANK 1 — KIM WONG-JUN**

Kim looks at it.

A small smile appears on his face.

The student beside him looks at the result.

**STUDENT**
YOU GOT FIRST!

Kim nods.

**Kim Wong-jun**
Yeah.

**STUDENT**
That's it?!

Kim looks at him.

**Kim Wong-jun**
What should I do?

**STUDENT**
I don't know! Celebrate!

Kim laughs.

---

### **[SCENE 6 — OUTSIDE THE RESULTS CENTRE]**

Students leave the building.

Some celebrate with their friends.

Others look disappointed.

Kim walks outside.

His phone starts buzzing.

**BZZZT.**

He checks it.

Several messages have arrived.

> **CONGRATULATIONS!**

> **RANK 1?!**

> **BRO HOW?!**

Kim smiles.

> **“Hard work.”**

He sends it.

Another message arrives.

> **“Teach me.”**

Kim laughs.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — DAEGU | SMALL CAFÉ | AFTERNOON]**

He looks at it.

**FRIEND**

Kim thinks.

**Kim Wong-jun**
Go home.

The friend laughs.

**FRIEND**
That's it?

**Kim Wong-jun**
What else?

**FRIEND**
You got first place!

Kim shrugs.

**Kim Wong-jun**
There's another test someday.

His friend stares at him.

**FRIEND**
You're unbelievable.

Kim smiles.

### **[SCENE 8 — DAEGU | STREET | EVENING]**

The child runs over.

**CHILD**
Sorry!

Kim picks up the ball.

**Kim Wong-jun**
Here.

He throws it back.

**CHILD**
Thank you!

Smiles.

**10:41 PM.**

### **[SCENE 6 — SCHOOL CAFETERIA | LUNCH]**

Emma sits nearby with hers.

**Emma Bennett**

Coincidence.

**Emma Bennett**
One.

# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 2 — THE FIRST CHANGE

### PART 1 — THE REPLACEMENT

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | RESEARCH FACILITY | MORNING]**

*Black screen.*

**SFX: LOW ELECTRONIC HUM**

*The sound of laboratory equipment slowly fades in.*

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

**FADE IN.**

The same high-security research facility stands quietly in the German countryside.

Inside, scientists move between laboratories.

But today, the focus isn't on the virus.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — RESEARCH LABORATORY]**

*William Brown stands in front of a large digital screen.*

On the screen is an ecosystem diagram.

Plants.

Insects.

Birds.

Bats.

Fish.

Frogs.

The diagram shows arrows connecting them.

**Mr. John Wright** stands beside him, holding a tablet.

**John Wright**
So the virus works.

William nods.

**William Brown**
The mosquito population will eventually fall dramatically.

John looks at the diagram.

**John Wright**
Which is what we wanted.

William doesn't answer immediately.

He points toward several animals on the screen.

**William Brown**
Not completely.

John looks confused.

**John Wright**
Why?

William zooms in on the diagram.

**William Brown**
Because mosquitoes aren't just annoying us.

He points toward a bird.

**William Brown**
They're food.

Then a frog.

**William Brown**
For frogs.

Then a fish.

**William Brown**
Fish.

Then a bat.

**William Brown**
Bats.

John slowly understands.

**John Wright**
If the mosquitoes disappear...

**William Brown**
Some animals lose part of their food supply.

John looks at the screen.

**John Wright**
So the virus could solve one problem...

**William Brown**
...and create another.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — RESEARCH LABORATORY | CONTINUOUS]**

William walks toward another workstation.

John follows.

**John Wright**
So what do we do?

William stops beside a sealed glass enclosure.

Inside—

A strange-looking insect moves slowly across a small branch.

John looks at it.

**John Wright**
What's that?

William smiles.

**William Brown**
Our solution.

John steps closer.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — CONTROLLED ECOLOGY CHAMBER]**

*The camera moves closer to the glass.*

Inside the chamber are plants, small branches and soil.

Several of the new insects move through the environment.

They look completely harmless.

William watches them.

**William Brown**
We've been developing them for months.

John looks surprised.

**John Wright**
And you never told me?

William smiles.

**William Brown**
You never asked.

John gives him a look.

**John Wright**
I'm your best friend.

**William Brown**
Exactly.

John laughs.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — THE NEW SPECIES]**

William points toward the insects.

**William Brown**
They feed mainly on plant matter.

John looks at the plants.

**John Wright**
So they're herbivores?

**William Brown**
Mostly.

William taps the glass.

One insect climbs onto a leaf.

**William Brown**
Their purpose isn't to replace mosquitoes completely.

John nods.

**William Brown**
It's to provide another food source for animals that normally consume small insects.

John looks at the insect.

**John Wright**
So birds could eat them.

**William Brown**
Yes.

**John Wright**
Frogs?

**William Brown**
Yes.

**John Wright**
Fish?

**William Brown**
Certain species, yes.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
Not bad.

---

### **[SCENE 6 — ECOLOGY SIMULATION ROOM]**

William and John enter a larger room.

A giant screen displays a simulated ecosystem.

On one side:

**MOSQUITO POPULATION ↓**

On the other:

**NEW SPECIES ↑**

The simulation shows birds, frogs and fish continuing to receive food.

William watches carefully.

**John Wright**
So we're basically filling the gap.

William nods.

**William Brown**
Exactly.

John looks at him.

**John Wright**
We're replacing their role.

William smiles.

**William Brown**
Not replacing them.

He looks toward the simulation.

**William Brown**
Replacing their role.

John nods.

---

Healthy.

**David Anderson**

Harry quickly turns down the heat.

**Harry Wilson**
Okay, maybe not.

They continue cooking.

David adds something.

Harry looks at him.

**Harry Wilson**
What did you just put in?

David freezes.

**David Anderson**
I don't know.

Harry stares at him.

**Harry Wilson**
You don't know?!

David laughs.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — DINING TABLE]**

A plate sits between them.

It doesn't look perfect.

Harry takes a bite.

He thinks.

David watches him.

**David Anderson**
Well?

Harry nods slowly.

**Harry Wilson**
Actually...

David takes a bite.

He pauses.

**David Anderson**
It's not terrible.

Harry smiles.

**Harry Wilson**
That's basically a five-star review.

### **[SCENE 8 — LIVING ROOM | EVENING]**

**Harry Wilson**
Fixed.

**David Anderson**
It's leaning.

Harry steps back.

He looks.

The shelf is definitely leaning.

**Harry Wilson**
It's artistic.

David bursts out laughing.

---

### **[SCENE 9 — OUTSIDE THE HOUSE | EVENING]**

Harry and David sit outside.

The neighbourhood is peaceful.

The sky slowly becomes darker.

For once, neither of them is joking.

**David Anderson**
You ever think about the future?

Harry looks at him.

**Harry Wilson**
Sometimes.

**David Anderson**
Like where we'll be in ten years?

Harry thinks.

**Harry Wilson**
Hopefully somewhere better.

David smiles.

**David Anderson**
Together?

Harry looks at him.

**Harry Wilson**
Obviously.

David nods.

### **[SCENE 10 — OUTSIDE THE HOUSE | NIGHT]**

**Harry Wilson**
Pretty normal day.

David nods.

**David Anderson**
Yeah.

**Harry Wilson**
Let's keep it that way.

David looks at him.

**David Anderson**
Deal.

### PART 8 — DO DO-HEE: THE TALENT

### **[SCENE 1 — SEOUL | COMMUNITY CENTRE | AFTERNOON]**

*The community centre is busy.*

**Soo-jin**
You really think you can finish that?

Do Do-Hee looks at the pieces.

**Do Do-Hee**
I think so.

**Soo-jin**
You've got ten minutes.

Do Do-Hee nods.

**Do Do-Hee**
Deal.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — THE CHALLENGE]**

A timer begins.

**SFX: BEEP!**

Do Do-Hee starts sorting the pieces.

She doesn't rush.

She studies the shapes and colours.

Soo-jin watches.

**Soo-jin**
You're already doing that thing again.

Do Do-Hee looks up.

**Do Do-Hee**
What thing?

**Soo-jin**
Thinking for five minutes without saying anything.

**Do Do-Hee**
It works.

She continues.

### **[SCENE 3 — THE PUZZLE]**

**CLICK.**

Another.

**CLICK.**

Looks at it.

**Do Do-Hee**
Wait.

It fits perfectly.

**CLICK.**

**Soo-jin**
How did you know?

**Do Do-Hee**
The colours didn't match before.

**Soo-jin**

Do Do-Hee continues.

### **[SCENE 4 — THE FINAL PIECES]**

**CLICK.**

**SFX: BEEP!**

**Do Do-Hee**
With twelve seconds left.

Soo-jin shakes her head.

**Soo-jin**

Do Do-Hee laughs.

**Soo-jin**

**Do Do-Hee**
I don't know.

**Soo-jin**

Do Do-Hee smiles.

**Do Do-Hee**
Maybe I just pay attention.

Soo-jin nods.

**Soo-jin**
Fair enough.

They continue walking.

### **[SCENE 6 — SEOUL | SMALL SHOP]**

**Soo-jin**

**Do Do-Hee**
Nothing.

**Soo-jin**
You're having something.

**Do Do-Hee**
Fine.

### **[SCENE 7 — SEOUL | PARK | EVENING]**

Everything is peaceful.

**Do Do-Hee**
It's quiet today.

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | RESEARCH FACILITY | MORNING]**

*The research facility is already busy.*

**John Wright**

**William Brown**
The first successful batch.

Inside the controlled habitat are several small organisms.

**John Wright**

**William Brown**
That's what we wanted.

John smiles.

**John Wright**

**William Brown**
That's the important part.

---

**William Brown**
They can feed on plants.

John studies the information.

**John Wright**
So they'll have their own food source.

**William Brown**
Exactly.

John looks back at the organisms.

**John Wright**
And animals can eat them?

William nods.

**William Brown**
That's the second part of the plan.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
So if mosquitoes disappear...

William finishes his sentence.

**William Brown**
...these species can become part of the food chain.

John nods.

**John Wright**

William smiles.

**William Brown**
That's the idea.

**John Wright**
They're eating.

**William Brown**
Good.

**RESEARCHER**
Activity levels are normal.

**William Brown**

**John Wright**

The organism remains still.

**John Wright**

**RESEARCHER**

The organism begins moving again.

**RESEARCHER**
Activity restored.

John exhales.

**John Wright**
That was close.

William nods.

**William Brown**
That's why we're testing them in controlled conditions.

John looks at him.

**John Wright**

William thinks for a moment.

**William Brown**
Not yet.

John smiles.

**John Wright**
Good answer.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — RESEARCH OFFICE]**

**John Wright**

William looks at him.

**William Brown**
What?

**John Wright**

William smiles.

**William Brown**
We're getting there.

**John Wright**
Healthy species.

**William Brown**
Stable behaviour.

**John Wright**
Plant-based diet.

**William Brown**

John grins.

**John Wright**
Not bad.

William laughs.

**William Brown**
Not bad at all.

### **[SCENE 6 — LABORATORY CORRIDOR]**

**John Wright**

**William Brown**

John nods.

**John Wright**

He looks through the glass at the laboratory.

**John Wright**

William smiles.

**William Brown**
That's what we're hoping for.

### **[SCENE 6 — SEOUL | SAME TIME]**

Do Do-Hee walks through a busy Seoul street.

She puts her phone away.

Around her, everything is normal.

People laugh.

Cars pass.

Shopkeepers talk with customers.

She continues walking.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — GERMANY | RESEARCH FACILITY | AFTERNOON]**

**William Brown** sits at his desk.

He looks through the final report of the environmental project.

He smiles.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
Completed.

He closes the file.

A researcher walks past.

**RESEARCHER:**
Congratulations, William.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
Thank you.

William looks through the laboratory window.

The new species remain inside their controlled habitat.

Everything is stable.

William smiles.

His project is finally finished.

---

### **[SCENE 8 — UNKNOWN LOCATION | EVENING]**

**Sarah Smith** walks through a quiet area.

She carries a small case.

She stops and looks around.

Nobody is nearby.

She checks the case.

It is still securely closed.

Sarah continues walking.

Her expression remains completely calm.

---

### **[SCENE 9 — GERMANY | JOHN WRIGHT'S HOME | NIGHT]**

The house is completely silent.

**John Wright** is still lying on the floor.

He hasn't moved.

The infection mark remains visible on his skin.

The room is dark.

Then—

**THUMP.**

His heartbeat.

**THUMP.**

Another beat.

John's fingers twitch.

But he remains unconscious.

---

### **[SCENE 10 — THE WORLD CONTINUES]**

**NEW YORK**

Michael, Henry and Alex laugh together.

**CALIFORNIA**

Harry and David walk home.

**HAWAII**

Chris and Emma leave school.

**MUMBAI**

Kartik meets his friends.

**DAEGU**

Kim checks his phone.

**SEOUL**

Do Do-Hee walks through the city.

**GERMANY**

William leaves the research facility.

**UNKNOWN LOCATION**

Sarah continues walking with the case.

Everyone is living their normal lives.

Nobody knows what has begun.

---

### **[SCENE 11 — JOHN'S HOME | NIGHT]**

The camera returns to John.

Silence.

**THUMP.**

**THUMP.**

His heartbeat becomes slightly faster.

His eyes remain closed.

His fingers move again.

Then—

**John's hand suddenly clenches.**

The screen cuts to black.

# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 4 — **BEFORE EVERYTHING CHANGED**

### PART 2 — **THE FIRST ATTACK**

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | JOHN WRIGHT'S HOME | MORNING]**

*Morning sunlight enters through the curtains.*

John Wright is still lying on the floor.

His eyes suddenly open.

He takes a deep breath.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Huh...?

He slowly sits up.

He looks around the room, confused.

His head hurts.

He touches his forehead.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
What happened...?

He notices the strange mark on his arm.

His expression changes.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
That wasn't there before.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — JOHN'S HOME]**

John stands up.

He feels dizzy.

He holds onto the wall.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Sarah...

He remembers the previous night.

Sarah.

The case.

The injection.

His breathing becomes heavier.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
What did she do to me?

He grabs his phone.

The screen is cracked from when he dropped it.

He tries calling William.

**CALLING WILLIAM BROWN...**

No answer.

John tries again.

Still nothing.

He puts the phone down.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — THE HUNGER]**

John suddenly hears something.

**SFX: STOMACH GROWL**

He looks confused.

He walks into the kitchen.

He opens the refrigerator.

He sees food.

He grabs something.

He takes a bite.

He stops.

He doesn't seem satisfied.

Another strange hunger hits him.

John looks worried.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Why am I still hungry?

He closes the refrigerator.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — OUTSIDE]**

John leaves his house.

He walks down the quiet street.

People pass by normally.

John keeps his head down.

He looks sick.

A man walking in the opposite direction notices him.

The stranger slows down.

**STRANGER:**
Sir? Are you okay?

John stops.

He looks at the stranger.

For a moment, he says nothing.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I'm...

He struggles to speak.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I'm fine.

The stranger looks concerned.

**STRANGER:**
You don't look fine.

John suddenly hears the man's voice very clearly.

His breathing changes.

His eyes focus on him.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — THE FIRST ATTACK]**

The stranger takes a step closer.

**STRANGER:**
Do you need help?

John suddenly lunges toward him.

**STRANGER:**
Whoa!

The stranger falls backward.

John grabs his arm.

The stranger struggles to get away.

**STRANGER:**
Let go!

John's behaviour becomes increasingly aggressive.

The stranger manages to push him away and gets back on his feet.

He runs.

John stands there for a moment.

He watches him disappear down the street.

John looks at his own hands.

He's shocked by what he just did.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
What... was that?

---

### **[SCENE 6 — THE STRANGER RUNS]**

The stranger runs down the street.

He looks back.

John isn't following him.

He stops and catches his breath.

He looks shaken.

**STRANGER:**
That guy was insane...

He checks his arm where John grabbed him.

There's a small mark.

He doesn't think much of it.

He walks away.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — JOHN]**

John remains standing in the street.

He looks confused and frightened.

His breathing slowly becomes normal again.

He looks toward the direction the stranger ran.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I need help.

He takes out his phone again.

This time, he calls emergency services.

The phone rings.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

### **END OF EPISODE 4 — PART 2**

## EPISODE 4 — **BEFORE EVERYTHING CHANGED**

### PART 3 — **THE CALL**

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | JOHN WRIGHT'S HOME | MORNING]**

*John stands in his living room, still shaken by what happened outside.*

He looks at his hands.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
What is happening to me?

His phone is still in his hand.

He looks at the emergency number on the screen.

He hesitates.

Then presses **CALL**.

**SFX: BEEP... BEEP...**

The call connects.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
Emergency services. What is your emergency?

John struggles to explain.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I... I think something is wrong with me.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
Are you injured?

John looks at the mark on his arm.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I don't know.

He pauses.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Someone attacked me last night.

The operator becomes more attentive.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
Who attacked you?

John looks toward the door.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Someone I know.

---

### **[SCENE 2 — JOHN EXPLAINS]**

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
Where are you located?

John gives his address.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
An ambulance is being sent to you.

John sits down.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Please hurry.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
Are you experiencing any other symptoms?

John thinks.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I'm extremely tired.

He looks at his hands.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
And... I keep feeling strange.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
Stay inside and wait for the ambulance.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Okay.

The call ends.

---

### **[SCENE 3 — JOHN'S MEMORY]**

John closes his eyes.

A memory flashes through his mind.

**Sarah standing in his house.**

**SARAH SMITH:**
It's already started.

John opens his eyes.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Sarah...

He suddenly remembers the injection.

His expression becomes frightened.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
She did this.

---

### **[SCENE 4 — OUTSIDE JOHN'S HOME]**

A few streets away, the stranger from earlier walks toward a bus stop.

He keeps rubbing his arm.

He looks at the small mark where John grabbed him.

**STRANGER:**
That guy really hurt.

He notices that the mark looks slightly red.

He ignores it.

The bus arrives.

He gets on.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — AMBULANCE]**

An ambulance speeds through the streets.

**SFX: WEE-OOO! WEE-OOO!**

Two emergency workers sit inside.

**EMERGENCY WORKER 1:**
What do we know?

**EMERGENCY WORKER 2:**
Male patient. Confusion, weakness and unusual behaviour.

**EMERGENCY WORKER 1:**
Any injuries?

**EMERGENCY WORKER 2:**
Unknown.

The ambulance continues toward John's house.

---

### **[SCENE 6 — JOHN'S HOME]**

John sits on the sofa.

He looks at the clock.

**TICK.**

**TICK.**

**TICK.**

His breathing becomes heavier.

He hears a vehicle approaching outside.

**SFX: SIREN**

John looks toward the window.

The ambulance arrives.

He stands.

For a moment, he looks relieved.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Finally.

---

### **[SCENE 7 — THE AMBULANCE ARRIVES]**

The emergency workers get out.

They approach the front door.

**KNOCK. KNOCK.**

John opens it.

One worker looks at him.

**EMERGENCY WORKER 1:**
Mr. Wright?

John nods.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Yes.

**EMERGENCY WORKER 1:**
We're here to help.

John steps aside.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Thank you.

The workers enter.

One of them notices the strange mark on John's arm.

He looks at it carefully.

**EMERGENCY WORKER 2:**
How did you get that?

John looks down.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
I...

He hesitates.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
Someone injected me.

The two workers exchange a concerned glance.

---

### **[SCENE 8 — FINAL SHOT]**

The camera moves outside.

The ambulance remains parked outside John's house.

People continue walking down the street.

Nobody knows what is happening inside.

The camera slowly moves toward the bus stop where the stranger is sitting.

He looks tired.

He closes his eyes.

Then—

**THUMP.**

His heartbeat.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

Heartbeat is stable.

Temperature is slightly elevated.

### **[SCENE 7 — HOSPITAL]**

**DOCTOR 2:**
It's changing.

The first doctor looks at the mark.

**DOCTOR:**
Get a blood sample.

John hears them.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**
What's happening?

Nobody answers.

The doctor prepares to take a sample.

### **[SCENE 8 — BLOOD SAMPLE]**

John watches.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**

The doctor looks at him.

**JOHN WRIGHT:**

**DOCTOR:**
We're going to find out.

Sir?

**PASSENGER:**

**THUMP.**

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | HOSPITAL LABORATORY | MORNING]**

**TECHNICIAN:**
Same thing.

Any known virus?

Nothing matches.

Nothing.

I have no idea.

Hello?

What did they say?

Sarah.

Sarah?

## EPISODE 4 — **BEFORE EVERYTHING CHANGED**

### PART 6 — **THE TRANSFORMATION**

---

### **[SCENE 1 — GERMANY | HOSPITAL | NIGHT]**

*John Wright lies unconscious on a hospital bed.*

Machines surround him.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

William Brown stands outside the room, watching through the glass.

A doctor checks John's condition.

**DOCTOR:**
His condition hasn't improved.

William looks worried.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
How long has he been unconscious?

**DOCTOR:**
Several hours.

William looks through the glass.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
Sarah did this...

---

### **[SCENE 2 — SOMETHING CHANGES]**

Suddenly—

**BEEP...**

The monitor changes.

The doctor's expression changes.

**DOCTOR:**
Wait.

John's heartbeat begins slowing.

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

William steps closer to the glass.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
What's happening?

The doctor watches the monitor.

**DOCTOR:**
His heartbeat is dropping.

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

Then—

**FLATLINE.**

**BEEEEEEEEEP—**

William freezes.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
John...

---

### **[SCENE 3 — SILENCE]**

Doctors rush into the room.

**DOCTOR:**
He's gone into cardiac arrest!

They begin trying to revive John.

William watches helplessly from outside.

**DOCTOR:**
Come on!

The monitor remains flat.

**BEEEEEEEEEP—**

William looks devastated.

Then—

**THUMP.**

Everyone freezes.

The monitor shows a heartbeat.

**THUMP.**

Another.

**THUMP.**

The doctor stares at the screen.

**DOCTOR:**
That's impossible...

---

### **[SCENE 4 — JOHN OPENS HIS EYES]**

John's fingers suddenly move.

The doctor steps back.

**DOCTOR:**
John?

John's eyes slowly open.

William watches.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
John?

John sits up.

His breathing is strange.

The doctor approaches carefully.

**DOCTOR:**
Mr. Wright?

John doesn't respond.

He looks around the room.

His eyes appear unnaturally red.

William's expression changes.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
John...

John slowly turns toward him.

For a moment—

William sees his friend.

Then John's expression suddenly changes.

---

### **[SCENE 5 — THE FIRST ZOMBIE]**

John lets out a strange growl.

**DOCTOR:**
Stay back!

The doctor steps away.

John suddenly lunges forward.

The doctor barely moves out of the way.

**DOCTOR:**
Security!

William backs away from the glass.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
John, stop!

John doesn't respond.

He slams against the hospital room door.

**BANG!**

Everyone outside jumps.

**BANG!**

The glass shakes.

William stares at him.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
That's not John anymore...

---

### **[SCENE 6 — WILLIAM REALISES]**

Security arrives.

William steps back.

He looks at John's red eyes.

The infection marks have spread across his skin.

William remembers Sarah.

The case.

The injection.

The strange virus.

His face becomes pale.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
Sarah...

He whispers:

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
What did you create?

---

### **[SCENE 7 — JOHN'S FIRST MOVEMENT]**

Inside the room, John stops hitting the door.

He stands completely still.

The doctors watch nervously.

John slowly turns his head.

He looks toward the hallway.

He hears something.

**FOOTSTEPS.**

His head snaps toward the sound.

The security guards raise their hands.

**SECURITY GUARD:**
Stay back!

John takes one step forward.

Then another.

The guards prepare themselves.

---

### **[SCENE 8 — FINAL MOMENT]**

William slowly backs away.

He looks at John one final time.

His friend is gone.

Only the infected remains.

The hospital alarm begins sounding.

**SFX: ALARM!**

The screen cuts between:

**John's red eyes.**

**William's terrified face.**

**The hospital alarm.**

Then—

**CUT TO BLACK.**

SECURITY GUARD:

[SCENE 2 — JOHN

He takes another step.

BANG!

He hits the door again.

William watches from behind the security barrier.

WILLIAM BROWN:
John...

John doesn't react to his name.

William's expression falls.

[SCENE 3 — WILLIAM REALISES]

William watches John carefully.

The infection marks.

The strange behaviour.

The stopped heartbeat.

The heartbeat returning.

Sarah.

Everything connects in his mind.

WILLIAM BROWN:
This isn't our project...

The doctor looks at him.

DOCTOR:
What?

William turns toward him.

WILLIAM BROWN:
Whatever Sarah created... this isn't anything John and I were working on.

The doctor looks back at John.

DOCTOR:
Then what is it?

William has no answer.

[SCENE 4 — SECURITY ROOM]

A security officer watches the hospital cameras.

One monitor shows John's room.

Another shows the corridor.

The officer rewinds the footage.

He watches:

John unconscious.

Heartbeat stops.

Heartbeat returns.

John's eyes open.

John attacks.

The officer stares at the screen.

SECURITY OFFICER:
What... is that?

He quickly saves the footage.

[SCENE 5 — HOSPITAL CORRIDOR]

The lockdown continues.

Security guards keep everyone away from John's room.

William remains nearby.

Suddenly—

BANG!

John hits the door again.

Everyone jumps.

DOCTOR:
We need to keep that door closed.

William looks at John.

WILLIAM BROWN:
He's still in there somewhere.

The doctor looks at him.

DOCTOR:
William...

William doesn't answer.

[SCENE 6 — ANOTHER HOSPITAL | GERMANY | NIGHT]

At another hospital, the stranger lies unconscious.

A nurse checks his condition.

NURSE:
His heartbeat is stable.

The doctor nods.

DOCTOR:
Keep monitoring him.

They walk away.

Silence.

The stranger's fingers move.

[SCENE 7 — THE STRANGER WAKES]

The stranger suddenly opens his eyes.

He looks around.

A nurse notices.

NURSE:
You're awake.

The stranger slowly sits up.

STRANGER:
Where am I?

NURSE:
You're in the hospital. You collapsed on a bus.

The stranger touches his head.

STRANGER:
I remember...

He suddenly hears another patient's voice from across the room.

His eyes focus on the sound.

The nurse notices his expression.

NURSE:
Are you okay?

The stranger looks at her.

His eyes begin turning red.

CUT TO BLACK.

🌎 [FINAL SCENE — THE REST OF THE WORLD]
🇺🇸 NEW YORK

Michael, Henry and Alex are walking together.

MICHAEL CARTER:
Where should we go tomorrow?

HENRY RIVERS:
You decide.

They laugh.

🇺🇸 CALIFORNIA

Harry and David sit together.

HARRY WILSON:
We should call Michael later.

DAVID ANDERSON:
Yeah.

🇺🇸 HAWAII

Chris and Emma walk home.

CHRIS BENNETT:
I'm hungry.

EMMA BENNETT:
Again?

Chris laughs.

🇮🇳 MUMBAI

Kartik walks through a crowded street.

KARTIK SHARMA:
I'll call you later.

🇰🇷 DAEGU

Kim Wong-jun looks at his phone.

A message from Do Do-Hee appears.

DO DO-HEE:
Good night.

Kim smiles.

KIM WONG-JUN:
Good night.

🇰🇷 SEOUL

Do Do-Hee puts her phone down.

She looks out of her window at Seoul.

The city is completely normal.

[FINAL SHOT]

The camera slowly pulls away from the cities.

New York.

California.

Hawaii.

Mumbai.

Daegu.

Seoul.

Everyone is living normally.

Meanwhile, thousands of kilometres away—

John is locked inside a hospital.

The stranger has just awakened.

The world doesn't know it yet...

but the outbreak has already begun.

# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 4 — BEFORE EVERYTHING CHANGED

### PART 8 — THE UNEXPECTED

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 1**

John Wright was still lying inside the isolated room.

Doctors stood outside the glass, watching him carefully.

A technician placed John's blood sample under a microscope.

**TECHNICIAN:**
“There’s something unusual in the sample.”

**DOCTOR:**
“What kind of unusual?”

The technician adjusted the microscope.

**TECHNICIAN:**
“I don't know yet. The cells are behaving differently from the earlier sample.”

The doctor looked at the screen.

**DOCTOR:**
“Could it be contamination?”

**TECHNICIAN:**
“Maybe. But I've checked the equipment twice.”

The doctor became silent.

Nobody knew exactly what they were looking at.

They didn't even know what was causing it.

Inside the room, John suddenly moved.

His fingers twitched.

The monitor beside him continued beeping.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The stranger who had been attacked by John was in a completely different hospital.

He was unconscious on a bed.

A doctor examined the strange mark on his arm.

**DOCTOR:**
“The mark has changed.”

A nurse looked closer.

**NURSE:**
“It wasn't this large earlier.”

The doctor checked the patient's heartbeat.

**DOCTOR:**
“Still stable.”

The nurse looked at the blood sample.

**NURSE:**
“Should we compare it with the sample from the other hospital?”

The doctor paused.

**DOCTOR:**
“We don't even know what we're dealing with yet.”

The nurse nodded.

They continued examining the patient.

Neither hospital understood the connection.

---

**UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH SMITH**

Sarah sat alone in front of her tablet.

She watched several readings change on the screen.

Suddenly—

**WARNING: UNEXPECTED VARIATION DETECTED**

Sarah's expression changed.

She leaned closer to the screen.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“What...?”

She checked the readings again.

Then again.

The result remained the same.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“That wasn't supposed to happen.”

She stared at the screen.

For the first time, Sarah looked genuinely confused.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“What did I make...?”

She didn't have the answer.

---

**HAWAII — BENNETT HOUSE**

The evening was completely normal.

Chris Bennett sat on the sofa watching television.

Emma Bennett was sitting nearby.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Emma, give me the remote.”

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“No. I'm watching this.”

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“You've been watching this for an hour!”

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“So?”

Chris reached for the remote.

Emma pulled it away.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Come on!”

The remote slipped from Emma's hand.

It fell onto the floor.

**CLICK.**

The channel changed.

Neither of them noticed immediately.

Emma shifted her foot.

Her foot accidentally tapped the remote lying on the floor.

**CLICK.**

The television switched again.

A news channel appeared.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“Breaking news from Germany—”

Emma looked toward the television.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Wait.”

Chris turned around.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“What happened?”

On the screen, footage of a German hospital appeared.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“Authorities are investigating an unexplained incident involving a man identified as John Wright. Witnesses reported extremely violent behaviour before the man was taken to a hospital.”

Chris stared at the screen.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“That's weird.”

Emma watched for a few seconds.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Maybe it's some kind of accident.”

Chris shrugged.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Probably.”

Emma picked up the remote.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Anyway, I want my show back.”

She changed the channel.

The news disappeared.

The room returned to normal.

Neither Chris nor Emma realised that they had just seen the first public report of what would become a global disaster.

---

**NEW YORK**

Michael Carter walked down the street with Henry Rivers and Alex Brooks.

People laughed.

Cars passed.

Everything looked normal.

---

**CALIFORNIA**

Harry Wilson and David Anderson continued talking.

They had no idea what was happening thousands of kilometres away.

---

**MUMBAI**

Kartik Sharma walked through the crowded streets.

Nothing seemed unusual.

---

**SEOUL**

Do Do-Hee continued with her evening.

Her phone remained in her hand.

No warning.

No emergency.

Nothing.

---

**DAEGU**

Kim Wong-jun returned home.

He looked outside his window.

The city was calm.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 1**

John Wright suddenly opened his eyes.

The monitor began beeping faster.

A doctor stepped toward the room.

**DOCTOR:**
“What's happening?”

John slowly lifted his head.

His eyes were changing.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The stranger's fingers moved.

His heartbeat suddenly increased.

The nurse noticed.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor...”

The doctor turned toward the monitor.

The readings were changing.

---

**UNKNOWN LOCATION**

Sarah stared at her tablet.

The warning remained on the screen.

She whispered to herself—

**SARAH SMITH:**
“This isn't what I expected.”

She had created something.

But she didn't know what it was becoming.

And the world still had no idea.

# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 4 — BEFORE EVERYTHING CHANGED

### PART 9 — THE SECOND INFECTED

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The hospital was quiet.

The stranger lay unconscious on the bed.

A heart monitor beside him beeped steadily.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

A doctor looked at the monitor.

**DOCTOR:**
“His condition hasn't changed for the last few minutes.”

The nurse checked the patient's temperature.

**NURSE:**
“Temperature is still rising.”

The doctor looked at the strange mark on the patient's arm.

It had spread further.

**DOCTOR:**
“Something is changing.”

Suddenly—

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

The heartbeat became faster.

The nurse looked at the monitor.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor?”

The stranger's fingers twitched.

The doctor immediately stepped closer.

**DOCTOR:**
“Sir? Can you hear me?”

No response.

Then—

The stranger's eyes opened.

The doctor froze.

For a moment, the stranger simply stared at the ceiling.

**DOCTOR:**
“Sir, can you hear me?”

The stranger slowly turned his head.

His eyes were beginning to turn **red**.

The doctor stepped closer.

**DOCTOR:**
“Stay calm. You're in a hospital.”

The stranger suddenly sat upright.

The nurse stepped back.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor...”

The stranger looked directly at them.

His breathing became heavier.

**DOCTOR:**
“Easy...”

Suddenly—

The stranger lunged forward.

**DOCTOR:**
“GET BACK!”

The doctor stumbled backwards.

The nurse screamed and ran toward the door.

The stranger slammed into the hospital bed, knocking it aside.

**NURSE:**
“HELP!”

She ran into the corridor.

The doctor grabbed the emergency button.

**DOCTOR:**
“SECURITY! WE NEED SECURITY NOW!”

The stranger rushed toward him.

The doctor barely escaped through the doorway.

**BANG!**

The door slammed shut.

The stranger hit it from the other side.

**BANG!**

Everyone in the corridor froze.

Another hit.

**BANG!**

The nurse stared at the door in fear.

**NURSE:**
“What is happening to him?”

The doctor had no answer.

**DOCTOR:**
“I don't know.”

Security officers rushed down the corridor.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“What happened?”

**DOCTOR:**
“The patient attacked us.”

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Is he dangerous?”

The doctor looked at the shaking door.

**DOCTOR:**
“Yes.”

Another violent impact came from inside.

**BANG!**

The security officers moved into position.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 1**

William Brown stood outside John's isolated room.

John was still behind the glass.

Silent.

Motionless.

William looked at him carefully.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“John...”

John suddenly turned his head toward William.

William stepped back.

A doctor approached him.

**DOCTOR:**
“Mr. Brown, there's something you need to know.”

William looked at him.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“What?”

The doctor handed him a report.

**DOCTOR:**
“Another hospital just reported a violent patient.”

William looked at the report.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“Another?”

**DOCTOR:**
“Yes.”

William's expression changed.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“Was the patient attacked before becoming violent?”

The doctor shook his head.

**DOCTOR:**
“We don't know.”

William looked back through the glass.

John was staring directly at him.

William slowly lowered the report.

Two hospitals.

Two patients.

And the same strange symptoms.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

Security officers entered the room.

The stranger stood in the corner.

Completely still.

One officer raised his hand.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Stay where you are!”

The stranger didn't respond.

The officer took one step forward.

The stranger suddenly charged.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“MOVE!”

The officers rushed backwards.

The stranger slammed into the wall.

The room erupted into chaos.

A second security officer tried to block him.

The stranger attacked.

The first officer pulled his partner away.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“GET HIM OUT OF HERE!”

The emergency alarm began ringing throughout the hospital.

**ALARM:**
**WEE-OO! WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

Patients opened their doors.

Doctors and nurses ran through the corridors.

Nobody understood what was happening.

But everyone knew one thing—

**Something was very wrong.**

---

**UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH SMITH**

Sarah watched her tablet.

The number on the screen had changed.

> **INFECTED: 2**

Sarah stared at it.

She expected the first infected.

She expected the experiment to change.

But the second case had appeared far sooner than she expected.

Sarah whispered—

**SARAH SMITH:**
“Two already...”

She looked at John's data.

Then at the second patient's data.

The patterns were similar.

But not identical.

Sarah's expression became serious.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“They're changing.”

She slowly sat down.

For the first time, Sarah looked afraid of her own creation.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The emergency doors slammed shut.

The hospital entered lockdown.

Inside, the alarm continued.

**WEE-OO! WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

Outside, people began gathering around the hospital.

They had no idea what was happening inside.

And the authorities had no idea what they were about to face.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

### **END OF PART 9**

## EPISODE 4 — BEFORE EVERYTHING CHANGED

### PART 10 — THE OUTBREAK BEGINS

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The emergency alarm continued throughout the hospital.

**WEE-OO! WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

Security officers stood in the corridor.

The stranger was still inside the room.

Completely silent.

One security officer looked at the doctor.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“What exactly happened to him?”

The doctor shook his head.

**DOCTOR:**
“I don't know.”

Suddenly—

**BANG!**

The stranger slammed against the door.

Everyone stepped back.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Keep everyone away from this corridor!”

Nurses began moving patients to another section of the hospital.

Doctors rushed through the corridors.

Nobody knew what they were dealing with.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 1**

John Wright suddenly slammed against the glass of his isolated room.

**BANG!**

William Brown jumped.

John stared through the glass.

His eyes were completely red.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“John...”

John slammed against the glass again.

**BANG!**

A doctor quickly moved William away.

**DOCTOR:**
“Stay back, Mr. Brown.”

William stared at John.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“He isn't responding to anything.”

**DOCTOR:**
“We know.”

William looked at the report in his hand.

Then at John.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“And now there's another patient.”

The doctor nodded.

**DOCTOR:**
“At a completely different hospital.”

William's face became serious.

**WILLIAM BROWN:**
“This can't be a coincidence.”

---

**GERMANY — OUTSIDE HOSPITAL 2**

Police vehicles arrived.

People gathered outside the hospital.

Some were recording on their phones.

A police officer spoke into his radio.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
“We have an emergency situation inside the hospital. Multiple people are reporting violent behaviour.”

Another officer approached him.

**SECOND POLICE OFFICER:**
“There's another call.”

**POLICE OFFICER:**
“Where?”

**SECOND POLICE OFFICER:**
“Three streets away.”

The first officer looked confused.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
“Another violent patient?”

**SECOND POLICE OFFICER:**
“No. A person attacked someone on the street.”

The officer looked toward the hospital.

Then back at his partner.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
“Get another unit there.”

---

**UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH SMITH**

Sarah stared at her tablet.

The number had changed again.

> **INFECTED: 2**
> **VARIATION: INCREASING**
> **CONTROL: UNKNOWN**

Sarah slowly stood up.

She looked at John's data.

Then the stranger's.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“They're becoming different.”

She tried changing a setting.

Nothing happened.

She tried again.

Still nothing.

Sarah's expression changed.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“Why can't I stop it?”

She stared at the screen.

For the first time, Sarah realised something terrifying.

She didn't completely control what she had created anymore.

---

**NEW YORK — EVENING**

Michael Carter was sitting with Henry Rivers and Alex Brooks.

Michael's phone suddenly vibrated.

**BUZZ.**

He looked at the screen.

A breaking-news notification appeared.

> **BREAKING NEWS: UNEXPLAINED VIOLENT INCIDENTS REPORTED IN GERMANY**

Michael opened it.

Henry looked over.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What is it?”

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Something's happening in Germany.”

Alex leaned closer.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“What kind of something?”

Michael read the report.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“They're saying people are suddenly becoming extremely violent.”

Henry shrugged.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Could be anything.”

Michael locked his phone.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Yeah. Probably.”

They continued talking.

None of them thought much about it.

---

**CALIFORNIA**

Harry Wilson and David Anderson were walking down the street.

A television inside a shop was showing the German news.

They stopped for a moment.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“Authorities in Germany are investigating multiple unexplained violent incidents involving several individuals. Officials have not yet identified the cause.”

Harry looked at David.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“That's strange.”

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Yeah.”

They continued walking.

---

**HAWAII**

Chris Bennett and Emma Bennett were at home.

The television was playing normally.

A news report suddenly appeared.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“More information is emerging from Germany tonight—”

Emma looked toward the television.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Germany again?”

Chris looked over.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Looks like it.”

They watched for a few seconds.

The report showed police vehicles outside a hospital.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“Authorities are asking the public to remain calm while the situation is investigated.”

Chris picked up the remote.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Let's watch something else.”

He changed the channel.

---

**MUMBAI — NIGHT**

Kartik Sharma walked through a crowded street.

A group of people nearby were talking about the news.

**PERSON:**
“Did you hear about Germany?”

**SECOND PERSON:**
“Yeah. Something weird is happening there.”

Kartik briefly looked toward them.

Then continued walking.

---

**SEOUL**

Do Do-Hee looked at her phone.

A news article about Germany appeared on her screen.

She read the headline.

> **UNEXPLAINED ATTACKS REPORTED IN GERMANY**

Do Do-Hee frowned.

**DO DO-HEE:**
“What is going on there...?”

She put her phone away.

---

**DAEGU**

Kim Wong-jun received a message.

He opened it.

> **Have you seen the news about Germany?**

Kim looked at the message.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Germany?”

He opened the news.

His expression became slightly concerned.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The stranger suddenly charged toward the corridor.

**BANG!**

The door shook.

Security officers backed away.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Keep that door closed!”

The stranger hit it again.

**BANG!**

The emergency alarm continued.

The hospital was no longer quiet.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 1**

John stood behind the glass.

William watched him from a distance.

John suddenly turned toward another doctor.

The doctor froze.

William whispered—

**WILLIAM BROWN:**

**UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH SMITH**

> **INFECTED: 2**
> **VARIATION: INCREASING**

Then another.

And the rest of the world continued living normally.

For now.
