# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 1 — THE ESCAPE

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The emergency alarm continued ringing.

**WEE-OO! WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

The stranger stood inside the locked room.

Two security officers watched him through the glass.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Keep the door locked.”

**SECOND SECURITY OFFICER:**
“He hasn't moved for almost a minute.”

The doctor stood nearby, watching carefully.

**DOCTOR:**
“Don't get too close.”

Suddenly, the stranger turned toward the door.

He stared at the security officers.

Then—

**BANG!**

He slammed into the door.

The officers stepped back.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Hold the door!”

Another hit.

**BANG!**

The lock shook.

**DOCTOR:**
“Get everyone away from this corridor!”

Nurses immediately began moving patients away.

The stranger slammed into the door again.

**BANG!**

A crack appeared near the door frame.

**SECOND SECURITY OFFICER:**
“The lock is giving way!”

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Everyone move!”

The officers pulled people away from the corridor.

The stranger stepped back.

For a moment, everything became silent.

Then—

**BANG!**

The door burst open.

The stranger rushed into the corridor.

**NURSE:**
“RUN!”

Doctors and nurses scattered.

The stranger chased after them.

A security officer tried to block his path.

The stranger knocked him to the floor and continued running.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“STOP HIM!”

Another officer ran after him.

The stranger turned down another corridor.

He passed the emergency ward.

Patients screamed as they saw him.

**PATIENT:**
“What is happening?!”

A nurse quickly closed a nearby door.

**NURSE:**
“Stay inside!”

The stranger kept moving.

He reached the hospital entrance.

Two security officers were standing there.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Don't let him out!”

They moved toward him.

The stranger suddenly charged.

Both officers were forced backwards.

The automatic doors opened.

The stranger ran outside.

---

**GERMANY — OUTSIDE THE HOSPITAL**

People on the pavement turned as the stranger rushed out of the hospital.

A woman stopped walking.

**WOMAN:**
“What's wrong with him?”

The stranger ran past her.

She looked back in confusion.

Behind him, hospital staff shouted from the entrance.

**NURSE:**
“CALL THE POLICE!”

A man nearby pulled out his phone.

**MAN:**
“What's happening?”

The stranger disappeared around the corner.

The man started recording.

---

**GERMANY — CITY STREET**

The stranger ran through the street.

Cars passed.

People walked normally.

Nobody knew what had just escaped from the hospital.

A cyclist noticed him.

**CYCLIST:**
“Hey! Are you okay?”

The stranger suddenly stopped.

He turned toward the cyclist.

The cyclist froze.

The stranger rushed toward him.

**CYCLIST:**
“HEY!”

The cyclist dropped his bicycle and ran.

The stranger chased him.

People began screaming.

Cars slowed down.

Someone called the police.

The quiet street suddenly became chaotic.

---

**GERMANY — HOSPITAL 2**

The doctor looked through the entrance.

The stranger was gone.

A security officer rushed toward him.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“He escaped.”

The doctor stared at the empty street.

**DOCTOR:**
“Call the police.”

The officer picked up his radio.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Suspect has escaped the hospital. Repeat, the patient has escaped.”

The doctor looked at the bloodstained corridor behind him.

His face became pale.

**DOCTOR:**
“This is getting worse.”

---

**GERMANY — CITY STREET**

The cyclist ran as fast as he could.

Behind him, the stranger continued chasing.

The cyclist looked back.

The stranger was getting closer.

**CYCLIST:**
“HELP!”

People began running in different directions.

A car suddenly stopped in the middle of the road.

A police siren could be heard in the distance.

**SIREN:**
**WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

The stranger disappeared into a crowded street.

The police had arrived—

but they didn't know where he had gone.

And now, for the first time...

**the infection was outside the hospital.**

### **END OF PART 1 EPISODE 5**




# 🧟 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 2 — THE SPREAD

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — CITY STREET

The escaped infected runs through the crowded streets.

People move aside as he rushes past them.

A police siren echoes in the distance.

**SIREN... SIREN... SIREN...**

The infected suddenly disappears around a corner.

Moments later, two police vehicles arrive.

Several officers get out.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“Which way did he go?”**

The cyclist, still shaken, points down the road.

**CYCLIST:**
**“That way! He attacked me and just kept running!”**

The officers immediately begin searching the area.

But the infected is already gone.

---

### 🩸 THE FIRST ATTACK

Several streets away...

An ordinary man walks home from work.

He hears footsteps behind him.

**FOOTSTEPS...**

He slows down.

The footsteps stop.

He turns around.

Nobody.

He continues walking.

Then—

A figure suddenly steps out from a narrow alley.

It is the escaped infected.

The man stops.

**MAN:**
**“Hey... are you okay?”**

The infected stares at him.

Its eyes are red.

The man takes a step backwards.

**MAN:**
**“What's wrong with you?”**

Suddenly—

The infected charges.

**MAN:**
**“WHAT THE—?!”**

The man runs.

The infected catches him and attacks.

The man fights back desperately.

He manages to push the infected away and escape.

He runs down the street without looking back.

He finally stops several blocks away.

Breathing heavily, he looks at his arm.

There is a wound.

**MAN:**
**“What the hell...”**

He covers it with his sleeve.

He has no idea what just happened.

He keeps walking home.

---

### 🧬 THE FIRST INFECTED OUTSIDE

The man reaches his house.

His wife opens the door.

She immediately notices his condition.

**WIFE:**
**“What happened to your arm?”**

The man looks down.

**MAN:**
**“Some guy attacked me.”**

**WIFE:**
**“Attacked you? Who?”**

**MAN:**
**“I don't know. He just came out of nowhere.”**

His wife looks worried.

**WIFE:**
**“You need to clean that wound.”**

The man nods.

He walks into the living room and sits down.

A few minutes pass.

He suddenly starts sweating.

He wipes his forehead.

**MAN:**
**“Why is it so hot in here?”**

His wife looks at him.

**WIFE:**
**“You're sweating.”**

The man touches his forehead.

His breathing becomes heavier.

He looks at his arm.

The skin around the wound has begun changing.

A strange mark slowly spreads across his skin.

His expression changes.

**MAN:**
**“That's... not normal.”**

---

### 🚨 THE CHASE

Meanwhile...

Back on the streets...

A police vehicle suddenly stops.

An officer points ahead.

The escaped infected is standing at the end of the road.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“THERE!”**

The infected turns.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“STOP! POLICE!”**

The infected runs.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“MOVE! MOVE!”**

The officers chase him.

**SIRENS BLARE.**

Cars stop.

People scream and move away.

The infected runs between vehicles.

One police officer nearly catches him.

But the infected suddenly turns and charges towards a passer-by.

**PASSER-BY:**
**“HELP!”**

The infected attacks.

The police rush forward.

The passer-by escapes.

But the infected uses the confusion to disappear into the crowd.

The officers stop.

They look around.

Nothing.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“Where did he go?!”**

Another officer looks through the crowd.

**POLICE OFFICER 2:**
**“He disappeared.”**

---

### 📱 THE VIDEO

Earlier...

The man who recorded the infected escaping the hospital sits inside his apartment.

He watches the video again.

The footage shows the infected running out of the hospital.

People screaming.

The hospital doors opening.

The infected disappearing into the city.

The man hesitates.

Then he uploads it.

**UPLOAD COMPLETE.**

At first, nothing happens.

Then—

**10,000 VIEWS**

**50,000 VIEWS**

**200,000 VIEWS**

The video spreads rapidly.

Comments appear one after another.

*"Is this real?"*

*"What is happening in Germany?"*

*"That guy looks completely insane!"*

*"Someone should call the police!"*

*"This can't be real."*

The video continues spreading.

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK

Michael Carter is sitting with Henry Rivers and Alex Brooks.

Michael's phone vibrates.

He looks at the notification.

Then another.

And another.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
**“Guys... look at this.”**

He opens the video.

Henry watches.

Alex watches.

The three stare at the screen.

The infected runs out of the hospital.

People scream.

The video ends.

Silence.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
**“That's not normal.”**

**ALEX BROOKS:**
**“No. It isn't.”**

Michael looks at the video again.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
**“Something's wrong in Germany.”**

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — THE INFECTED MAN

Back at the house...

The infected man suddenly drops his phone.

**THUD.**

His wife rushes toward him.

**WIFE:**
**“Are you okay?”**

He doesn't respond.

His eyes stare blankly ahead.

His breathing becomes slower.

**WIFE:**
**“Hey...”**

She touches his shoulder.

Nothing.

He suddenly collapses.

**THUD.**

His wife screams.

**WIFE:**
**“CALL AN AMBULANCE!”**

She grabs her phone with shaking hands.

The camera moves toward the man.

His eyes remain closed.

His heartbeat continues.

For now.

---

### 🚨 THE CALLS BEGIN

Across Germany...

An emergency operator answers another call.

**EMERGENCY OPERATOR:**
**“Emergency services. What's your location?”**

A frightened voice answers.

**CALLER:**
**“Someone attacked my friend!”**

Another call comes in.

Then another.

**CALLER 2:**
**“There's a man acting violently!”**

Another.

**CALLER 3:**
**“My husband suddenly collapsed! He's burning up!”**

The operator looks increasingly confused.

The calls keep coming.

---

### 🌆 GERMANY — CITY

Police vehicles move through the streets.

Ambulances race toward hospitals.

People continue walking.

Cars continue moving.

Shops remain open.

Most people have no idea what is happening.

But somewhere among them...

people are already infected.

The escaped infected is still somewhere in the city.

And the man who was attacked earlier is lying unconscious in his home.

His strange infection mark continues spreading.

---

### 🌍 ELSEWHERE

The news begins showing short reports about the strange incidents in Germany.

In California...

Harry Wilson watches the screen.

David Anderson stands beside him.

In Hawaii...

Chris Bennett and Emma Bennett hear people discussing the footage.

In Mumbai...

Kartik Sharma notices the same story appearing on his phone.

In Seoul...

Do Do-Hee watches the news.

And in Daegu...

Kim Wong-jun sees the video spreading online.

Nobody knows what it means.

Nobody knows how far it has spread.

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — NIGHT

The ambulance arrives at the infected man's house.

Paramedics rush inside.

His wife points toward the living room.

**WIFE:**
**“He's there!”**

The paramedics kneel beside him.

One checks his pulse.

**PARAMEDIC:**
**“He's alive.”**

They prepare to move him.

The camera focuses on his arm.

The infection mark has spread further.

The paramedic doesn't notice.

Not yet.

---

### 🌑 FINAL MOMENTS

Across Germany...

More emergency calls come in.

More people are being attacked.

More people are becoming sick.

The authorities still don't understand the connection.

The public still thinks these are isolated incidents.

But they aren't.

The camera rises above the city.

Thousands of people continue their normal lives.

And hidden among them...

**the infection has begun to spread.**

**CUT TO BLACK.**

# 🧟 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 3 — THE WORLD STARTS WATCHING

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — OUTSIDE THE INFECTED MAN HOME 

The ambulance doors open.

The infected man is rushed inside on a stretcher.

His wife follows behind.

**WIFE:**
**“Please! Help him!”**

The medical staff immediately take over.

**DOCTOR:**
**“Ma'am, please wait outside.”**

**WIFE:**
**“But he's my husband!”**

**DOCTOR:**
**“We'll do everything we can.”**

The doors close.

She stands outside, frightened.

Inside, doctors examine the man.

His temperature is extremely high.

His breathing is irregular.

The strange mark on his arm has spread further.

A nurse looks at it.

**NURSE:**
**“Doctor... have you seen this before?”**

The doctor examines the mark.

**DOCTOR:**
**“No.”**

---

### ❤️ THE FAMILY

Outside the room, his wife sits alone.

She looks at her phone.

There are several missed calls from family members.

She doesn't answer.

She keeps staring at the hospital doors.

A doctor eventually walks out.

**WIFE:**
**“Is he okay?”**

The doctor pauses.

**DOCTOR:**
**“We don't know yet.”**

**WIFE:**
**“What happened to him?”**

**DOCTOR:**
**“We're still trying to understand it.”**

She looks confused.

**WIFE:**
**“He was perfectly fine this morning.”**

The doctor has no answer.

---

### 🇩🇪 INSIDE THE ROOM

The man lies unconscious.

Machines monitor him.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

His breathing slows.

The monitor begins showing a weaker heartbeat.

**BEEP...**

A doctor looks at the screen.

**DOCTOR:**
**“His heart rate is dropping.”**

**BEEP...**

The heartbeat becomes slower.

**BEEP...**

Then—

**FLATLINE.**

Everyone freezes.

**NURSE:**
**“He's gone into cardiac arrest!”**

Doctors rush toward him.

---

### 🧬 UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH

Sarah sits alone in a dark room.

Her tablet displays several infection readings.

She scrolls through them.

One infected.

Then another.

Then several more.

Her expression changes.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“No...”**

She opens another file.

The infection patterns are changing.

Some readings don't match John's original data.

She quickly compares them.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“That's impossible.”**

A warning appears:

**UNEXPECTED VARIATION DETECTED**

Sarah stares at it.

She expected the virus to spread.

She did **not** expect it to change this quickly.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“You're changing...”**

She looks at the screen.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“Why?”**

Another warning appears.

**VARIATION RATE: INCREASING**

Sarah's expression turns serious.

For the first time...

she looks genuinely afraid of what she created.

---

### 🌍 THE WORLD STARTS WATCHING

News channels begin reporting the strange incidents in Germany.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
**“Authorities in Germany are investigating a growing number of unexplained violent incidents.”**

Footage shows police vehicles outside hospitals.

Another channel reports:

**NEWS ANCHOR 2:**
**“Officials say there is currently no confirmed explanation for the incidents.”**

The story spreads internationally.

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK

Michael Carter watches the news.

Henry Rivers stands beside him.

Alex Brooks looks at his phone.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
**“It's everywhere now.”**

Michael looks at the television.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
**“They're still calling them isolated incidents.”**

**HENRY RIVERS:**
**“Do you believe that?”**

Michael doesn't answer.

---

### 🇺🇸 CALIFORNIA

Harry Wilson watches another report.

David Anderson sits nearby.

**HARRY WILSON:**
**“This started with that hospital.”**

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
**“And now there are attacks across the city.”**

They continue watching.

---

### 🇮🇳 MUMBAI

Kartik Sharma scrolls through the latest reports on his phone.

He stops.

The same video appears again.

Germany.

The hospital.

The infected.

Kartik looks concerned.

---

### 🇰🇷 SEOUL

Do Do-Hee watches the news on her phone.

A notification appears:

**BREAKING NEWS — GERMANY**

She opens it.

Her expression becomes serious.

---

### 🇰🇷 DAEGU

Kim Wong-jun sees the same report.

He watches silently.

The video ends.

He looks around.

Everything outside is completely normal.

For now.

---

### 🚨 THE FIRST PUBLIC PANIC

Back in Germany...

The news continues spreading.

People begin sharing videos.

Rumours appear everywhere.

*"It's a new drug."*

*"It's a terrorist attack."*

*"It's some kind of disease."*

*"The government is hiding something."*

Nobody knows the truth.

Crowds begin forming outside supermarkets.

People start buying food.

Water.

Medicine.

Anything they think they might need.

A shopkeeper looks at the growing crowd.

**SHOPKEEPER:**
**“What's going on?”**

A customer answers:

**CUSTOMER:**
**“People are saying something is happening across the city.”**

The shopkeeper looks outside.

More people are arriving.

---

### 🇩🇪 HOSPITAL

Inside the hospital...

The man's wife suddenly hears shouting from the corridor.

She stands up.

A nurse runs past.

**WIFE:**
**“What's happening?”**

The nurse doesn't stop.

**NURSE:**
**“Please stay here!”**

The wife looks toward the emergency room.

Inside...

The monitor is still flatlining.

**BEEEEEEEEEP...**

A doctor continues trying to revive the man.

Then—

**BEEP.**

Everyone freezes.

The monitor shows a heartbeat.

**BEEP.**

Another.

**BEEP.**

The man's fingers twitch.

The doctor looks at him.

His eyes remain closed.

Then his eyes suddenly open.

They are beginning to turn—

**RED.**

The doctor takes a step backwards.

---

### 🌑 FINAL MOMENTS

Sarah watches the infection data.

Germany is panicking.

News channels around the world are watching.

Michael.

Harry.

Chris.

Kartik.

Do Do-Hee.

Kim Wong-jun.

All of them are seeing the same story.

But none of them understand what it really means.

Sarah looks at the growing numbers on her screen.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“This wasn't supposed to happen.”**

The screen continues updating.

**INFECTED: 2**

The number changes.

**INFECTED: 7**

Sarah stares.

Another update.

**INFECTED: 13**

Her expression freezes.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

# 🧟 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 4 — THE WARNING

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — HOSPITAL

The infected man's eyes slowly turn red.

The doctor takes another step backwards.

**DOCTOR:**
**“Everyone... move away from him.”**

The man suddenly sits upright.

**NURSE:**
**“Doctor...”**

The infected man looks around the room.

He doesn't speak.

He simply stares.

Then—

**BANG!**

He suddenly lunges at the nearest doctor.

**DOCTOR:**
**“RUN!”**

The medical staff scatter.

The doctor crashes into a table.

The infected man gets off the bed and charges toward the door.

**BANG!**

The door swings open.

He runs into the corridor.

Patients scream.

**PATIENT:**
**“WHAT IS HAPPENING?!”**

A nurse presses the emergency button.

**BEEP! BEEP! BEEP!**

Security officers rush toward the corridor.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
**“Get everyone out!”**

The infected man charges.

The officers try to stop him.

He knocks one of them to the ground.

People begin running.

The hospital suddenly descends into chaos.

Meanwhile, the wife had already come to the hospital for her husband.

---

### ❤️ OUTSIDE THE ROOM

The infected man's wife hears the screaming.

She stands up.

**WIFE:**
**“What's happening to my husband?”**

Nobody answers.

She sees people running down the corridor.

A nurse grabs her arm.

**NURSE:**
**“You need to leave!”**

**WIFE:**
**“Where is my husband?!”**

The nurse looks frightened.

**NURSE:**
**“He's... not responding normally.”**

The wife freezes.

She looks toward the corridor.

Her husband's silhouette disappears around the corner.

---

### 🚨 THE FIRST OFFICIAL WARNING

Hours later...

The situation has become impossible to ignore.

News channels interrupt their normal programming.

A government press conference begins.

A German official stands behind a podium.

Cameras flash.

**GERMAN OFFICIAL:**
**“We are currently investigating a number of unexplained violent incidents occurring in several locations.”**

Reporters immediately begin shouting questions.

**REPORTER:**
**“Are these incidents connected?”**

The official pauses.

**GERMAN OFFICIAL:**
**“We are investigating that possibility.”**

Another reporter stands.

**REPORTER 2:**
**“Is there a danger to the public?”**

The official looks serious.

**GERMAN OFFICIAL:**
**“As a precaution, we strongly advise citizens to remain indoors and avoid areas where violent incidents have been reported.”**

The room becomes silent.

**GERMAN OFFICIAL:**
**“Please remain calm and follow instructions from local authorities.”**

The press conference ends.

Across Germany...

Millions of people have heard the warning.

---

### 🏙️ NORMAL LIFE BREAKS

A school bell rings.

Students begin leaving their classrooms.

Then a teacher receives a message.

She looks at her phone.

Her expression changes.

**TEACHER:**
**“Everyone, stay inside for a moment.”**

Students begin whispering.

**STUDENT:**
**“What's happening?”**

The teacher doesn't answer.

---

### 🚇 GERMANY — TRAIN STATION

A large crowd waits for a train.

Everyone's phones suddenly start receiving the same emergency notification.

People look down at their screens.

**EMERGENCY ALERT**

**“Citizens are advised to remain indoors and avoid affected areas.”**

People begin looking at one another.

Some immediately leave.

Others start calling their families.

---

### 🛒 SUPERMARKET

A supermarket becomes packed.

People grab food.

Water.

Medicine.

Batteries.

The shelves begin emptying.

A customer looks at the crowd.

**CUSTOMER:**
**“Why is everyone buying so much?”**

Another customer answers:

**CUSTOMER 2:**
**“Did you not hear the warning?”**

The first customer looks worried.

---

### 🚗 CITY STREET

Traffic becomes heavier.

People try to leave the city.

Police vehicles block several roads.

Sirens echo through the streets.

**SIREN... SIREN... SIREN...**

A police officer speaks through a loudspeaker.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“Please remain calm and return to your homes.”**

But people continue moving.

Normal life is beginning to disappear.

---

### 🌍 THE WORLD REACTS

The German warning reaches international news.

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK

Michael Carter watches the press conference replay.

Henry Rivers stands beside him.

Alex Brooks scrolls through his phone.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
**“They're telling everyone to stay inside.”**

**HENRY RIVERS:**
**“That's serious.”**

Michael keeps watching the screen.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
**“Something bigger is happening.”**

---

### 🇺🇸 CALIFORNIA

Harry Wilson watches the news.

David Anderson sits beside him.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
**“Do you think it can reach here?”**

Harry doesn't answer immediately.

**HARRY WILSON:**
**“I don't know.”**

He looks back at the television.

---

### 🇺🇸 HAWAII

Chris Bennett and Emma Bennett watch the report.

Emma lowers her phone.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
**“They're actually telling people to stay home.”**

Chris nods.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
**“Something's definitely wrong.”**

---

### 🇮🇳 MUMBAI

Kartik Sharma watches the German warning on his phone.

He looks around at the busy streets.

Everything around him is normal.

For now.

---

### 🇰🇷 SEOUL

Do Do-Hee watches the international report.

Her phone receives another notification.

**BREAKING NEWS — GERMANY**

She opens it.

The number of reported incidents has increased again.

---

### 🇰🇷 DAEGU

Kim Wong-jun watches the news.

He looks outside.

People are still walking normally.

Cars are still moving.

But now...

everyone knows about Germany.

---

### 🧬 UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH

Sarah sits in front of her computer.

The infection data continues changing.

She looks at the numbers.

**INFECTED: 13**

The number changes.

**INFECTED: 18**

Sarah's eyes widen.

Another update.

**INFECTED: 24**

She quickly opens the patient profiles.

The infection is progressing differently in different people.

Some are changing faster.

Some are changing slower.

Sarah compares the data.

Her expression becomes increasingly worried.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“They're not following the same pattern...”**

She opens John's original data.

Then the second infected.

Then the newest cases.

The patterns don't match.

Sarah leans back.

She finally understands something.

The virus isn't simply spreading.

**It's changing.**

Her computer displays another warning.

**VARIATION RATE: INCREASING**

Sarah stares at the screen.

Another notification appears.

**NEW INFECTED DETECTED**

She freezes.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“No...”**

The number changes again.

**INFECTED: 31**

Sarah slowly stands.

For the first time...

she looks completely terrified.

**SARAH SMITH:**
**“What are you becoming?”**

The screen continues updating.

**INFECTED: 32**

**INFECTED: 33**

**INFECTED: 34**

Sarah watches helplessly.

The numbers keep rising.

---

### 🌑 FINAL MOMENTS

Germany is no longer behaving normally.

People are staying inside.

Hospitals are struggling.

Police are responding to multiple incidents.

The world is watching.

And Sarah...

is losing control.

The computer continues counting.

**INFECTED: 41**

**INFECTED: 42**

**INFECTED: 43**

Sarah doesn't move.

### PART 6 — THE ESCAPE

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — AIRPORT

**FLIGHT CANCELLED.**

Then another.

**FLIGHT CANCELLED.**

**MAN:**
**“The roads are blocked too.”**

Security officers inspect passengers more carefully than usual.

**FATHER:**
**“Anywhere outside Germany.”**

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
**“Next.”**

---

### 🧳 AIRPORT TERMINAL

A little girl holds her mother's hand.

**GIRL:**
**“Mom... are we leaving Germany?”**

Her mother looks down at her.

**MOTHER:**
**“Yes.”**

**GIRL:**
**“Why?”**

The mother doesn't know what to say.

She simply holds her daughter's hand tighter.

---

### 📢 AIRPORT ANNOUNCEMENT

**AIRPORT ANNOUNCEMENT:**
**“Attention passengers. Several flights have been delayed due to the ongoing emergency situation.”**

People groan.

Someone starts arguing with airport staff.

**PASSENGER:**
**“My family has been waiting here for hours!”**

**AIRPORT STAFF:**
**“Sir, please step back.”**

Security officers move closer.

---

### 🚆 TRAIN STATION

The situation isn't better.

The station is overflowing.

People push toward the platforms.

Announcements echo through the building.

**ANNOUNCEMENT:**
**“Please do not push. Additional trains are being arranged.”**

A man looks at the crowd.

**MAN:**
**“Everyone's trying to get out.”**

---

### 🚨 OUTSIDE THE STATION

Police vehicles arrive.

Several officers try to control the crowd.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“Please remain calm!”**

People continue pushing forward.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
**“Everyone will be processed!”**

A woman points toward the station.

**WOMAN:**
**“My family is inside!”**

---

### 🇩🇪 CITY ROAD

Cars stretch as far as the eye can see.

**HONK! HONK!**

A driver looks at the traffic ahead.

Nothing is moving.

He looks at his phone.

Another emergency notification appears.

**EMERGENCY ALERT:**
**“Citizens are advised to avoid affected areas.”**

The driver looks toward his family.

**DRIVER:**
**“We're not getting through this road.”**

---

### ✈️ AIRPORT — LATER

A few departure gates remain open.

Passengers rush toward them.

A final boarding announcement plays.

**AIRPORT ANNOUNCEMENT:**
**“Final boarding call for Flight 782.”**

People immediately start moving.

Security checks continue.

The airport is still tense.

But some planes are leaving.

---

### ✈️ RUNWAY

A passenger plane begins moving.

It reaches the runway.

Then—

**WHOOSH!**

The plane takes off.

For the passengers inside...

Germany is disappearing beneath them.

---

### 🇩🇪 AIRPORT — ANOTHER TERMINAL

A security officer watches passengers entering a restricted area.

His radio suddenly activates.

**RADIO:**
**“Security team to Terminal B.”**

He looks concerned.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
**“What's happening?”**

**RADIO:**
**“Possible disturbance.”**

He immediately moves toward the terminal.

---

### 🚨 TERMINAL B

A crowd gathers around a man.

He looks pale.

Sweat covers his face.

A woman steps away from him.

**WOMAN:**
**“Sir... are you okay?”**

The man doesn't answer.

He looks confused.

An airport employee approaches.

**AIRPORT EMPLOYEE:**
**“Sir, do you need medical assistance?”**

The man suddenly grabs the wall for support.

**MAN:**
**“I... don't feel well.”**

The employee immediately calls medical staff.

---

### 🚑 AIRPORT MEDICAL AREA

Medical workers examine the man.

His temperature is extremely high.

The employee looks worried.

**MEDICAL WORKER:**
**“We need to move him somewhere isolated.”**

The man is placed on a stretcher.

Passengers watch from a distance.

Nobody knows what is happening.

---

### ✈️ DEPARTURE GATE

Another plane prepares to leave.

Passengers board quickly.

One passenger looks back through the window.

Outside, police vehicles move across the airport.

He looks uneasy.

The aircraft doors close.

---

### 🇩🇪 AIRPORT — FINAL SCENE

The terminal remains crowded.

Some people are still trying to leave.

Others are being told their flights are cancelled.

Security continues checking passengers.

A plane takes off.

Another is grounded.

Another prepares for boarding.

Germany is trying to close itself off...

while thousands are desperately trying to get out.

---

### 🌍 SOMEWHERE ABOVE EUROPE

A passenger sits quietly beside the airplane window.

He looks down at the clouds.

His phone shows a news notification.

**BREAKING NEWS — GERMANY EMERGENCY**

He turns the phone off.

He doesn't notice the small mark on his arm.

He closes his eyes.

The plane continues flying.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 7 — THE ARRIVAL

---

### ✈️ SCENE 1 — THE PLANE

**HOURS AFTER LEAVING GERMANY**

The plane continues through the clouds.

Passengers are calm.

Some sleep.

Some watch movies.

Some talk quietly.

The infected passenger sits by the window.

He checks his phone.

No new notifications.

He looks completely normal.

Then he rubs his arm.

The small infection mark is still there.

He quickly pulls his sleeve over it.

**PASSENGER:**
*"Probably nothing."*

The plane continues flying.

---

### 🛬 SCENE 2 — THE ARRIVAL

**HOURS LATER**

The plane lands.

**AIRPORT — ANOTHER COUNTRY**

The passengers begin leaving.

The infected passenger walks through the terminal with everyone else.

Nobody stops him.

Nobody knows.

He collects his luggage.

Then walks towards the exit.

---

### 🛂 SCENE 3 — THE CHECKPOINT

Airport authorities have increased security.

Passengers arriving from Germany are being checked.

The infected passenger reaches the checkpoint.

**OFFICER:**
"Passport, please."

He hands it over.

The officer checks it.

**OFFICER:**
"Where did you travel from?"

**PASSENGER:**
"Germany."

The officer looks at him.

**OFFICER:**
"Any fever? Coughing? Breathing problems?"

The passenger thinks for a moment.

**PASSENGER:**
"No."

The officer lets him pass.

**OFFICER:**
"Next."

Another passenger steps forward.

The infected passenger walks away.

The screening continues.

Thousands of people are being checked.

But the system cannot detect what has not happened yet.

---

### 🇩🇪 SCENE 4 — NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA

**COLOGNE**

People run from an infected crowd.

**DÜSSELDORF**

Officers struggle to control the crowd.

**DORTMUND**

A hospital emergency entrance is packed.

Doctors rush between stretchers.

**ESSEN**

Sirens echo through nearly empty streets.

The situation is getting worse.

---

### 🇩🇪 SCENE 5 — IT'S SPREADING

**BAVARIA — MUNICH**

Police respond to multiple attacks.

People lock themselves inside buildings.

**HESSE — FRANKFURT**

An emergency shelter fills with frightened people.

**LOWER SAXONY — HANOVER**

A train station is evacuated.

Passengers are ordered outside.

**SAXONY — DRESDEN**

Police block an entire street.

People watch from behind windows.

The infection is no longer limited to one state.

---

### 🏠 SCENE 6 — HOME

**ANOTHER COUNTRY**

The infected passenger finally reaches his destination.

He opens the door.

His family is waiting.

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
"You're finally home!"

The passenger smiles.

**PASSENGER:**
"Yeah. Long flight."

He puts his luggage down.

His family continues talking.

Everything feels normal.

For now.

---

### ✈️ SCENE 7 — TOO MANY PLANES

A plane takes off.

**CUT.**

Another plane takes off.

**CUT.**

Another.

**CUT.**

Departure boards display destinations across the world.

**LONDON**

**PARIS**

**MADRID**

**NEW YORK**

**MUMBAI**

**SEOUL**

But the world is already connected.

Police vehicles race through an intersection.

**HAMBURG**

People flee a shopping district.

**FRANKFURT**

An emergency announcement tells residents to remain indoors.

**MUNICH**

Hospital workers struggle to keep up.

**COLOGNE**

A barricade collapses.

**DÜSSELDORF**

More infected appear in the streets.

**DORTMUND**

The streets become almost empty.

Traffic moves normally.

**LONDON**

Passengers leave an airport.

**PARIS**

**MADRID**

People walk through crowded streets.

The world continues living normally.

Nobody realises how close the infection already is.

### 🏠 SCENE 10 — SOMETHING IS WRONG

His family talks nearby.

His breathing gets heavier.

He looks down at his arm.

The infection mark has spread.

He pulls his sleeve down quickly.

He looks around.

**PASSENGER:**
*"Something's wrong..."*

He stands.

His breathing becomes even heavier.

The room falls silent.

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 8 — THE FIRST FOREIGN CASE

---

### 🏠 SCENE 1 — SOMETHING IS WRONG

**ANOTHER COUNTRY — NIGHT**

The infected passenger sits on the edge of his bed.

His breathing is heavy.

His family watches him.

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
"Are you okay?"

He doesn't answer.

He looks at his arm.

The infection mark has spread further.

His hands begin shaking.

**PASSENGER:**
"I... I don't feel well."

He suddenly stands.

His breathing becomes faster.

He collapses.

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
"Call an ambulance!"

---

### 🚑 SCENE 2 — THE AMBULANCE

Paramedics rush inside.

The passenger is unconscious.

They check his pulse.

**PARAMEDIC:**
"He's got a heartbeat."

They place him on a stretcher.

His family follows.

The ambulance doors slam shut.

**SIREN.**

---

### 🏥 SCENE 3 — THE HOSPITAL

The passenger is rushed into an emergency room.

Doctors examine him.

**DOCTOR:**
"High fever."

**NURSE:**
"His breathing is getting worse."

The doctor looks at the strange mark on his arm.

**DOCTOR:**
"What's that?"

Nobody knows.

The monitor begins beeping faster.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

His heart rate suddenly drops.

**DOCTOR:**
"His heart rate is falling!"

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

**FLATLINE.**

**NURSE:**
"He's gone into cardiac arrest!"

Doctors begin resuscitation.

---

### ❤️ SCENE 4 — THE RETURN

**BANG!**

The defibrillator is used.

Nothing.

Again.

**BANG!**

Silence.

Then—

**BEEP.**

The monitor comes back.

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

The passenger's fingers twitch.

His eyes slowly open.

The doctor steps closer.

The passenger's eyes begin turning **RED**.

**DOCTOR:**
"Step back."

The passenger suddenly sits upright.

---

### 🚨 SCENE 5 — THE FIRST ATTACK ABROAD

The passenger lunges at the doctor.

The doctor barely moves out of the way.

**DOCTOR:**
"RUN!"

The nurse escapes through the door.

The passenger follows.

A hospital worker sees him.

**WORKER:**
"What is happening?!"

The infected rushes towards him.

The worker runs.

A crash echoes through the corridor.

**BANG!**

The emergency alarm begins screaming.

**SIREN! SIREN! SIREN!**

The first attack outside Germany has happened.

---

### 🚔 SCENE 6 — POLICE ARRIVE

Police vehicles arrive outside.

Officers rush into the hospital.

**OFFICER:**
"Where is the violent patient?"

A nurse points towards the corridor.

**NURSE:**
"He's in there!"

The officers move carefully.

They hear footsteps.

**THUD.**

They stop.

The infected suddenly charges.

**OFFICER:**
"BACK!"

The officers retreat.

The hospital doors slam shut.

---

### 🇩🇪 SCENE 7 — GERMANY

**BERLIN**

A police convoy moves through the city.

**HAMBURG**

Emergency workers evacuate a building.

**MUNICH**

Another hospital reaches full capacity.

**FRANKFURT**

Police block an entire road.

**STUTTGART**

People run through a railway station.

**COLOGNE**

More infected appear in the streets.

**DÜSSELDORF**

A barricade is overwhelmed.

The map of Germany continues filling with warning markers.

---

### 🇩🇪 SCENE 8 — MORE STATES

**RHINELAND-PALATINATE**

Emergency calls increase.

**BADEN-WÜRTTEMBERG**

Police respond to multiple attacks.

**LOWER SAXONY**

Several towns begin imposing emergency restrictions.

**BAVARIA**

Hospitals request additional medical supplies.

The outbreak is spreading across Germany.

---

### 🧬 SCENE 9 — SARAH

**UNKNOWN LOCATION**

Sarah studies two sets of infection data.

One belongs to John Wright.

The other belongs to the foreign passenger.

She compares them.

The patterns are different.

Sarah freezes.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"They changed again."

She checks the readings.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"John's infection was different..."

She looks at the foreign case.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"...and now this."

She looks at the screen for several seconds.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"What are you becoming?"

---

### 🛑 SCENE 10 — CLOSE THE BORDERS

Announcements echo through the terminal.

Passengers are redirected.

Security checks become stricter.

At another airport, more flights are suspended.

At another, German arrivals are sent for additional screening.

Governments are beginning to restrict travel from Germany.

But officials receive a new report.

**OFFICIAL:**
"How many people left before the restrictions?"

An assistant looks at the data.

**ASSISTANT:**
"Thousands."

The official goes silent.

The restrictions came too late.

---

### ✈️ SCENE 11 — ALREADY OUT THERE

He is healthy.

---

Healthy.

---

Another passenger exits an airport.

Healthy.

---

Another passenger gets into a taxi.

Healthy.

---

**PARIS**

Healthy.

None of them know what is happening in Germany.

And none of them are infected.

---

### 📺 SCENE 12 — THE FIRST FOREIGN CASE

**INTERNATIONAL NEWSROOM**

A news anchor receives an update.

The expression on the anchor's face changes.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
"Authorities have confirmed a case outside Germany connected to the recent outbreak."

A map appears.

Germany is highlighted.

Then another country.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
"The patient had recently travelled from Germany."

The broadcast continues.

This is no longer a problem contained within Germany.

---

### 🇩🇪 SCENE 13 — GERMANY

More infected.

### 🌍 FINAL SCENE

**SIREN...**

**SIREN...**

**SIREN...**

Germany.

Different.

"More than yesterday."

Countries across Europe.

The problem is no longer only inside Germany.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"This one changed faster."

Then another.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"And they're not all changing at the same rate."

The infection is behaving differently in different people.

**SARAH SMITH:**
"Why?"

# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 5 — EVERYTHING CHANGES

### PART 10 — THE FIRST BITES

**FOREIGN CITY — NIGHT**

The streets are quieter now.

Police vehicles block the road outside the hospital.

The escaped infected is still somewhere in the city.

Two police officers move carefully through a side street.

**OFFICER 1:**
“Keep your eyes open.”

**OFFICER 2:**
“Yeah.”

A noise comes from behind them.

They turn.

The infected is standing at the end of the street.

Still.

Watching them.

**OFFICER 1:**
“Police! Stay where you are!”

The infected suddenly charges.

**OFFICER 2:**
“MOVE!”

Both officers step back.

Officer 1 grabs the infected's arm, trying to stop him.

The infected violently twists around.

Before Officer 1 can react—

**BITE.**

The infected bites his arm.

**OFFICER 1:**
“AAAH!”

Officer 2 pulls the infected away.

**OFFICER 2:**
“GET OFF HIM!”

The infected turns toward Officer 2.

He charges again.

Officer 2 tries to push him away.

The infected grabs his shoulder.

**BITE.**

Officer 2 screams and falls backward.

The infected releases him and runs down the street.

**OFFICER 1:**
“Don't let him get away!”

But the infected disappears around the corner.

The two officers remain on the ground.

Both look at their wounds.

**OFFICER 2:**
“What... was that?”

**OFFICER 1:**
“I don't know.”

Officer 1 reaches for his radio.

**OFFICER 1:**
“We need medical assistance. Two officers injured.”

A response comes through.

**RADIO:**
“Copy. Ambulance on the way.”

---

### 🏥 THE MEDICAL CHECK

Minutes later.

An ambulance arrives.

The two officers are taken inside.

A doctor examines the wounds.

**DOCTOR:**
“These are serious bites.”

**OFFICER 1:**
“Can you tell what attacked us?”

The doctor looks at the wound.

**DOCTOR:**
“No.”

The doctor cleans the wound.

**DOCTOR:**
“We'll monitor both of you.”

The officers are moved into separate beds.

Neither has a fever.

Neither has red eyes.

Neither feels different.

For now.

---

### 🩸 SOMETHING BEGINS

The doctor leaves the room.

Officer 1 looks down at his arm.

A tiny dark mark appears near the bite.

He touches it.

**OFFICER 1:**
“That's strange...”

Across the room, Officer 2 suddenly looks at his own wound.

A similar mark begins spreading.

Neither notices the connection.

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — SAME NIGHT

Police vehicles race through Berlin.

Another ambulance rushes past.

A hospital entrance is surrounded by people.

A police officer shouts at civilians to move back.

Across Germany, emergency calls continue increasing.

The country is no longer dealing with isolated incidents.

The outbreak is spreading faster.

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK

Michael, Henry and Alex sit together watching the news.

A reporter speaks from Germany.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Authorities continue to investigate the violent incidents spreading across multiple German cities. Officials are also investigating a possible related case outside Germany.”

Michael looks up.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Outside Germany?”

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“That's what she said.”

Alex looks at the television.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“So it got out.”

Michael doesn't answer.

---

### 🇮🇳 MUMBAI

Kartik walks through a crowded street.

His phone vibrates.

He checks the notification.

> **BREAKING NEWS — CASE REPORTED OUTSIDE GERMANY**

Kartik stares at the screen.

Around him, everyone continues walking normally.

Nobody seems worried yet.

---

### 🇰🇷 SEOUL

Do Do-Hee receives the same notification.

She stops walking.
