**DO DO-HEE:**
“Outside Germany...?”

---

### 🇰🇷 DAEGU

Kim Wong-jun sees the same news.

He looks toward the busy street.

Everything is normal.

For now.

---

### 🧬 SARAH'S LAB

Sarah watches several screens.

One screen shows Germany.

Another shows the foreign case.

She compares the data.

Then—

A new line appears.

> **NEW EXPOSURES: 2**

Sarah freezes.

She checks the information again.

The two new cases are connected to the foreign infected.

Sarah understands.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“They were bitten.”

She watches the infection readings.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“So that's how you're spreading now...”

Another warning appears.

> **TRANSMISSION CONFIRMED**

Sarah's expression changes.

The virus isn't behaving the way she expected.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“It's spreading differently.”

She looks at the growing data.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“And it's changing.”

---

### 🏥 FOREIGN HOSPITAL

Officer 1 lies on the bed.

His eyes are closed.

His breathing becomes slightly heavier.

The infection mark spreads a little farther up his arm.

Across the room, Officer 2's mark does the same.

A heart monitor continues beeping.

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

**BEEP.**

Neither officer wakes.

Yet.

---

### 🌎 FINAL MONTAGE

Germany.

Sirens.

Police.

Empty streets.

Hospitals.

Running people.

---

The foreign city.

Police vehicles.

The escaped infected somewhere in the darkness.

---

New York.

Michael watching the news.

---

Mumbai.

Kartik walking through the crowd.

---

Seoul.

Do Do-Hee staring at her phone.

---

Daegu.

Kim Wong-jun looking outside.

---

Sarah's laboratory.

She stares at the screen.

> **INFECTED: 43**

The number changes.

> **44**

Sarah's eyes widen.

Then—

> **45**

She slowly steps away from the screen.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“What are you becoming?”

### CUT TO BLACK.

# END OF EPISODE 5



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 1 — THE MEMORY

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — MORNING

The city is quieter than it used to be.

People still walk to work.

Cars still move through the streets.

Cafés are open.

But almost everyone has their phone in their hand.

Germany is on every news channel.

Michael Carter sits alone outside a café.

His phone is on the table.

A breaking-news notification appears.

> **OUTBREAK CONTINUES TO SPREAD**

Michael looks at it.

He doesn't open it.

Instead, he turns his phone face down.

A little boy runs past him.

His father follows behind, carrying the boy's backpack.

**FATHER:**
“Slow down!”

The boy laughs.

**BOY:**
“Catch me!”

Michael watches them.

His expression changes.

The laughter fades into something from his past.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

Michael is younger.

He's sitting at the kitchen table with his father.

His father is fixing an old wristwatch.

Michael watches carefully.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“Does it actually work?”

**FATHER:**
“Of course it works.”

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“You said that yesterday.”

His father laughs.

**FATHER:**
“Then ask me tomorrow.”

Michael smiles.

His father puts the watch around Michael's wrist.

**FATHER:**
“Take care of it.”

Michael looks at it.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“Why?”

**FATHER:**
“Because one day you'll want something that reminds you of home.”

Michael doesn't fully understand.

But he nods.

---

### 🌧️ ONE YEAR LATER

It's raining heavily.

Michael stands near the front door.

His father is putting on his jacket.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“Where are you going?”

**FATHER:**
“I've got to go to work.”

Michael looks outside.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“It's raining.”

**FATHER:**
“I'll be back tonight.”

Michael looks at the clock.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“Can you stay?”

His father smiles.

**FATHER:**
“I wish I could.”

He kneels beside Michael.

**FATHER:**
“I'll see you tonight.”

Michael nods.

His father opens the door.

Before leaving—

**FATHER:**
“Take care of that watch.”

Michael looks down at his wrist.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“I will.”

The door closes.

---

### 🌧️ THAT NIGHT

The clock moves past midnight.

Michael sits on the sofa.

The television is on.

His mother is beside him.

Michael keeps looking toward the front door.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“He said he'd be back.”

His mother doesn't answer.

A phone rings.

His mother picks it up.

Her expression changes.

Michael notices.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“Mom?”

She slowly puts the phone down.

Michael waits.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“What happened?”

His mother looks at him.

She can't speak.

Michael understands something is wrong.

---

### 🕰️ THE NEXT MORNING

Michael sits alone at the kitchen table.

The wristwatch is still on his arm.

He stares at it.

His mother sits across from him.

Neither speaks.

Michael finally looks up.

**YOUNG MICHAEL:**
“If I had asked him to stay...

Would he still be here?”

His mother immediately shakes her head.

**MOTHER:**
“Michael, no.”

But Michael doesn't believe her.

He looks down at the watch.

---

### 🇺🇸 PRESENT DAY — NEW YORK

Michael's fingers slowly touch his wrist.

The same watch is still there.

Older now.

Scratched.

But still working.

**TICK.**

**TICK.**

**TICK.**

Michael looks at it.

His eyes become watery.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I know it wasn't my fault.”

He pauses.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“But I still think about it.”

A tear rolls down his cheek.

He quickly wipes it away.

His phone vibrates.

A message from HENRY RIVERS:

> **Where are you?**

Michael looks at the message.

He types:

> **At the café.**

Another message arrives.

> **Coming.**

Michael puts the phone away.

He looks at the watch one more time.

Then quietly whispers—

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I should've asked you to stay.”

He closes his hand around the watch.

The ticking continues.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 1**



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 2 — THE CALL

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — MORNING

Michael Carter walks back into the café.

Henry Rivers is already there.

He is sitting at their usual table.

Alex Brooks is across from him, looking at the news on his phone.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Took you long enough.”

Michael sits down.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Sorry.”

Henry looks at him.

He notices something is different.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“You okay?”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Yeah.”

Henry doesn't look convinced.

Alex puts his phone down.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Germany's getting worse.”

Michael looks at the television above the counter.

Another report is playing.

> **NEWS REPORTER:**
> “Authorities continue to investigate the growing number of violent incidents—”

Henry turns the volume down.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“I don't know why, but this whole thing feels familiar.”

Michael looks at him.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Familiar?”

Henry looks down at the table.

For a moment, he doesn't answer.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Not the outbreak.”

He pauses.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“The feeling.”

Michael waits.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Like something is happening and you can't do anything about it.”

Alex stays quiet.

Henry slowly leans back.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

Henry is younger.

He sits on his bed, playing a video game.

His phone rings.

He looks at the screen.

**CALLING — LUCAS**

Henry answers.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“What's up?”

There is a pause.

**LUCAS:**
“Can you come over?”

Henry looks at his game.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“Now?”

**LUCAS:**
“Yeah.”

Henry laughs.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“Why?”

Lucas doesn't answer immediately.

**LUCAS:**
“I just... don't want to be alone.”

Henry's smile fades slightly.

He looks toward the door.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“Okay. Give me a little while.”

**LUCAS:**
“Alright.”

The call ends.

Henry looks at the game.

Then at the clock.

He continues playing.

---

### 🌙 LATER THAT NIGHT

Henry's phone rings again.

It's Lucas.

Henry answers.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“Hey.”

Nothing.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“Lucas?”

Still nothing.

Henry sits up.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“Hello?”

The call suddenly ends.

Henry immediately calls back.

No answer.

Again.

No answer.

---

### 🚑 THE NEXT MORNING

Henry stands outside a hospital.

His parents are nearby.

A doctor walks toward them.

Henry watches his face.

The doctor stops.

**DOCTOR:**
“I'm sorry.”

Henry doesn't move.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“But... he called me.”

The doctor says nothing.

Henry looks down.

His hands begin shaking.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“He asked me to come.”

His voice breaks.

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“I said I'd come later.”

His mother puts an arm around him.

**MOTHER:**
“Henry...”

**YOUNG HENRY:**
“If I'd gone when he called...”

He can't finish.

---

### 🇺🇸 PRESENT DAY — CAFÉ

Henry is staring at the table.

Michael understands.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“You still think about that call?”

Henry gives a small nod.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Sometimes.”

Alex looks at him.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“You couldn't have known.”

Henry gives a faint smile.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“I know.”

A pause.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“That's the worst part.”

Michael looks at him.

Henry reaches for his phone.

He opens his old contacts.

Lucas' name is still there.

Henry doesn't call.

He just looks at it.

Then locks his phone.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“I keep thinking about what would've happened if I'd gone.”

Michael quietly answers.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Maybe you would've helped him.”

Henry looks at him.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Maybe you wouldn't have.”

A pause.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“But you couldn't have known.”

Henry nods.

He looks out the café window.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Yeah.”

The three sit silently.

For once, none of them looks at the news.

They just sit together.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 2**




# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 3 — THE WORDS WE NEVER SAID

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — AFTERNOON

The café is quieter now.

Michael Carter, Henry Rivers and Alex Brooks are still sitting together.

The news continues playing in the background.

None of them are really listening.

Alex looks at the empty chair beside their table.

For a moment, he seems lost in thought.

Henry notices.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What?”

Alex looks at him.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Nothing.”

Michael looks toward Alex.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“You've been staring at that chair for five minutes.”

Alex gives a small smile.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“It reminded me of someone.”

Henry leans forward.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Who?”

Alex looks down.

He takes a breath.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“My sister.”

Michael and Henry become quiet.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

Alex is younger.

He sits on the floor of his bedroom, surrounded by school books.

His older sister sits on the bed.

She is holding one of his notebooks.

**SISTER:**
“You call this handwriting?”

Alex looks up.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“It's readable.”

She laughs.

**SISTER:**
“Barely.”

She throws the notebook toward him.

Alex catches it.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“You're annoying.”

**SISTER:**
“And you're terrible at accepting criticism.”

Alex smiles.

She gets up.

**SISTER:**
“Come on.”

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“Where?”

**SISTER:**
“Kitchen.”

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“Why?”

She looks at him.

**SISTER:**
“Because I'm hungry.”

Alex laughs.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“That's your reason for everything.”

They leave the room together.

---

### 🌙 ONE EVENING

Alex and his sister sit on the balcony.

The city lights shine below them.

She looks at him.

**SISTER:**
“You know you can tell me anything, right?”

Alex shrugs.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“I know.”

**SISTER:**
“Good.”

She smiles.

**SISTER:**
“Because one day you're going to become successful and completely forget about me.”

Alex laughs.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“Never.”

She smiles.

**SISTER:**
“Promise?”

Alex looks at her.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“Promise.”

---

### 📱 DAYS LATER

Alex is walking home from school.

His phone vibrates.

A message from his sister.

> **Can we talk when you get home?**

Alex looks at the message.

He types.

> **Yeah.**

He puts his phone away.

A few seconds later, another message arrives.

> **Okay.**

Alex smiles.

He keeps walking.

---

### 🌧️ THAT EVENING

Alex comes home.

The house is unusually quiet.

His parents are standing in the living room.

Alex immediately knows something is wrong.

His father looks at him.

**FATHER:**
“Alex...”

Alex looks around.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“Where's my sister?”

Nobody answers.

Alex's face changes.

---

### 🏥 LATER

Alex sits outside a hospital room.

His parents sit nearby.

A doctor walks out.

Alex looks up.

The doctor slowly shakes his head.

Alex stares at the floor.

He remembers the message.

> **Can we talk when you get home?**

His eyes fill with tears.

**YOUNG ALEX:**
“She wanted to talk to me.”

His mother holds his hand.

Alex looks at his phone.

The message is still there.

He never found out what she wanted to say.

---

### 🇺🇸 PRESENT DAY — CAFÉ

Alex looks down at his hands.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“She messaged me that day.”

Michael quietly asks—

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“What did she want to tell you?”

Alex shakes his head.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“I don't know.”

Silence.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“I've thought about it for years.”

Henry looks at him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What do you think she would've said?”

Alex smiles sadly.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“I don't know.”

He looks toward the window.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“But I wish I'd asked.”

Michael puts his phone down.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“You loved her.”

Alex nods.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Yeah.”

He looks at the old message again.

His thumb hovers over it.

Then he locks the phone.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“I just wish I had one more conversation.”

The three sit quietly.

Outside, people continue walking.

Cars continue moving.

The world is still normal.

For now.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 3**




# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 4 — WHAT WE REMEMBER

### 🇺🇸 CALIFORNIA — AFTERNOON

The sun is beginning to set.

Harry Wilson and David Anderson sit on the roof of an apartment building.

The city stretches out in front of them.

Cars move along the streets.

People walk through the neighbourhood.

For a few minutes, neither of them says anything.

David holds a small photograph in his hand.

Harry notices it.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“You still carry that?”

David looks at the photograph.

A faint smile appears on his face.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Yeah.”

Harry leans back.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Who is it?”

David turns the photograph around.

It shows David as a child standing beside another boy.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“My cousin.”

Harry looks at the picture.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“You were close?”

David nods.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Very.”

He looks at the photograph again.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

A younger David and his cousin run through a park.

**YOUNG DAVID:**
“Wait!”

His cousin keeps running.

**COUSIN:**
“Catch me!”

David laughs and chases him.

They reach a small tree and stop.

Both are out of breath.

**COUSIN:**
“You're too slow.”

**YOUNG DAVID:**
“You cheated.”

**COUSIN:**
“That's called winning.”

David pushes him lightly.

They both laugh.

---

### 🌤️ ANOTHER DAY

The two sit on the same tree.

David is eating a snack.

His cousin looks at him.

**COUSIN:**
“Save some.”

**YOUNG DAVID:**
“No.”

**COUSIN:**
“David.”

**YOUNG DAVID:**
“You should've brought your own.”

His cousin grabs the packet.

**YOUNG DAVID:**
“Hey!”

They start fighting over it.

Then both burst out laughing.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS LATER

David sits alone in his room.

The photograph is on his desk.

His mother enters.

She doesn't say anything.

She simply places a hand on his shoulder.

David looks at the photograph.

He knows.

His cousin is gone.

There was no argument.

No unfinished promise.

No final conversation.

Just a person who had been part of his childhood suddenly missing from it.

---

### 🇺🇸 PRESENT DAY — ROOFTOP

David puts the photograph back into his pocket.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“I don't really remember the day he died.”

Harry looks at him.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Then what do you remember?”

David smiles.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Everything before it.”

Harry nods slowly.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“His laugh.”

A pause.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“The stupid jokes.”

Another pause.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“How he always cheated when we raced.”

Harry laughs quietly.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Sounds like you deserved to lose.”

David looks at him.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Shut up.”

They both laugh.

The laughter slowly fades.

Harry looks toward the city.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“I had someone too.”

David turns toward him.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Who?”

Harry doesn't answer immediately.

He looks down at his hands.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“My older brother.”

David waits.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

Harry and his older brother stand in their garage.

His brother is working on an old bicycle.

**YOUNG HARRY:**
“Does it even work?”

**BROTHER:**
“Of course.”

**YOUNG HARRY:**
“You said that yesterday.”

His brother smiles.

**BROTHER:**
“Then tomorrow I'll prove it.”

Harry laughs.

His brother finally gets the bicycle working.

**BROTHER:**
“See?”

Harry looks impressed.

**YOUNG HARRY:**
“Okay. That's actually cool.”

His brother grins.

**BROTHER:**
“Want to ride?”

Harry nods.

---

### 🌧️ YEARS LATER

Harry sits on the front steps of his house.

His brother's bicycle is beside him.

Nobody touches it anymore.

Harry runs his hand across the handlebar.

He remembers all the times his brother helped him.

Homework.

Arguments.

Jokes.

Everything.

Harry looks at the bicycle.

**YOUNG HARRY:**
“You were supposed to teach me.”

His voice breaks.

He looks away.

---

### 🇺🇸 PRESENT DAY — ROOFTOP

Harry takes a slow breath.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“I still have his bicycle.”

David looks at him.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“You ride it?”

Harry shakes his head.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“No.”

A pause.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“I don't know why.”

David looks toward the sunset.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Maybe because some things aren't meant to be used.”

Harry looks at him.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Maybe they're just meant to remind us.”

Harry gives a small smile.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Yeah.”

The two sit together.

No more words are needed.

The photograph remains in David's pocket.

And somewhere far away, an old bicycle waits in Harry's home.

Two different memories.

Two different losses.

But both still carried with them.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 4**




# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 5 — THE LAST SUMMER

### 🇺🇸 HAWAII — PRESENT DAY — EVENING

The sun is slowly disappearing over the ocean.

Chris Bennett and Emma Bennett sit together on the beach.

The waves move gently toward the shore.

Emma picks up a small shell.

She looks at it for a moment.

Chris notices.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“You remember this place?”

Emma looks toward the ocean.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Yeah.”

A small smile appears on her face.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Grandpa used to bring us here.”

Chris smiles.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Every summer.”

Emma looks down at the shell.

Her expression becomes distant.

---

### 🕰️ FLASHBACK — 10 YEARS EARLIER

The same beach.

But Chris and Emma are children.

They run across the sand.

Their grandfather follows behind them.

**YOUNG CHRIS:**
“Emma! Wait!”

**YOUNG EMMA:**
“Catch me!”

Chris runs after her.

Emma suddenly stops and points toward the sand.

**YOUNG EMMA:**
“Look!”

She picks up a shell.

**YOUNG CHRIS:**
“It's just a shell.”

Their grandfather walks over.

He looks at it seriously.

**GRANDFATHER:**
“No.”

Chris and Emma look at him.

**GRANDFATHER:**
“That’s a lucky shell.”

Emma's eyes widen.

**YOUNG EMMA:**
“Really?”

Their grandfather nods.

**GRANDFATHER:**
“Very lucky.”

Chris laughs.

**YOUNG CHRIS:**
“Grandpa, you say everything is lucky.”

Their grandfather smiles.

**GRANDFATHER:**
“Maybe everything is.”

Emma carefully puts the shell in her pocket.

---

### 🌅 LATER THAT DAY — 10 YEARS EARLIER

Chris, Emma and their grandfather sit near the water.

Chris throws small stones into the ocean.

Emma watches the waves.

Their grandfather looks at both of them.

**GRANDFATHER:**
“Promise me something.”

Chris looks at him.

**YOUNG CHRIS:**
“What?”

**GRANDFATHER:**
“Whatever happens... stay together.”

Chris looks at Emma.

Emma looks back at him.

**YOUNG EMMA:**
“We will.”

**YOUNG CHRIS:**
“Promise.”

Their grandfather smiles.

**GRANDFATHER:**
“Good.”

The three watch the sunset together.

---

### 🕰️ ONE YEAR LATER

The beach is empty.

Chris and Emma, now slightly older, sit beside each other.

The same ocean is in front of them.

Their grandfather is gone.

Emma holds the lucky shell tightly.

Chris looks toward the empty spot beside them.

Neither sibling says anything.

After a long silence—

**YOUNG EMMA:**
“I don't want to come here anymore.”

Chris looks at her.

**YOUNG CHRIS:**
“Why?”

Emma looks down.

**YOUNG EMMA:**
“Because Grandpa isn't here.”

Chris doesn't know what to say.

He simply sits beside her.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS LATER — PRESENT DAY

The sound of the ocean brings us back to the present.

Chris and Emma are teenagers now.

They are sitting in the exact same place.

Emma looks at the shell in her hand.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“You really stopped coming here.”

Emma nods.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Yeah.”

Chris looks toward the ocean.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“I didn't.”

Emma looks at him.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“You came here?”

Chris nods.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Sometimes.”

Emma smiles slightly.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Without me?”

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“You said you hated this place.”

Emma looks down at the shell.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“I hated remembering.”

Chris pauses.

Then he looks at the ocean.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“I think that's why I kept coming.”

Emma looks at him.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“So I wouldn't forget.”

A quiet moment passes.

Emma looks at the shell again.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“He'd probably call it lucky again.”

Chris smiles.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Definitely.”

Emma laughs softly.

The two siblings sit together.

The same place.

The same ocean.

The same memory.

But this time—

they aren't remembering it alone.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 5**



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 6 — THE LAST CALL

### 🇮🇳 MUMBAI — AFTERNOON

Mumbai is busy as usual.

Cars move through the streets.

People walk through crowded markets.

Kartik Sharma walks through the crowd, holding his phone.

A man nearby is talking to someone on a call.

**MAN:**
“Don't worry. You'll be fine.”

Kartik suddenly stops.

Those words hit him.

He looks down at his phone.

His expression changes.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

Kartik is younger.

He stands outside his home with a family member who is carrying a travel bag.

**KARTIK:**
“How long are you going for?”

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
“Just a few days.”

Kartik looks at the bag.

**KARTIK:**
“Then why did you pack so much?”

They laugh.

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
“You never know.”

Kartik smiles.

**KARTIK:**
“You'll be fine.”

The family member looks at him.

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
“Yeah.”

They smile at each other.

**FAMILY MEMBER:**
“I'll see you soon.”

Kartik nods.

**KARTIK:**
“See you.”

They leave.

Kartik watches them walk away.

Then turns back toward the house.

---

### 📱 THAT NIGHT

Kartik is sitting in his room.

His phone rings.

He answers.

**KARTIK:**
“Hello?”

There is silence on the other end.

Then—

A voice he doesn't recognise.

Kartik's expression slowly changes.

He listens.

His eyes widen.

The phone slips slightly from his hand.

---

### 🌧️ DAYS LATER

Kartik sits alone.

The house is quiet.

His phone is beside him.

He opens his contacts.

He finds the person's name.

He stares at it.

He doesn't press call.

Instead, he opens their last conversation.

The final message is still there.

> **I'll see you soon.**

Kartik reads it again.

And again.

His eyes become watery.

He whispers—

**KARTIK:**
“You weren't supposed to be gone.”

He looks down.

Then remembers his own words.

> **You'll be fine.**

Kartik closes his eyes.

---

### 🇮🇳 PRESENT DAY — MUMBAI

The memory fades.

Kartik is still standing in the middle of the crowded street.

The man on the phone has already walked away.

Kartik looks at his phone.

He opens his old messages.

The conversation is still there.

He scrolls up.

The final message appears again.

> **I'll see you soon.**

Kartik touches the screen.

**KARTIK:**
“I still remember.”

A pause.

**KARTIK:**
“I shouldn't have said that.”

He locks his phone.

He takes a breath.

Then starts walking again.

The crowd surrounds him.

Everyone keeps moving.

But Kartik carries those final words with him.

Not because they caused anything.

But because sometimes—

the smallest sentence can become the hardest memory to forget.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 6**



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 7 — THE EMPTY SEAT

### 🇰🇷 SEOUL — EVENING

The streets of Seoul glow beneath the evening lights.

Do Do-Hee walks through a busy neighbourhood.

People pass around her.

Couples laugh.

Friends talk.

A small café catches her attention.

She stops.

Through the window, she sees an empty table near the glass.

Her expression changes.

She remembers.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS AGO

Do Do-Hee is younger.

She sits at the same café.

Across from her is someone she loves.

Two cups of hot chocolate sit on the table.

**PERSON:**
“You're late.”

**YOUNG DO DO-HEE:**
“I'm five minutes late.”

**PERSON:**
“Five minutes is still late.”

Do Do-Hee rolls her eyes.

**YOUNG DO DO-HEE:**
“You could've started without me.”

**PERSON:**
“And miss this?”

They smile.

Do Do-Hee takes a sip.

**YOUNG DO DO-HEE:**
“This place is expensive.”

**PERSON:**
“Then stop ordering the biggest one.”

Do Do-Hee looks at the cup.

**YOUNG DO DO-HEE:**
“It was the biggest one?”

They both laugh.

---

### ☕ WEEKS LATER

They sit at the same table again.

The same drinks.

The same conversation.

The same laughter.

It becomes their place.

Every time they meet—

the same table.

---

### 🕰️ MONTHS LATER

Do Do-Hee sits alone at home.

Her phone is on the table.

A message appears.

She reads it.

Her expression slowly falls.

She doesn't reply.

She simply places the phone down.

---

### ☕ SOME TIME LATER

The café.

The same table.

Do Do-Hee walks inside.

She looks toward the chair across from her.

It's empty.

She sits down.

A waiter approaches.

**WAITER:**
“Same as usual?”

Do Do-Hee looks at the empty chair.

Then quietly shakes her head.

**YOUNG DO DO-HEE:**
“No.”

She stands.

She leaves the café.

---

### 🇰🇷 PRESENT DAY — SEOUL

Do Do-Hee stands outside the same café.

She looks through the window.

The table is still there.

The empty seat is still there.

She almost walks away.

But this time—

she opens the door.

---

### ☕ INSIDE THE CAFÉ

Do Do-Hee sits at the old table.

The waiter comes over.

**WAITER:**
“What can I get you?”

Do Do-Hee looks at the menu.

Then smiles faintly.

**DO DO-HEE:**
“The usual.”

The waiter nods and walks away.

Do Do-Hee looks at the empty seat across from her.

For a moment, she remembers the laughter.

She smiles.

A drink is placed in front of her.

She takes a sip.

Her eyes become slightly watery.

But she doesn't leave.

She stays.

---

Outside, Seoul continues moving.

Cars pass.

People walk.

Lights shine.

Do Do-Hee sits quietly by the window.

She hasn't forgotten.

She doesn't want to.

She simply learned how to remember without running away.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 7**



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 8 — THE PHOTOGRAPH

### 🇰🇷 DAEGU — EVENING

The sun is setting over Daegu.

Kim Wong-jun walks down a quiet street.

He notices someone sitting alone outside a small convenience store.

It's Min-jae.

Kim stops.

Min-jae is looking at an old photograph.

Kim walks over.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Min-jae.”

Min-jae looks up.

He smiles slightly.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Hey.”

Kim sits beside him.

For a moment, neither speaks.

Then Kim looks at the photograph.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Who is that?”

Min-jae looks down at it.

This time, he doesn't hide it.

**MIN-JAE:**
“My older sister.”

Kim looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You had a sister?”

Min-jae nods.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Yeah.”

He looks at the photograph.

It shows a younger Min-jae standing beside his older sister.

Both are smiling.

---

### 🕰️ FLASHBACK — YEARS EARLIER

Min-jae is a young boy.

His older sister is sitting beside him while helping him with homework.

**SISTER:**
“You've done this question wrong.”

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“No, I haven't.”

She points at his notebook.

**SISTER:**
“You definitely have.”

Min-jae looks at the answer.

He realises she's right.

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“Don't tell Mom.”

She laughs.

**SISTER:**
“Fine.”

She ruffles his hair.

**SISTER:**
“But next time, check your work.”

---

### 🌤️ ANOTHER DAY

Min-jae walks home with his sister.

She carries his schoolbag.

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“I can carry it.”

**SISTER:**
“You complained about it yesterday.”

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“That was yesterday.”

She smiles.

**SISTER:**
“Right. And today you're suddenly strong?”

Min-jae grabs the bag.

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“Exactly.”

He struggles to carry it.

His sister laughs.

**SISTER:**
“Sure.”

Min-jae laughs too.

---

### 📸 ONE EVENING

His sister holds up a camera.

**SISTER:**
“Come here.”

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“Why?”

**SISTER:**
“Picture.”

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“No.”

She points the camera at him.

**SISTER:**
“Too late.”

**CLICK.**

Min-jae makes an annoyed face.

His sister laughs.

**SISTER:**
“Perfect.”

That photograph becomes the one Min-jae still carries.

---

### 🌧️ YEARS LATER

Min-jae is older now.

He sits at home.

His sister's phone is on the table.

It hasn't moved.

A family member enters the room.

Their expression is enough.

Min-jae looks up.

**MIN-JAE:**
“What happened?”

Silence.

Min-jae slowly understands.

His sister is gone.

He looks at the photograph.

His hands begin shaking.

He says nothing.

---

### 🕰️ SOME TIME LATER

Min-jae sits alone in his room.

The photograph is in his hands.

He looks at it.

Tears run down his face.

He whispers—

**YOUNG MIN-JAE:**
“You said you'd always be there.”

He closes his eyes.

For years after that day, Min-jae changes.

He talks less.

He keeps his feelings to himself.

He stops letting people get close.

Whenever someone asks if he's okay—

**MIN-JAE:**
“I'm fine.”

Every time.

---

### 🇰🇷 PRESENT DAY — DAEGU

Min-jae finishes telling the story.

Kim remains silent.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You never told me.”

Min-jae looks down.

**MIN-JAE:**
“I didn't want anyone to know.”

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Why?”

Min-jae thinks for a moment.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Because once people know what hurts you...”

He looks at Kim.

**MIN-JAE:**
“...they can see the part of you that's weak.”

Kim shakes his head.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“That's not weakness.”

Min-jae looks away.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“That's being human.”

Min-jae doesn't respond.

But his expression softens.

---

### 🕰️ KIM'S MEMORY

Kim looks at the photograph.

Then he reaches into his pocket.

He takes out a small folded piece of paper.

Min-jae notices.

**MIN-JAE:**
“What is that?”

Kim unfolds it.

It's an old handwritten note.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“My father wrote it.”

Min-jae looks at him.

---

### 🕰️ YEARS EARLIER

A younger Kim sits beside his father.

His father is teaching him how to ride a bicycle.

Kim struggles to balance.

**YOUNG KIM:**
“I can't!”

His father holds the bicycle.

**FATHER:**
“You can.”

Kim tries again.

He moves forward.

Then—

His father lets go.

Kim doesn't notice.

He keeps riding.

**YOUNG KIM:**
“Dad!”

His father laughs.

**FATHER:**
“See?”

Kim suddenly realises he's riding by himself.

He smiles.

---

### 🌙 YEARS LATER

Kim sits with his father at home.

His father gives him the folded note.

**FATHER:**
“Keep this.”

**YOUNG KIM:**
“What is it?”

**FATHER:**
“You'll understand someday.”

Kim laughs.

**YOUNG KIM:**
“That's not an answer.”

His father smiles.

**FATHER:**
“Maybe it is.”

---

### 🕰️ SOME TIME LATER

Kim stands outside his home.

His father is gone.

Kim holds the same folded note.

He finally opens it.

Inside is a short message.

> **Keep moving forward.**

Kim reads it again.

His eyes fill with tears.

---

### 🇰🇷 PRESENT DAY — DAEGU

Kim folds the note again.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“I used to hate this.”

Min-jae looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Every time I read it, I wished he was still here.”

A pause.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Then I realised something.”

Kim looks at Min-jae.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“He didn't write it so I would keep looking back.”

Min-jae listens.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“He wrote it so I'd keep going.”

Min-jae looks down at his sister's photograph.

For the first time, he smiles.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Maybe I should've understood that earlier.”

Kim smiles.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You understand it now.”

Min-jae carefully puts the photograph back into his wallet.

He stands.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Thanks, Kim.”

Kim stands beside him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“For what?”

Min-jae looks at him.

**MIN-JAE:**
“For asking.”

They begin walking together.

Min-jae is still carrying the memory of his sister.

But he is no longer hiding it.

And for the first time—

he isn't trying to forget the past.

He's learning how to live with it.

### CUT TO BLACK.

**END OF PART 8**




# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 9 — THE PEOPLE BESIDE US

**NEW YORK — MORNING**

The city continues moving.

Cars pass through the streets.

People walk to work.

Cafés are filled.

For most people, it is still an ordinary day.

Michael sits at a café table with Henry and Alex.

Henry looks at his coffee.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“You know what’s weird?”

Michael looks at him.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“What?”

Henry looks at both of them.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“We've known each other for so long... but we never really talk about this stuff.”

Alex smiles slightly.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Because we're usually too busy arguing.”

Henry laughs.

Michael smiles.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“That's true.”

Alex reaches for his cup.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Still... I'm glad you're both here.”

Michael and Henry look at him.

For a moment, nobody says anything.

Henry smiles.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Yeah.”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Me too.”

They continue drinking their coffee.

Just three friends.

Nothing special.

But somehow—

It feels important.

---

### **CALIFORNIA — AFTERNOON**

Harry and David are outside.

A basketball bounces across the pavement.

**THUMP.**

David catches it.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“You still can't beat me.”

Harry raises an eyebrow.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“You've been saying that for years.”

David throws the ball.

Harry catches it.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Your turn.”

David laughs.

They start playing.

For a few minutes, there are no news reports.

No emergency alerts.

No fear.

Just two friends playing basketball.

David misses a shot.

Harry laughs.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Don't say anything.”

**HARRY WILSON:**
“I wasn't going to.”

Harry starts laughing harder.

David shakes his head.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“You're impossible.”

They keep playing.

---

### **HAWAII — EVENING**

Chris and Emma sit at home together.

A plate of food sits between them.

Emma takes a bite.

Chris looks at her.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“You remember Grandpa used to complain when we ate too fast?”

Emma smiles.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“He complained about everything.”

Chris laughs.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“True.”

Emma looks down for a moment.

Then smiles again.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“I miss him.”

Chris nods.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Me too.”

They continue eating.

No sadness takes over.

They simply remember.

Together.

---

### **MUMBAI — EVENING**

Kartik sits at home.

His phone is in his hand.

He looks at his family.

For a moment, he doesn't say anything.

Then—

**KARTIK SHARMA:**
“Can we eat together?”

His family looks at him.

Someone smiles.

They sit down together.

Kartik puts his phone aside.

No news.

No notifications.

Just dinner.

---

### **SEOUL — NIGHT**

Do Do-Hee walks through the city.

She passes the café from earlier.

She slows down.

She looks through the window.

For a moment, she remembers the empty seat.

Then she smiles faintly.

She takes out her phone.

She opens her messages.

Instead of looking at the old conversation—

She starts a new one.

She types:

> **“How are you?”**

She presses send.

Then continues walking.

---

### **DAEGU — NIGHT**

Kim and Min-jae sit outside the convenience store.

Two drinks sit between them.

Kim hands one to Min-jae.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Here.”

Min-jae takes it.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Thanks.”

They sit quietly.

For once, Min-jae isn't holding the old photograph.

It is safely inside his pocket.

Kim looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You feeling better?”

Min-jae thinks for a moment.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Not better.”

Kim looks at him.

Min-jae gives a small smile.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Just... lighter.”

Kim nods.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“That's good enough.”

Min-jae looks at him.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Yeah.”

They sit together.

No big conversation.

No need for one.

---

### **A WORLD STILL LIVING**

New York.

Michael, Henry and Alex leave the café together.

California.

Harry and David continue playing basketball.

Hawaii.

Chris and Emma clean up after dinner.

Mumbai.

Kartik sits with his family.

Seoul.

Do Do-Hee walks beneath the city lights.

Daegu.

Kim and Min-jae sit together outside the store.

For one evening—

Everything feels almost normal.

---

### **NEW YORK — NIGHT**

Michael walks beside Henry and Alex.

Henry suddenly stops.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Hey.”

Michael turns.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“What?”

Henry looks at both of them.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Let's not disappear on each other.”

Alex smiles.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Deal.”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Deal.”

They continue walking.

The city lights shine around them.

The world is changing.

But they still have each other.

### CUT TO BLACK.

# END OF PART 9




# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY

### PART 10 — TOMORROW

**NEW YORK — NIGHT**

The city is quieter now.

Michael stands near his window.

The lights of New York stretch across the distance.

His phone sits on the table.

A news report plays quietly.

> **NEWS REPORT:**
> “Authorities continue to monitor the situation in Germany. Several countries have increased security measures following reports of unexplained violent incidents.”

Michael looks at the television.

He doesn't say anything.

He looks at an old photograph sitting beside his phone.

His father.

Michael picks it up.

For a moment, he smiles.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I remember.”

He places the photograph back down.

Then he turns off the television.

---

### **NEW YORK — HENRY**

Henry sits on his bed.

His phone is beside him.

He opens his old contacts.

He finds:

> **LUCAS**

Henry stares at the name.

He doesn't call.

Instead, he locks his phone.

He looks toward the window.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“I should've answered sooner.”

He takes a breath.

Then quietly adds—

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“But I can't change yesterday.”

He lies back.

---

### **NEW YORK — ALEX**

Alex sits at his desk.

He opens an old message.

A conversation with his sister.

He scrolls through it.

He reaches the last message.

> **“Can we talk when you get home?”**

Alex pauses.

His eyes become slightly watery.

He closes the conversation.

Then smiles faintly.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“I wish I could've had that conversation.”

He puts his phone away.

---

### **CALIFORNIA — HARRY AND DAVID**

Harry and David sit outside.

The basketball rests beside them.

Neither speaks for a while.

David looks toward the sky.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Do you ever think about the past?”

Harry smiles.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“All the time.”

David nods.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Me too.”

Harry looks at him.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“But we're still here.”

David looks back.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Yeah.”

They sit together.

---

### **HAWAII — CHRIS AND EMMA**

Chris closes the curtains.

Emma stands near the window.

Outside, the ocean is barely visible in the darkness.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Do you think tomorrow will be normal?”

Chris looks at her.

He thinks for a moment.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“I don't know.”

Emma looks down.

Chris smiles.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“But whatever happens... we'll handle it.”

Emma smiles.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Together?”

Chris nods.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Together.”

They turn off the lights.

---

### **MUMBAI — KARTIK**

Kartik lies in bed.

His old message is still open on his phone.

> **“I'll see you soon.”**

He reads it one last time.

Then closes it.

He places the phone beside him.

**KARTIK SHARMA:**
“Goodnight.”

He turns off the light.

---

### **SEOUL — DO DO-HEE**

Do Do-Hee sits beside her window.

Her phone vibrates.

She looks at the message she sent earlier.

A reply has arrived.

She smiles.

She doesn't open it immediately.

Instead, she looks outside at Seoul.

The city is glowing.

For the first time in a long time—

She doesn't feel the need to look back.

She looks forward.

---

### **DAEGU — KIM AND MIN-JAE**

Kim walks home.

Min-jae walks beside him.

The old photograph is still in Min-jae's pocket.

Kim looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You still have it?”

Min-jae touches the photograph.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Yeah.”

Kim nods.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You don't have to hide it anymore.”

Min-jae smiles.

**MIN-JAE:**
“I know.”

They stop at a crossroads.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“See you tomorrow.”

Min-jae looks at him.

A small smile appears.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Yeah.”

They walk in different directions.

---

### **THE WORLD — NIGHT**

New York.

Michael sleeps.

Henry closes his eyes.

Alex turns off his lamp.

---

California.

Harry and David head home.

---

Hawaii.

Chris and Emma sleep peacefully.

---

Mumbai.

Kartik lies quietly in his room.

---

Seoul.

Do Do-Hee watches the city lights.

---

Daegu.

Min-jae places the photograph beside his bed.

Kim looks out his window.

---

### **GERMANY**

The mood changes.

A police vehicle races through a dark street.

Sirens echo.

**WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

A hospital entrance remains crowded.

Doctors move quickly through the corridors.

A news screen shows:

> **BREAKING NEWS — GERMANY EMERGENCY CONTINUES**

The screen changes.

> **CASES OUTSIDE GERMANY CONFIRMED**

Then another update appears.

> **AUTHORITIES ISSUE NEW TRAVEL RESTRICTIONS**

---

### **SARAH'S LAB**

Sarah sits alone.

The room is dark.

Several screens glow in front of her.

> **INFECTED: 45**

The number changes.

> **46**

Sarah watches silently.

Another screen displays:

> **VARIATION DETECTED**

Sarah closes her eyes.

She remembers the first test.

The first infected.

The first change.

Everything she thought she understood—

Is beginning to disappear.

She whispers—

**SARAH SMITH:**
“Tomorrow...”

She looks at the screens.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“Everything could be different.”

---

### **THE NEXT MORNING**

The sun rises.

New York.

California.

Hawaii.

Mumbai.

Seoul.

Daegu.

Germany.

Different places.

Different people.

One world.

One night behind them.

One unknown day ahead.

The characters wake up believing they still have time.

They don't know what tomorrow will bring.

They don't know how quickly everything can change.

But for tonight—

They remember.

They care.

And they are still together.

A final message appears on screen:

> **TOMORROW IS NEVER GUARANTEED.**

### FADE TO BLACK.

# END OF EPISODE 6

## 🌍 END OF EPISODE 6 — THE MEMORIES WE CARRY



# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 1 — THE FIRST WARNING

**NEW YORK — MORNING**

The city wakes up normally.

Cars move through the streets.

People rush to work.

Cafés open.

Trains arrive.

But something feels different.

More people are checking their phones.

More television screens are showing the same headline.

> **BREAKING NEWS — OUTBREAK SPREADS OUTSIDE GERMANY**

Henry Rivers walks down the street, looking at his phone.

He stops.

He reads the headline again.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“It's spreading.”

Alex Brooks walks up beside him.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Yeah.”

Henry looks at him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Michael said something was wrong in Germany.”

Alex puts his phone away.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“He was right.”

They continue walking.

---

### **THE CAFÉ**

Henry and Alex enter the café.

Michael Carter is already there.

He looks up.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“You two saw it?”

Henry sits down.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Yeah.”

Alex sits opposite him.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“They confirmed another case outside Germany.”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I know.”

Henry looks toward the television.

A news reporter is speaking.

> **NEWS REPORTER:**
> “Authorities continue to investigate the unexplained illness and violent behaviour. Several countries have now increased airport security and travel restrictions.”

Henry watches carefully.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“This isn't staying in Germany.”

Michael doesn't answer.

Alex looks at him.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“What do you think we should do?”

Michael thinks.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Stay alert.”

Henry nods.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“And if something happens?”

Michael looks at both of them.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“We stay together.”

Henry smiles slightly.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Deal.”

Alex nods.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Deal.”

---

### **NEW YORK — STREET**

Later.

Henry and Alex walk together.

Michael has gone home.

Henry keeps looking at people around them.

Alex notices.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“You're checking everyone.”

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Can you blame me?”

Alex looks around.

Everyone seems normal.

A man drinks coffee.

A woman walks her dog.

A cyclist passes them.

Nothing unusual.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Everyone looks fine.”

Henry looks ahead.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“That's what worries me.”

Alex becomes quiet.

Then—

A phone notification sounds.

Both look down.

> **NEW UPDATE — FOREIGN CASES UNDER INVESTIGATION**

Henry's expression changes.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Alex.”

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“I saw it.”

They stop walking.

For the first time—

They don't know what to say.

---

### **DAEGU — SOUTH KOREA**

Min-jae walks through the city.

Kim Wong-jun walks beside him.

Min-jae looks at the people around them.

Most are still going about their normal lives.

Kim notices his expression.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“What are you thinking?”

Min-jae looks at his phone.

The same international news is displayed.

**MIN-JAE:**
“About what we talked about yesterday.”

Kim looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Your sister?”

Min-jae shakes his head.

**MIN-JAE:**
“No.”

He looks toward the busy street.

**MIN-JAE:**
“About tomorrow.”

Kim becomes serious.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You think it'll reach Korea?”

Min-jae doesn't answer immediately.

**MIN-JAE:**
“I don't know.”

He looks at the news again.

**MIN-JAE:**
“But I don't think we should pretend nothing is happening.”

Kim nods.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Then what do we do?”

Min-jae looks around.

For once, he doesn't wait for someone else to decide.

**MIN-JAE:**
“We prepare.”

Kim looks surprised.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Prepare for what?”

Min-jae looks directly at him.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Whatever comes.”

Kim nods.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Okay.”

---

### **NEW YORK — AFTERNOON**

Henry and Alex return to the café.

Michael is gone.

Henry sits down.

Alex puts his phone on the table.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Do you think we're overreacting?”

Henry thinks.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Maybe.”

He looks at the television.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“But I'd rather be wrong.”

Alex nods.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Same.”

Henry takes out his phone.

He opens his family contacts.

Alex notices.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“You calling them?”

Henry looks at the screen.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Yeah.”

He presses call.

The phone rings.

---

### **DAEGU — EVENING**

Min-jae and Kim walk into a small shop.

Min-jae looks at the shelves.

Water.

Food.

Batteries.

Basic supplies.

Kim picks up a bottle of water.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“How much should we get?”

Min-jae looks around.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Enough for a few days.”

Kim raises an eyebrow.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“You're taking this seriously.”

Min-jae gives a small smile.

**MIN-JAE:**
“I've learned not to ignore warnings.”

Kim looks at him.

He understands.

They continue gathering supplies.

---

### **NEW YORK — NIGHT**

Henry sits near his window.

His family call has ended.

He looks relieved.

Alex sits nearby.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Everything okay?”

Henry nods.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Yeah.”

Alex smiles.

Henry looks toward the city.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Let's hope it stays that way.”

---

### **DAEGU — NIGHT**

Min-jae and Kim return home with their supplies.

Min-jae places everything down.

He looks at the photograph of his sister.

Then at the supplies.

Then at Kim.

**MIN-JAE:**
“We're ready.”

Kim nods.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“As ready as we can be.”

Min-jae looks outside.

The city lights are still shining.

People are still walking.

Cars are still moving.

Everything still looks normal.

But now—

Min-jae knows better than to trust appearances.

---

### **FINAL MONTAGE**

New York.

Henry watches the news.

Alex sits beside him.

---

Daegu.

Min-jae checks the locked door.

Kim checks the supplies.

---

Germany.

Police vehicles race through another street.

---

Another country.

An ambulance drives through the night.

---

A news screen displays:

> **GLOBAL OUTBREAK CONTINUES TO SPREAD**

Henry looks at the screen.

Min-jae looks at his phone.

Alex looks toward the window.

Kim turns off the lights.

The world is still standing.

But the warning has been heard.

### CUT TO BLACK.

# END OF PART 1 — EPISODE 7




# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 2 — THE LAST NORMAL DAY

**NEW YORK — MORNING**

The college campus is busy.

Students walk between buildings.

Some carry books.

Others rush toward their classes.

The campus feels completely normal.

For now.

---

### **MICHAEL CARTER — COLLEGE**

Michael sits inside a classroom.

A professor writes on the board.

Students take notes.

Michael glances at his phone.

Another notification appears.

> **BREAKING NEWS — OUTBREAK CASES CONTINUE TO RISE**

Michael quickly locks his phone.

The professor notices.

**PROFESSOR:**
“Everything okay?”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Yeah.”

The professor continues teaching.

Michael looks around the classroom.

Nobody seems too concerned.

---

### **HENRY RIVERS — COLLEGE**

Henry walks through the hallway.

He meets Alex outside another classroom.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“You saw the news?”

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Yeah.”

Henry looks around.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Nobody here seems worried.”

Alex shrugs.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Maybe they think it's far away.”

Henry looks at the students around them.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Germany probably thought the same thing.”

Alex becomes quiet.

The bell rings.

**BELL:**
**RIIING!**

Students begin entering their classrooms.

Henry and Alex exchange a look.

Then they separate.

---

### **CALIFORNIA — COLLEGE**

Harry Wilson and David Anderson sit inside a lecture hall.

The professor is explaining something.

David whispers to Harry.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“Did you finish the assignment?”

Harry looks at him.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“What assignment?”

David stares at him.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“You're joking.”

Harry smiles.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Maybe.”

David shakes his head.

Suddenly—

A student's phone rings.

Then another.

Then another.

The room becomes filled with notification sounds.

The professor stops talking.

**PROFESSOR:**
“Phones away, please.”

Nobody listens.

The professor looks confused.

David checks his phone.

His smile disappears.

Harry looks at him.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“What?”

David turns the screen toward him.

> **BREAKING NEWS — INTERNATIONAL HEALTH ALERT**

Harry reads it.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Oh.”

The professor looks toward the students.

**PROFESSOR:**
“Everyone, please remain calm.”

The room becomes silent.

---

### **MUMBAI — COLLEGE**

Kartik Sharma sits in a classroom.

The professor continues the lesson.

Kartik looks outside the window.

Students in the corridor are talking.

One student rushes into the classroom.

**STUDENT:**
“Sir, they're saying the outbreak has reached another country.”

The professor looks at him.

**PROFESSOR:**
“Please sit down.”

The student sits.

Kartik checks his phone.

The news confirms it.

He slowly puts his phone away.

The professor continues teaching.

But now—

Nobody is listening.

---

### **DAEGU — COLLEGE**

Kim Wong-jun sits in class.

The professor is speaking.

Kim looks at his phone beneath the desk.

A notification appears.

> **GLOBAL OUTBREAK — NEW INTERNATIONAL CASES**

Kim looks toward Min-jae.

Min-jae is sitting several seats away.

Kim quietly shows him the screen.

Min-jae reads it.

His expression becomes serious.

He looks around the classroom.

Students are still taking notes.

The professor continues teaching.

Min-jae whispers—

**MIN-JAE:**
“They're pretending everything is normal.”

Kim looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Maybe they need it to feel normal.”

Min-jae looks back at the screen.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Maybe.”

---

### **HAWAII — HIGH SCHOOL**

Chris Bennett sits in class.

The teacher is explaining a lesson.

Emma Bennett is in another classroom across the school.

A few students whisper about the outbreak.

The teacher turns around.

**TEACHER:**
“Everyone, please focus.”

A student raises his hand.

**STUDENT:**
“Is it true that schools might close?”

The teacher pauses.

**TEACHER:**
“We haven't received any official information.”

The class becomes quiet.

Chris looks toward the window.

Outside, the school grounds are completely normal.

Students walk between buildings.

Teachers talk.

Someone laughs.

Chris takes out his phone.

A message from Emma appears.

> **EMMA:**
> “Have you heard anything?”

Chris replies:

> **CHRIS:**
> “Nothing official.”

A few seconds later—

> **EMMA:**
> “Okay.”

Chris puts his phone away.

---

### **SEOUL — HIGH SCHOOL**

Do Do-Hee sits in class.

The teacher continues writing on the board.

A student whispers to another.

**STUDENT:**
“My parents said the airports are getting stricter.”

Do Do-Hee hears it.

She looks at her phone.

Another notification.

> **TRAVEL RESTRICTIONS EXPANDED**

She locks the screen.

The teacher turns around.

**TEACHER:**
“Do Do-Hee?”

She looks up.

**DO DO-HEE:**
“Yes?”

**TEACHER:**
“Are you listening?”

Do Do-Hee smiles faintly.

**DO DO-HEE:**
“Yes.”

The teacher continues.

Do Do-Hee looks at the clock.

The seconds move normally.

**TICK.**

**TICK.**

**TICK.**

---

### **NEW YORK — COLLEGE CAFETERIA**

Michael meets Henry and Alex during lunch.

They sit together.

Trays of food sit on the table.

Henry looks around.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“This feels weird.”

Alex looks at him.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“What does?”

Henry gestures toward the room.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Everyone's just eating lunch.”

Michael looks around.

Hundreds of students are laughing and talking.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Maybe that's what people do when they're scared.”

Henry looks at him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What?”

Michael looks at the students.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Keep acting normal.”

The three become quiet.

---

### **CALIFORNIA — COLLEGE CAMPUS**

Harry and David walk outside.

Students are gathered around their phones.

One student says—

**STUDENT:**
“Do you think they'll close the college?”

Another replies—

**STUDENT 2:**
“No way.”

David looks at Harry.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“People don't believe it yet.”

Harry nods.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Neither did we.”

---

### **MUMBAI — COLLEGE CAMPUS**

Kartik leaves his classroom.

He walks through the corridor.

A group of students stand around a phone.

Kartik stops nearby.

The screen shows Germany.

Police.

Ambulances.

Crowds.

Kartik watches silently.

Then he walks away.

---

### **DAEGU — COLLEGE CAMPUS**

Kim and Min-jae walk together.

Students pass them.

Everything is still normal.

Kim looks at Min-jae.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Still think we should prepare?”

Min-jae nods.

**MIN-JAE:**
“More than before.”

---

### **HAWAII — SCHOOL**

The final bell rings.

**BELL:**
**RIIING!**

Students rush out.

Chris walks through the hallway.

Emma comes from the opposite direction.

They meet.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Finally.”

Chris smiles.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“Yeah.”

They walk together toward the exit.

For a moment—

They look like any other siblings leaving school.

---

### **SEOUL — HIGH SCHOOL**

Do Do-Hee walks out of the school gates.

She looks at the city.

People continue moving.

Buses arrive.

Cars pass.

Everything is normal.

But her phone vibrates.

She checks it.

> **EMERGENCY ALERT — PLEASE MONITOR OFFICIAL UPDATES**

Do Do-Hee looks at the message.

Her expression changes.

---

### **THE LAST NORMAL DAY**

New York.

Michael, Henry and Alex leave college.

---

California.

Harry and David walk across campus.

---

Mumbai.

Kartik leaves the college building.

---

Daegu.

Kim and Min-jae walk home.

---

Hawaii.

Chris and Emma leave school together.

---

Seoul.

Do Do-Hee walks through the crowded street.

Seven college students.

Four school students.

Different countries.

Different lives.

But all of them are seeing the same thing.

The world outside is changing.

And their schools and colleges are still open.

For now.

A final emergency notification appears on millions of phones:

> **⚠️ GOVERNMENT NOTICE**
> **PLEASE REMAIN ALERT AND FOLLOW OFFICIAL INSTRUCTIONS.**

Students across different countries stop and look at their screens.

Nobody knows what tomorrow will bring.

### CUT TO BLACK.

# END OF PART 2 — EPISODE 7




# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 3 — THE FLIGHT

---

### ✈️ **EUROPE — IN THE AIR**

A passenger plane flies through the clouds.

Everything inside is calm.

Passengers talk quietly.

Some sleep.

Some watch movies.

A flight attendant walks through the aisle.

Then—

A man near the window begins sweating.

He looks uncomfortable.

He rubs his arm.

A small infection mark is visible beneath his sleeve.

He quickly pulls the sleeve down.

A passenger sitting beside him notices.

**PASSENGER:**
“Are you okay?”

The man doesn't respond.

His breathing becomes heavier.

**PASSENGER:**
“Sir?”

The man suddenly grabs his chest.

He collapses into his seat.

The passenger immediately calls for help.

**PASSENGER:**
“HELP!”

The flight attendant rushes over.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“What happened?”

**PASSENGER:**
“He just collapsed!”

The flight attendant checks him.

His heartbeat is weak.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“Is there a doctor on board?”

A man from another row stands.

**DOCTOR:**
“I am.”

He moves toward them.

Passengers begin watching.

The doctor checks the man's pulse.

**DOCTOR:**
“His heart rate is dropping.”

The flight attendant looks worried.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“What do we do?”

The doctor begins helping him.

Then—

The man's heartbeat stops.

The doctor freezes.

**DOCTOR:**
“He's not breathing.”

The cabin becomes silent.

Then—

**BEEP.**

A heartbeat returns.

**BEEP.**

The doctor's eyes widen.

The man's fingers twitch.

**DOCTOR:**
“Wait...”

The man's eyes suddenly open.

They are **red**.

The passenger steps backwards.

**PASSENGER:**
“What the—?”

The infected man suddenly lunges.

**SCREAMS**

He attacks the passenger beside him.

People jump out of their seats.

**PASSENGER 2:**
“GET AWAY FROM HIM!”

The flight attendant screams.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“Everyone move back!”

Passengers begin rushing toward the front and rear of the aircraft.

The infected knocks over a seat.

He charges toward another passenger.

The passenger pushes him away.

**PASSENGER:**
“GET OFF ME!”

The infected grabs him again.

Other passengers rush over.

Several people try to pull the infected away.

The cabin becomes chaos.

---

### ✈️ **THE FIGHT**

A passenger grabs the infected from behind.

Another pushes him against a row of seats.

**PASSENGER 3:**
“Hold him!”

The infected violently struggles.

**PASSENGER 4:**
“HE'S TOO STRONG!”

The flight attendants try to move passengers away.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“BACK! EVERYONE BACK!”

A suitcase falls from an overhead compartment.

**BANG!**

People scream.

The infected breaks free.

He rushes down the aisle.

Passengers scatter.

One man blocks his path.

The infected tackles him.

Two passengers pull the infected away.

Someone shouts—

**PASSENGER:**
“WE HAVE TO STOP HIM!”

The infected continues fighting.

The crew struggles to keep him contained.

---

### 🛬 **NEW YORK — APPROACHING**

The pilot speaks through the intercom.

**PILOT:**
“Ladies and gentlemen, we're beginning our descent into New York.”

The passengers remain terrified.

The infected is being held near the rear of the cabin.

Flight attendants keep everyone away from him.

A passenger looks toward the cockpit.

**PASSENGER:**
“Does the pilot know?”

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“He knows there's an emergency, but not what it is.”

She looks toward the infected.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“We're landing.”

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — AIRPORT**

The aircraft lands.

Airport emergency vehicles are already waiting nearby.

The plane taxis toward a gate.

Inside, the crew keeps the infected away from the other passengers as best they can.

The aircraft stops.

The engines begin powering down.

Airport security approaches the aircraft.

---

### 🚨 **EMERGENCY RESPONSE**

The aircraft door remains closed.

Security officers speak with the crew through the doorway.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“How many people are injured?”

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“We have several injured passengers.”

She points toward the rear.

**FLIGHT ATTENDANT:**
“And one passenger is extremely aggressive.”

The officer looks serious.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Keep everyone away from him.”

The jet bridge is connected to the aircraft.

More security officers arrive.

---

### 🚪 **THE EVACUATION**

The aircraft door opens.

Security officers enter first.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Everyone remain calm. We're going to get you off the aircraft.”

Passengers begin leaving in small groups.

Some are crying.

Others are helping injured passengers.

The infected remains near the rear of the aircraft.

Two security officers move toward him.

**SECURITY OFFICER 2:**
“Sir, stay where you are.”

The infected suddenly charges.

**OFFICER 2:**
“BACK!”

The officers struggle to hold him.

Passengers rush toward the exit.

The infected breaks away.

He runs toward the front of the aircraft.

People scream and move aside.

A security officer grabs him.

The infected throws him against a seat.

**OFFICER:**
“GET BACK!”

Another officer tries to block the aisle.

The infected pushes past him.

He reaches the aircraft door.

Security tries to stop him.

But passengers are still evacuating through the same doorway.

For a moment, everyone is trapped together.

Then—

The infected forces his way through the doorway and into the jet bridge.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“STOP HIM!”

Security officers chase after him.

Passengers scream and run in the opposite direction.

---

### 🏃 **THE JET BRIDGE**

The infected runs through the jet bridge.

A security officer follows.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Stop!”

The infected reaches the terminal entrance.

The automatic doors open.

He disappears inside.

The officer arrives seconds later.

He looks around.

Hundreds of people are moving through the terminal.

The infected is gone.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Where did he go?”

Another officer arrives.

**OFFICER 2:**
“I don't know.”

They look into the crowd.

Nothing.

---

### 🚨 **NEW YORK AIRPORT — PANIC**

Airport security begins closing nearby access points.

Passengers are moved away from the gate.

The airport announcement system activates.

> **AIRPORT ANNOUNCEMENT:**
> “Please remain calm and follow security instructions.”

But people are already talking.

Phones come out.

Videos begin recording.

Someone points toward the terminal.

**PASSENGER:**
“Was that the man from the plane?”

Another person starts running.

Then another.

Soon—

People throughout the terminal begin moving faster.

Security radios fill with voices.

**RADIO:**
“Possible hostile passenger inside Terminal.”

**RADIO:**
“Multiple officers searching.”

**RADIO:**
“Keep civilians away from the area.”

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — OUTSIDE**

Outside the airport, traffic continues normally.

Cars pass.

Taxis wait.

People leave the terminal carrying luggage.

Nobody outside understands what has just happened.

The first infected has entered New York.

And now—

He is somewhere inside the city.

---

### 🌎 **ELSEWHERE**

Germany.

Sirens continue.

---

California.

Harry and David remain unaware.

---

Hawaii.

Chris and Emma continue their evening.

---

Mumbai.

Kartik watches the latest news.

---

Seoul.

Do Do-Hee checks her phone.

---

Daegu.

Kim and Min-jae remain at home.

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — AIRPORT TERMINAL**

Security officers search through the terminal.

One officer looks at the crowd.

Nothing.

No infected.

No clear direction.

Then—

A distant scream echoes from another part of the terminal.

Everyone turns.

### CUT TO BLACK.

# END OF PART 3 ----- EPISODE 7




# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 4 — THE FIRST SPREAD

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — AIRPORT TERMINAL**

The distant scream echoes through the terminal.

Security officers immediately turn toward the sound.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Everyone stay where you are!”

People begin looking around.

Another scream.

Then—

**CRASH!**

A luggage cart falls over.

Passengers start running.

**PASSENGER:**
“What's happening?!”

Security officers rush toward the noise.

---

### 🧟 **THE INFECTED**

The infected man suddenly appears from behind a group of passengers.

His eyes are completely red.

His skin is pale.

The infection mark has spread across his arm.

A woman sees him.

She freezes.

**WOMAN:**
“Is that the man from the plane?”

The infected suddenly charges.

**WOMAN:**
“RUN!”

People scatter.

The infected grabs a nearby passenger.

The passenger struggles.

**PASSENGER:**
“GET OFF ME!”

The infected bites him.

**PASSENGER:**
“AH!”

Security officers rush forward.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“GET HIM OFF!”

Two officers pull the infected away.

The injured passenger falls to the floor.

Blood appears on his shirt.

---

### 🚨 **SECURITY TRIES TO STOP HIM**

The infected suddenly turns toward the crowd again.

A security officer raises his hand.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Stay back!”

The infected charges.

The officer tries to stop him.

The infected slams into him.

**BANG!**

The officer falls against a barrier.

Another officer grabs the infected from behind.

**OFFICER 2:**
“I'VE GOT HIM!”

The infected violently struggles.

A third officer rushes over.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Hold him!”

For a moment, they manage to restrain him.

Then—

The infected suddenly throws his body backwards.

The officers lose their grip.

**OFFICER 2:**
“HE'S BREAKING FREE!”

The infected grabs the nearest officer and pushes him aside.

The officer crashes into a row of seats.

**BANG!**

The infected runs.

---

### 🏃 **THE CHASE**

Two officers chase after him.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“STOP!”

The infected runs between passengers.

People scream and move out of the way.

One passenger accidentally drops a suitcase.

The infected jumps over it.

A security officer tries to cut him off.

The infected suddenly turns.

He charges directly at the officer.

The officer steps aside.

The infected continues toward the terminal exit.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Don't let him reach the doors!”

Another officer runs from the opposite direction.

For a moment, they almost surround him.

Then a crowd of frightened passengers suddenly rushes between them.

The officers lose sight of the infected.

**OFFICER 2:**
“Where is he?!”

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“I can't see him!”

---

### 🚪 **THE EXIT**

The infected reaches a side exit.

A security officer spots him.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“There!”

The officer runs toward him.

The infected pushes through the emergency exit.

The alarm immediately sounds.

**ALARM:**
**WEE-OO! WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

The officer reaches the door seconds later.

He looks outside.

The infected is already running across the airport service road.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Stop!”

The infected keeps running.

---

### 🚗 **AIRPORT SERVICE ROAD**

The infected crosses between vehicles.

A car suddenly brakes.

**CAR HORN:**
**HONK!**

The driver looks terrified.

The infected disappears behind a row of airport vehicles.

Police officers arrive at the road.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
“Where did he go?”

The security officer points ahead.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“He ran that way!”

Police officers spread out.

But the infected is already gone.

---

### 🚨 **BACK INSIDE THE TERMINAL**

Security officers begin closing nearby access points.

Passengers are moved away from the gate.

The airport announcement system activates.

> **AIRPORT ANNOUNCEMENT:**
> “Please remain calm and follow security instructions.”

But people are already talking.

Phones come out.

Videos begin recording.

Someone points toward the exit.

**PASSENGER:**
“He got outside!”

Another person starts running.

Then another.

Soon—

People throughout the terminal begin moving faster.

Security radios fill with voices.

**RADIO:**
“Possible hostile passenger outside Terminal.”

**RADIO:**
“Multiple officers searching.”

**RADIO:**
“Keep civilians away from the area.”

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — OUTSIDE**

Outside the airport, traffic continues normally.

Cars pass.

Taxis wait.

People leave the terminal carrying luggage.

Nobody outside understands what has just happened.

The first infected has escaped the airport.

And now—

He is somewhere in New York.

### CUT TO BLACK.

# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 5 — THE WARNING

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — AIRPORT SERVICE ROAD**

The infected runs between parked vehicles.

His breathing is heavy.

His red eyes scan the area.

Police sirens echo behind him.

He suddenly turns into a narrow street leading away from the airport.

---

### 🌃 **NEW YORK — SIDE STREET**

The infected runs through the darkness.

A man walks home while talking on his phone.

**MAN:**
“Yeah, I'm almost home.”

He hears footsteps.

He turns.

The infected is standing behind him.

**MAN:**
“Sir?”

The infected suddenly charges.

**MAN:**
“HEY!”

The man tries to run.

The infected catches him.

He bites his shoulder.

**MAN:**
“AH!”

The man falls.

The infected immediately runs away.

A woman across the street sees what happened.

She screams.

**WOMAN:**
“HELP!”

She runs into a nearby building.

---

### 🚲 **ANOTHER STREET**

The infected continues moving.

A cyclist rides past.

The infected suddenly turns.

He runs toward the cyclist.

**CYCLIST:**
“What the—?!”

The cyclist falls from his bicycle.

The infected grabs him.

The cyclist tries to push him away.

The infected bites his arm.

**CYCLIST:**
“GET OFF ME!”

A car horn suddenly blasts.

**HONK!**

The infected looks toward the vehicle.

He releases the cyclist and disappears down another street.

The driver immediately calls emergency services.

---

### 🚨 **911 CALL CENTRE**

Phones ring continuously.

**OPERATOR:**
“911. What's your emergency?”

**CALLER:**
“There's someone attacking people!”

Another call comes in.

**CALLER 2:**
“He bit someone!”

Another.

**CALLER 3:**
“There was an attack near the airport!”

The operators look at one another.

One operator pulls up the reports.

The calls are coming from different locations.

**OPERATOR:**
“We're getting multiple reports.”

A supervisor walks over.

**SUPERVISOR:**
“What's happening?”

The operator looks at the reports.

**OPERATOR:**
“We don't know yet.”

---

### 📺 **NEW YORK NEWS STATION**

A news reporter watches the incoming reports.

Several screens display footage from the airport.

The reporter looks at the video again.

Then she pauses.

She rewinds it.

The infected's red eyes.

The pale skin.

The strange mark on his arm.

The violent behaviour.

Her expression changes.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Wait.”

A producer looks over.

**PRODUCER:**
“What?”

She points toward the screen.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Pull up the Germany footage.”

The producer searches for the earlier reports.

The Germany footage appears beside the New York airport footage.

The reporter compares them.

Same red eyes.

Same pale skin.

Same strange marks.

Same violent behaviour.

She looks stunned.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“These are the same symptoms.”

The producer looks worried.

**PRODUCER:**
“You think they're connected?”

The reporter looks at the German footage again.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“The person in Germany looked exactly like this.”

She pauses.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“This isn't just another violent incident.”

---

### 📺 **BREAKING NEWS**

The reporter turns toward the camera.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“We are receiving new information regarding the incident at New York Airport.”

The screen behind her shows the airport footage.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Officials are investigating whether the New York attacks are connected to the unexplained violent incidents recently reported in Germany.”

She pauses.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“The similarities are difficult to ignore.”

The producer receives another message.

His face changes.

**PRODUCER:**
“We've got more attacks.”

The reporter looks at him.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Where?”

**PRODUCER:**
“Across New York.”

The reporter slowly looks back at the camera.

---

### 🧟 **NEW YORK — ANOTHER ATTACK**

The infected continues through the streets.

He sees a group of people standing outside a shop.

He suddenly charges.

**WOMAN:**
“RUN!”

Everyone scatters.

One man falls.

The infected grabs him.

Another man tries to pull him away.

The infected turns and bites him.

**MAN:**
“AH!”

The man manages to escape.

The infected disappears between the buildings.

---

### 🚑 **THE BITTEN**

The first bitten man is placed inside an ambulance.

A paramedic examines his shoulder.

**PARAMEDIC:**
“We need to get him to the hospital.”

The man looks frightened.

**MAN:**
“Something's wrong with me.”

The paramedic checks his temperature.

It is normal.

For now.

Nearby, the cyclist is placed into another ambulance.

He looks at the bite on his arm.

A tiny dark mark begins forming around it.

**CYCLIST:**
“What is that?”

The paramedic looks closer.

His expression changes.

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — CITY STREETS**

Police cars race through the streets.

Sirens echo between buildings.

People begin noticing the emergency vehicles.

Phones buzz.

News alerts appear.

**BREAKING NEWS — MULTIPLE ATTACKS REPORTED IN NEW YORK**

A woman closes her shop.

A man pulls his children inside.

A group of people stop walking and stare at the news on a giant screen.

The city is beginning to realise something is wrong.

---

### 🌍 **GERMANY — NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA**

Far away...

the situation is completely different.

There are no normal streets anymore.

Cologne.

Düsseldorf.

Dortmund.

Essen.

Bonn.

The cities are overwhelmed.

Millions have already been infected.

Hospitals have collapsed.

Police lines have disappeared.

The infected move through entire neighbourhoods.

North Rhine-Westphalia is no longer under normal government control.

A government emergency map displays:

**NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA**

**STATUS: OVERRUN**

---

### 📊 **GERMAN EMERGENCY CENTRE**

A giant digital map covers the wall.

Most of North Rhine-Westphalia is covered in warning markers.

An official looks at the latest estimates.

**OFFICIAL:**
“How many infected?”

A scientist looks at the data.

**SCIENTIST:**
“We don't have an exact number anymore.”

**OFFICIAL:**
“Give me an estimate.”

The scientist hesitates.

**SCIENTIST:**
“More than eighteen million in North Rhine-Westphalia alone.”

The room becomes silent.

**OFFICIAL:**
“And the rest of Germany?”

The scientist looks at the map.

**SCIENTIST:**
“Still increasing.”

---

### 🧬 **UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH**

Sarah sits in front of her computer.

Her dashboard is filled with enormous numbers.

**GLOBAL INFECTED — ESTIMATED: 20,000,000+**

Sarah stares at the screen.

Another line appears.

**GLOBAL STATUS: RAPIDLY INCREASING**

Then—

**TRANSMISSION: GLOBAL**

Sarah scrolls down.

A regional breakdown appears.

**NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA, GERMANY**

**STATUS: OVERRUN**

**ESTIMATED INFECTED: 18,000,000+**

At the bottom of the screen:

> **MOST INFECTED FROM: NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA 🇩🇪**

Sarah looks at it silently.

Germany is still the centre of the outbreak.

Then another section appears.

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — NEW EXPOSURES**

Sarah opens a separate window.

**NEW YORK — NEW EXPOSURES**

**4**

She watches the number.

Then—

**BITTEN INDIVIDUALS: 4**

Sarah's expression changes.

She understands what happened.

The infected passenger escaped the airport.

He has already bitten multiple people.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“It reached New York.”

She looks back at the global map.

Germany is almost completely covered.

New York has only a few warning markers.

For now.

---

### 📺 **NEW YORK — EMERGENCY BROADCAST**

The reporter receives another message.

She looks down.

Her expression becomes serious.

She turns toward the camera.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“We have just received an emergency statement from authorities.”

The studio screen changes.

A red warning symbol appears.

Phones across **New York City** begin buzzing.

Televisions switch to the emergency message.

Public screens change.

The reporter looks directly into the camera.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“New York City is now under **LOCKDOWN**.”

People watching the broadcast freeze.

The reporter continues.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“🚨 **EMERGENCY ALERT: All civilians are instructed to remain indoors. Do not leave your homes unless absolutely necessary.**”

The message appears across the screen.

> **🚨 EMERGENCY ALERT**
> **NEW YORK CITY IS UNDER LOCKDOWN.**
> **All civilians are instructed to remain indoors. Do not leave your homes unless absolutely necessary.**

The message repeats.

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — THE CITY REACTS**

People begin locking their doors.

Stores close their shutters.

Restaurants stop serving customers.

Office workers leave their buildings.

Cars turn around.

Families call one another.

A man looks at the emergency alert on his phone.

**MAN:**
“Lockdown...?”

A woman pulls her child inside.

**WOMAN:**
“Come on. We're going home.”

The streets slowly begin emptying.

---

### 🗽 **MICHAEL, HENRY & ALEX**

Michael Carter looks at his phone.

Henry Rivers reads the alert again.

Alex Brooks looks around the street.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“They actually called it a lockdown.”

Michael looks toward the road.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Then we need to get home.”

Alex nods.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Now.”

The three begin moving quickly.

---

### 🌎 **ELSEWHERE — NEWS REPORTS**

California.

Harry Wilson and David Anderson watch the New York lockdown announcement on television.

They exchange a serious look.

---

Hawaii.

Chris Bennett and Emma Bennett see the breaking news.

The words **NEW YORK — LOCKDOWN** appear across the screen.

---

Mumbai.

Kartik Sharma watches the international report.

He looks concerned.

---

Seoul.

Do Do-Hee watches the New York footage on her phone.

---

Daegu.

Kim Wong-jun and Min-jae watch the news together.

Min-jae slowly locks the door.

---

### 🗽 **NEW YORK — SOMEWHERE IN THE CITY**

The infected continues walking through the streets.

Sirens echo behind him.

Most people are now indoors.

But he keeps moving.

He turns down another street.

And disappears into the darkness.

---

### 🧬 **SARAH'S SCREEN**

Sarah watches the global map.

North Rhine-Westphalia remains completely overrun.

New York has begun lighting up.

A final message appears:

**OUTBREAK STATUS: GLOBAL**

Below it:

> **MOST INFECTED FROM: NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA 🇩🇪**

Sarah stares at the screen.

Then looks at the New York markers.

The outbreak has reached another continent.

And now—

**New York City is under lockdown.**

### CUT TO BLACK.
