# 🧟 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 6 — K7

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — EVENING

The lockdown has begun.

The streets that were once filled with people are almost empty.

Police barricades block major roads.

Shops pull down their shutters.

Restaurants close.

Cars turn around as police officers redirect traffic.

Helicopters move slowly across the skyline.

Sirens echo through the city.

**SIREN... SIREN... SIREN...**

A loudspeaker announcement plays from a police vehicle.

**POLICE OFFICER:**
“Residents are advised to remain indoors.”

The vehicle disappears down the street.

Silence returns.

---

### 🏙️ MANHATTAN

A subway train slowly enters a station.

Only a few passengers remain inside.

An announcement plays.

**AUTOMATED VOICE:**
“Due to emergency restrictions, this station will be closing shortly.”

Passengers immediately begin leaving.

One man looks at his phone.

A news notification appears.

**BREAKING NEWS — NEW YORK CITY UNDER LOCKDOWN**

He looks around.

Nobody speaks.

---

### 📞 NEW YORK — EMERGENCY CALL CENTRE

Phones continue ringing.

**RING!**

An operator answers.

**OPERATOR:**
“Emergency services. What's your location?”

A frightened voice answers.

**CALLER:**
“There's someone outside my building.”

**OPERATOR:**
“Can you describe the person?”

**CALLER:**
“I don't know... he's acting strange.”

Another phone rings.

Then another.

**RING!**

**RING!**

A supervisor looks across the room.

Operators are becoming overwhelmed.

**SUPERVISOR:**
“How many calls?”

An operator checks the screen.

**OPERATOR:**
“Too many to count.”

The supervisor looks at the growing list.

---

### 🏠 MICHAEL'S HOME

Michael, Henry and Alex enter the apartment.

Michael locks the door.

**CLICK.**

Henry checks the windows.

Alex closes the curtains.

For a moment, nobody speaks.

The city outside is unusually quiet.

Henry looks toward Michael.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“So... we're really locked down.”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Yeah.”

Alex looks at his phone.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“The news says they don't know how many people have been attacked.”

Michael looks at the television.

Another report begins.

---

### 📺 NEW YORK NEWS

The reporter sits behind a desk.

Behind her, a map of New York City is displayed.

Several locations are marked.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Authorities are continuing to investigate a growing number of violent incidents across New York City.”

A video from the airport plays.

People running.

Police vehicles.

The infected passenger disappearing into the crowd.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Officials have confirmed that the incidents began following an emergency at New York's international airport.”

Michael watches carefully.

Henry looks at him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“That's where it started.”

Michael doesn't answer.

---

### 🏥 NEW YORK — HOSPITAL

The two bitten passengers remain under observation.

Doctors monitor them carefully.

The first man lies quietly on a hospital bed.

His temperature is normal.

His breathing is steady.

A doctor checks his arm.

The infection mark is still small.

**DOCTOR:**
“Any pain?”

The man shakes his head.

**BITTEN MAN:**
“No.”

The doctor writes something on a clipboard.

Across the room, the second bitten passenger suddenly begins sweating.

A nurse notices.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor.”

The doctor turns.

**NURSE:**
“His temperature is rising.”

The doctor walks over.

The monitor begins beeping faster.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

---

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA

The situation is completely different.

There are no functioning crowds.

No normal traffic.

No peaceful streets.

Only abandoned vehicles.

Burning buildings.

Emergency sirens.

And infected people moving through the streets.

Cologne.

Düsseldorf.

Dortmund.

Essen.

Bonn.

The entire region has been overwhelmed.

A German emergency broadcast repeats across the remaining communication network.

**EMERGENCY BROADCAST:**
“Residents are advised to remain indoors...”

The message cuts out.

Static fills the screen.

---

### 🧬 UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH

Sarah sits in front of several computer monitors.

One screen displays the global outbreak.

Another shows Germany.

Another shows New York.

She watches the data carefully.

The numbers continue changing.

Her eyes move from one screen to another.

Then she opens a classified file.

The screen turns black.

A single line appears.

> **PROJECT: K7**

Sarah stares at it.

Below it:

> **STATUS: ACTIVE**

Another line appears.

> **VARIATION RATE: INCREASING**

Sarah doesn't speak.

She simply watches the screen.

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — HOSPITAL

The second bitten passenger suddenly grabs the side of the bed.

The nurse steps closer.

**NURSE:**
“Sir?”

The man looks at her.

His breathing becomes heavier.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor!”

The doctor rushes over.

The monitor suddenly changes.

**BEEP...**

The heartbeat slows.

**BEEP...**

Slower.

**BEEP...**

The doctor looks at the monitor.

**DOCTOR:**
“Get the emergency team.”

The nurse runs toward the door.

---

### 🏠 MICHAEL'S HOME

Michael switches off the television.

Henry looks at him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What are we going to do?”

Michael looks toward the window.

The city is almost completely silent.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“We stay here.”

Alex nods.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“And if they lift the lockdown?”

Michael turns toward him.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“We don't assume it's safe.”

Henry looks at Michael.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“You think this is going to get worse?”

Michael pauses.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Yes.”

---

### 🇺🇸 CALIFORNIA

Harry and David continue watching the news.

The New York lockdown dominates every channel.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“They actually locked down the city.”

Harry looks outside.

California remains normal.

People are still driving.

Businesses are still open.

**HARRY WILSON:**
“Not here.”

David looks back at the television.

**DAVID ANDERSON:**
“For now.”

---

### 🇺🇸 HAWAII

Chris and Emma sit together watching the same report.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“New York looks completely different.”

Chris watches the empty streets.

**CHRIS BENNETT:**
“They're trying to stop it.”

Emma looks at him.

**EMMA BENNETT:**
“Do you think they can?”

Chris doesn't answer.

---

### 🇮🇳 MUMBAI

Kartik watches an international news report.

The anchor discusses the outbreak in Germany and New York.

Kartik looks around.

Mumbai continues moving normally.

Traffic.

People.

Shops.

Everything is still alive.

But Kartik keeps watching.

---

### 🇰🇷 SEOUL

Do Do-Hee watches the same report.

A map appears on the television.

Germany is highlighted.

Then New York.

She stares at the screen.

**DO DO-HEE:**
“It's spreading.”

---

### 🇰🇷 DAEGU

Kim Wong-jun and Min-jae sit quietly.

The television continues playing.

Min-jae looks toward the locked front door.

**MIN-JAE:**
“Should we lock the windows too?”

Kim looks at him.

**KIM WONG-JUN:**
“Yeah.”

Min-jae gets up.

He checks them one by one.

---

### 🧬 UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH

Sarah is still staring at the screen.

**PROJECT: K7**

She opens another window.

A map appears.

Germany.

New York.

Other locations begin appearing.

One after another.

Sarah watches the markers.

Her expression becomes tense.

She closes the map.

The **K7** file remains open.

Sarah slowly reaches toward the screen.

She touches the name.

**K7**

She whispers:

**SARAH SMITH:**
“K7...”

She closes the file.

Sarah looks down.

Her expression changes.

She whispers:

**SARAH SMITH:**
“My—”

She stops.

Silence.

Sarah looks away from the screen.

**FADE TO BLACK.**

---

### 🏥 NEW YORK — HOSPITAL

The monitor suddenly goes flat.

**BEEEEEEEEEP...**

The doctor freezes.

The nurse looks at him.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor...”

The doctor immediately begins emergency procedures.

**DOCTOR:**
“Prepare the defibrillator!”

The medical team rushes into the room.

The first bitten passenger watches from the other bed.

He doesn't understand what is happening.

Then he looks down at his own arm.

The infection mark has grown.

---

### 🌑 FINAL MOMENTS

Outside...

New York remains silent.

Germany is collapsing.

Other countries are watching.

And somewhere in the darkness...

**K7** continues changing.

The screen shows:

> **PROJECT: K7**
>
> **STATUS: ACTIVE**
>
> **VARIATION RATE: INCREASING**

Sarah stares at the final line.

She says nothing.

**CUT TO BLACK.**

# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 7 — THE FIRST NIGHT

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — HOSPITAL

The emergency room is tense.

The bitten passenger lies motionless on the bed.

A heart monitor beside him continues beeping.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

The doctor watches the monitor.

A nurse checks his temperature.

**NURSE:**
“His temperature is still rising.”

The doctor looks at the infection mark on his arm.

It has spread farther.

**DOCTOR:**
“Keep monitoring him.”

Suddenly—

The heartbeat begins slowing.

**BEEP...**

The doctor looks at the monitor.

**BEEP...**

The gaps become longer.

**BEEP...**

Then—

**FLATLINE.**

The room goes silent.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor...”

The doctor immediately moves toward the bed.

**DOCTOR:**
“Start resuscitation.”

The medical team rushes into action.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

The doctor checks the patient's pulse.

Nothing.

**DOCTOR:**
“Come on...”

The defibrillator charges.

**DEFIBRILLATOR:**
“CLEAR!”

The doctor shocks him.

His body jolts.

Nothing.

Another shock.

**DEFIBRILLATOR:**
“CLEAR!”

The monitor remains flat.

The doctor looks at the screen.

Then—

**BEEP.**

Everyone freezes.

Another heartbeat.

**BEEP.**

The monitor begins moving again.

The doctor slowly looks at the patient.

The man's fingers twitch.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor... his heart's back.”

The doctor takes a step closer.

The man's eyes remain closed.

Then—

They open.

The doctor freezes.

The man's eyes are turning **red**.

---

### 🚨 THE ATTACK

The patient suddenly sits upright.

The nurse steps backwards.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor...”

The man looks around.

He doesn't speak.

He slowly turns toward the nurse.

**DOCTOR:**
“Stay back.”

The man suddenly lunges.

**NURSE:**
“AH!”

The doctor pulls her away.

The patient crashes into a medical cart.

Metal instruments fall onto the floor.

**CLANG!**

The nurse runs toward the door.

**NURSE:**
“SECURITY!”

The patient charges after her.

The doctor presses the emergency button.

**BEEP! BEEP! BEEP!**

Security officers rush toward the room.

One grabs the patient's shoulder.

The patient violently throws him into the wall.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“GET BACK!”

Another officer tries to stop him.

The patient knocks him aside.

The corridor erupts into panic.

Doctors run.

Patients scream.

A wheelchair is abandoned in the middle of the hallway.

The infected keeps moving.

---

### 🏃 THE ESCAPE

The infected reaches the emergency exit.

A security officer catches up.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“STOP!”

The infected turns.

He charges.

The officer falls backwards.

The infected pushes through the emergency doors.

**BANG!**

He disappears into the night.

The security officer immediately grabs his radio.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“He's outside! Repeat, the infected patient is outside!”

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — SIDE STREET

The infected runs through a quiet street.

Most people are already indoors because of the lockdown.

A man stands outside a closed shop, speaking on his phone.

**MAN:**
“Yeah, I'll call you when I get home.”

He turns.

The infected is standing behind him.

The man freezes.

**MAN:**
“Uh... are you okay?”

The infected suddenly charges.

**MAN:**
“HEY!”

He tries to run.

The infected catches him.

The man struggles.

Then—

**BITE.**

The infected bites his shoulder.

**MAN:**
“AAAH!”

The man falls to the ground.

The infected releases him.

A nearby window opens.

A woman sees what happened.

**WOMAN:**
“Oh my God!”

She quickly closes the window.

The infected runs away.

The bitten man remains on the pavement.

Alive.

---

### 🚑 THE FIRST NEW CASE

A few minutes later...

An ambulance arrives.

Paramedics rush toward the injured man.

**PARAMEDIC:**
“Sir! Can you hear me?”

The man groans.

The paramedic examines the bite.

**PARAMEDIC 2:**
“Deep bite wound.”

They place him on a stretcher.

**MAN:**
“What... attacked me?”

The paramedics don't answer.

They rush him into the ambulance.

The ambulance drives away.

---

### 🏥 NEW YORK — HOSPITAL

The bitten man is brought into another emergency room.

A doctor examines him.

**DOCTOR:**
“Any medical conditions?”

**MAN:**
“No.”

**DOCTOR:**
“Do you remember what attacked you?”

The man shakes his head.

**MAN:**
“I don't know.”

The doctor looks at the bite.

**DOCTOR:**
“Keep him under observation.”

The man lies back.

For now...

he looks completely normal.

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — ANOTHER STREET

The infected continues moving.

He turns onto another street.

A cyclist rides past.

The infected suddenly runs toward him.

**CYCLIST:**
“What the—?!”

The cyclist falls off his bicycle.

The infected grabs him.

**CYCLIST:**
“HELP!”

A car horn blasts.

**HONK!**

The infected turns toward the sound.

The cyclist escapes, leaving his bicycle behind.

The infected disappears into another alley.

A driver immediately calls emergency services.

---

### 📞 911 CALL CENTRE

Phones are ringing constantly.

**OPERATOR:**
“911. What is your emergency?”

A caller speaks rapidly.

**CALLER:**
“There was an attack outside my building!”

Another line rings.

**OPERATOR 2:**
“911. What's your location?”

Another call.

Another.

Another.

A supervisor looks at the growing list.

**SUPERVISOR:**
“How many incidents?”

The operator looks at the screen.

**OPERATOR:**
“I'm still counting.”

The supervisor's expression changes.

---

### 🏥 NEW YORK — FIRST BITTEN MAN

The bitten man lies on a hospital bed.

A nurse checks his temperature.

**NURSE:**
“Temperature is normal.”

The doctor nods.

**DOCTOR:**
“Good.”

The nurse leaves.

The man looks at his shoulder.

A tiny dark mark has appeared beside the bite.

He touches it.

**MAN:**
“What is that?”

The mark slowly spreads.

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — NIGHT

The original infected walks through another street.

A couple stands near their apartment entrance.

They notice him.

**WOMAN:**
“Let's go inside.”

They hurry toward the door.

The infected suddenly charges.

The man pushes the woman inside.

**MAN:**
“GO!”

The infected grabs him.

**BITE.**

The woman screams.

**WOMAN:**
“HELP!”

She slams the door shut.

The infected turns and runs.

The bitten man lies outside.

The woman locks every door.

---

### 🏥 SECOND BITTEN VICTIM

The second bitten victim is brought to another hospital.

Doctors examine him.

**DOCTOR:**
“Another bite wound?”

**PARAMEDIC:**
“Same type as the other case.”

**DOCTOR:**
“Where was he attacked?”

**PARAMEDIC:**
“Not far from the first incident.”

For the first time...

someone is beginning to notice the pattern.

### 🧬 SARAH'S LABORATORY

Sarah watches several screens.

The New York map now contains several warning markers.

**NEW YORK — TRANSMISSION EVENTS**

She watches the data.

**EXPOSURES: 4**

Then—

**EXPOSURES: 5**

Sarah stares.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“It's starting again.”

**SARAH SMITH:**

Another reading changes.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“But not immediately.”

**SARAH SMITH:**
“It takes time.”

### 🏥 NEW YORK — FIRST BITTEN MAN

**NURSE:**

**NURSE:**

**DOCTOR:**
“Keep him isolated.”

### 🏥 ANOTHER HOSPITAL

**NURSE:**
“Sir?”

“Doctor!”

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

**NURSE:**
“What's happening?”

### 🇩🇪 GERMANY — NORTH RHINE-WESTPHALIA

**SCIENTIST:**

sirens continue.

### 🇺🇸 MICHAEL'S APARTMENT — NEW YORK

Michael, Henry and Alex sit silently.

Another emergency report appears.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Authorities are investigating several new attacks across New York City tonight.”

Henry looks at Michael.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“How many?”

Michael looks at the screen.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“They don't know.”

Alex stands up.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“We're staying here.”

Michael nods.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Yeah.”

Suddenly—

A loud noise comes from somewhere outside.

**BANG!**

All three freeze.

Henry slowly looks toward the window.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What was that?”

Another sound.

**BANG!**

Michael walks toward the curtains.

Alex grabs his arm.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“Don't.”

Michael stops.

They listen.

Nothing.

Then—

A distant scream.

The three stare at each other.

---

### 🌑 FINAL MOMENTS

Across New York...

The first bitten man lies in a hospital bed.

His fever continues rising.

The second bitten victim lies unconscious.

The original infected is still somewhere in the city.

Police continue searching.

More emergency calls arrive.

But the city is not overrun yet.

Not even close.

It has only begun.

On Sarah's screen:

> **NEW YORK — ACTIVE INFECTION EVENTS: INCREASING**

Another line appears.

> **TRANSMISSION: CONFIRMED**

Sarah watches silently.

Then—

Back in New York...

The first bitten man's heart monitor suddenly slows.

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

His eyes remain closed.

The screen fades to black.

# END OF PART 7 — EPISODE 7




# 🌍 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 8 — THE CHAIN

---

### 🇺🇸 NEW YORK — HOSPITAL 1

The emergency room is quiet.

The first bitten man lies unconscious.

His heart monitor continues beeping.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

His temperature keeps rising.

A nurse checks the infection mark on his arm.

It has spread farther.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor... it's getting worse.”

The doctor looks at the monitor.

**DOCTOR:**
“Keep watching him.”

The nurse nods.

Then—

The heartbeat begins slowing.

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

The doctor looks at the screen.

**DOCTOR:**
“His heart rate is dropping.”

The man suddenly stops breathing.

The monitor goes flat.

**FLATLINE.**

**NURSE:**
“Doctor!”

The medical team rushes toward him.

---

### ❤️ STAGE 4

The doctor begins resuscitation.

The defibrillator charges.

**DEFIBRILLATOR:**
“CLEAR!”

The man's body jolts.

Nothing.

Another shock.

**DEFIBRILLATOR:**
“CLEAR!”

Silence.

Then—

**BEEP.**

The monitor moves.

**BEEP.**

Another heartbeat.

The man's fingers twitch.

The doctor steps closer.

His eyes suddenly open.

They are **red**.

The doctor immediately steps backwards.

**DOCTOR:**
“Everyone back!”

The infected man sits upright.

He looks around.

Then—

He lunges.

**NURSE:**
“RUN!”

The nurse escapes through the door.

Security officers rush in.

The infected knocks one officer aside.

Another grabs him.

The infected throws him into a wall.

The corridor erupts into chaos.

---

### 🏃 HOSPITAL 1 — ESCAPE

The infected runs down the corridor.

A security officer tries to block the exit.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“STOP!”

The infected charges.

The officer falls.

The infected bursts through the emergency doors.

He is outside.

Again.

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — STREET

The infected runs through the empty street.

A man is walking home.

He hears footsteps.

He turns.

The infected charges.

**MAN:**
“HEY!”

The man tries to escape.

The infected catches him.

**BITE.**

The man screams.

The infected lets go.

The man falls against a parked car.

The infected disappears into the darkness.

---

### 🩸 THE NEW VICTIM

The man looks at his shoulder.

Blood runs from the bite.

He touches it.

**MAN:**
“What the hell...?”

He reaches for his phone.

**MAN:**
“911...”

His hand begins shaking.

---

### 🏥 HOSPITAL 2

Meanwhile...

The second bitten victim is still unconscious.

A doctor watches him.

His infection mark has spread across his arm.

**DOCTOR:**
“His condition is changing.”

A nurse checks his pulse.

**NURSE:**
“Heartbeat is slowing.”

The doctor looks concerned.

**DOCTOR:**
“Prepare the resuscitation equipment.”

The nurse immediately moves.

The monitor slows.

**BEEP...**

**BEEP...**

Then—

**FLATLINE.**

The nurse freezes.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor!”

---

### ❤️ ANOTHER TRANSFORMATION

The medical team rushes toward the patient.

The doctor begins resuscitation.

**DEFIBRILLATOR:**
“CLEAR!”

Shock.

Nothing.

Another shock.

**CLEAR!**

Silence.

Then—

**BEEP.**

The patient's heart returns.

His fingers twitch.

His eyes open.

**RED.**

The nurse takes a step back.

**NURSE:**
“Oh no...”

The infected suddenly grabs the doctor.

**DOCTOR:**
“GET HIM OFF ME!”

Security rushes into the room.

The infected breaks free.

He charges through the doorway.

---

### 🚨 HOSPITAL 2 — SECOND ESCAPE

The infected runs through the emergency corridor.

People scream.

A security officer tries to stop him.

The infected knocks him down.

He reaches the exit.

The doors open.

He disappears into the night.

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — STREET 2

The second infected runs through another neighbourhood.

A woman is locking the door of a shop.

She hears something.

She turns.

The infected charges.

**WOMAN:**
“WAIT!”

She tries to close the door.

Too late.

The infected grabs her.

**BITE.**

She screams.

The infected runs away.

The woman falls to the ground.

---

### 🩸 THE CHAIN GROWS

The first newly bitten man is taken into an ambulance.

The woman bitten by the second infected is taken into another.

Neither has red eyes.

Neither is transformed.

Both are still conscious.

For now.

---

### 🏥 TWO DIFFERENT HOSPITALS

The first new victim lies on a bed.

His breathing is normal.

A doctor examines the wound.

**DOCTOR:**
“Keep him isolated.”

Across the city...

The woman lies in another hospital.

Her temperature is normal.

A nurse checks her wound.

A small dark mark appears beside the bite.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor...”

The doctor looks at it.

The mark is slowly spreading.

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — MULTIPLE LOCATIONS

The first infected moves through another street.

He sees a man standing outside his apartment.

He charges.

**BITE.**

The man falls.

---

The second infected turns down another road.

He finds two people trying to get into their car.

He attacks.

One manages to escape.

The other is bitten.

---

The newly transformed infected continue moving.

But the city is still mostly quiet.

Most people are inside.

The attacks are scattered.

Small.

Separate.

Yet each attack creates another possible infection.

---

### 📞 911 CALL CENTRE

The emergency lines continue ringing.

**OPERATOR:**
“911. What's your emergency?”

**CALLER:**
“Someone attacked my friend!”

Another call.

**CALLER 2:**
“There was a man biting people!”

Another.

**CALLER 3:**
“My neighbour is injured!”

The operator looks overwhelmed.

**OPERATOR:**
“Supervisor?”

The supervisor walks over.

**SUPERVISOR:**
“How many?”

The operator looks at the screen.

**OPERATOR:**
“Too many to track individually.”

The supervisor stares at the growing list.

---

### 🧬 SARAH'S LABORATORY

Sarah watches the New York data.

Several markers appear.

Then more.

She zooms in.

> **ACTIVE INFECTED: 9**

Another update.

> **ACTIVE INFECTED: 12**

Sarah watches silently.

Then another.

> **ACTIVE INFECTED: 15**

She opens the transmission records.

Bite.

Transformation.

Bite.

Transformation.

She slowly realises what is happening.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“It's multiplying through them.”

She watches another marker appear.

**SARAH SMITH:**
“Every infected creates another.”

Sarah looks at the map.

The markers are still separated.

But they're spreading.

---

### 🇺🇸 MICHAEL'S APARTMENT — NIGHT

Michael, Henry and Alex sit in silence.

The television reports another attack.

**NEWS REPORTER:**
“Authorities are investigating a growing number of violent attacks across New York City.”

Henry looks toward the window.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Another one.”

A siren passes outside.

**WEE-OO! WEE-OO!**

Alex looks at Michael.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“How many are there now?”

Michael doesn't answer.

Another siren.

Then another.

**WEE-OO!**

**WEE-OO!**

Michael slowly walks toward the window.

He doesn't open the curtains.

He simply listens.

---

### ❤️ MICHAEL'S REACTION

Michael looks at the closed curtains.

For a moment, he remembers the hospital from years ago.

His father.

The waiting room.

The doctor walking toward him.

That same feeling of helplessness.

Michael closes his eyes.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Not again...”

Henry looks at him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What?”

Michael opens his eyes.

He looks toward the television.

Another report is playing.

Another attack.

Another victim.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I've seen this before.”

Henry doesn't understand.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“What do you mean?”

Michael looks down.

His voice becomes quieter.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“When my father was in the hospital...”

He stops.

Alex looks at him.

Michael takes a breath.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I kept thinking someone would come and fix everything.”

Silence.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“They didn't.”

Henry sits beside him.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“Michael...”

Michael looks toward the door.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“I don't want to make that mistake again.”

Henry understands.

**HENRY RIVERS:**
“We're not leaving you.”

Alex nods.

**ALEX BROOKS:**
“None of us are.”

Michael looks at both of them.

For the first time that night...

he looks a little less alone.

Then—

A loud crash comes from somewhere outside.

**BANG!**

All three freeze.

Michael immediately stands.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“Lights off.”

Alex switches them off.

The apartment falls completely dark.

They listen.

Nothing.

Then—

A distant scream.

Michael looks toward the window.

His expression hardens.

**MICHAEL CARTER:**
“It's spreading.”

---

### 🌃 NEW YORK — LATE NIGHT

The city remains under lockdown.

Most buildings are dark.

Most streets are empty.

But in different parts of the city...

the infected continue moving.

One attacks a man.

**BITE.**

Another attacks a woman.

**BITE.**

Another chases someone through an alley.

**BITE.**

Another disappears into a subway entrance.

The chain continues.

Slowly.

Quietly.

Relentlessly.

---

### 🧬 SARAH'S SCREEN

The New York map contains more markers.

Sarah watches the numbers.

> **ACTIVE INFECTED: 18**

She doesn't smile.

She doesn't panic.

She simply stares.

Then another line appears:

> **NEW TRANSMISSIONS: INCREASING**

Sarah whispers—

**SARAH SMITH:**
“It's only the beginning.”

---

### 🌑 FINAL MOMENTS

Back in New York...

A hospital monitor continues beeping.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

A newly bitten patient opens his eyes.

He looks confused.

His infection mark has spread.

Across the city...

another bitten person begins sweating.

Another develops a fever.

Another becomes unconscious.

And somewhere in the darkness...

the infected continue walking.

New York has not fallen.

Not yet.

But the chain has begun.

### CUT TO BLACK.

# END OF PART 8 — EPISODE 7



# 🌎 GLOBAL OUTBREAK

## EPISODE 7 — THE WORLD BREAKS

### PART 9 — LONDON

---

### 🇬🇧 LONDON — EVENING

The city is busy.

People leave work.

Buses move through traffic.

Taxis pass through crowded streets.

The Underground is packed.

Everything looks normal.

For now.

A man walks along the pavement while checking his phone.

He suddenly hears shouting behind him.

He turns.

A pale man is running through the crowd.

People move out of his way.

**WOMAN:**
“What's wrong with him?”

The pale man suddenly stops.

He looks around.

His eyes are beginning to turn red.

Then—

He sees a man standing nearby.

The infected charges.

**MAN:**
“HEY!”

The infected grabs him.

**BITE.**

The man screams.

People panic.

The infected releases him and disappears into the crowd.

---

### 🚨 LONDON — FIRST RESPONSE

A woman immediately calls the police.

**WOMAN:**
“Someone's been attacked!”

Two police officers arrive.

**OFFICER 1:**
“Where is the attacker?”

She points down the street.

**WOMAN:**
“He ran that way!”

Officer 1 looks toward the crowd.

**OFFICER 2:**
“Stay here.”

The officers run after him.

---

### 🩸 THE VICTIM

The bitten man sits against a wall.

A stranger kneels beside him.

**STRANGER:**
“Are you okay?”

The man looks at his shoulder.

Blood is running down his shirt.

**MAN:**
“I... I don't know.”

A siren approaches.

An ambulance stops nearby.

Paramedics rush over.

**PARAMEDIC:**
“What happened?”

**MAN:**
“He bit me.”

The paramedic looks at the wound.

**PARAMEDIC:**
“Alright. We're taking you to hospital.”

---

### 🚔 LONDON — THE CHASE

The two officers run through a side street.

They spot the infected.

**OFFICER 1:**
“There!”

The infected turns.

He stares at them.

**OFFICER 2:**
“Police! Stop!”

The infected suddenly charges.

**OFFICER 1:**
“MOVE!”

The officers step aside.

The infected runs past them.

Officer 1 turns around.

**OFFICER 1:**
“After him!”

They chase him.

The infected disappears around a corner.

The officers stop.

He's gone.

---

### 🏥 LONDON — HOSPITAL

The bitten man is placed in an emergency room.

A doctor examines his shoulder.

**DOCTOR:**
“Your temperature is normal.”

The man looks confused.

**MAN:**
“Then why do I feel so strange?”

The doctor checks his pulse.

**DOCTOR:**
“Your heart rate is elevated.”

A nurse prepares medical equipment.

**NURSE:**
“We'll keep him under observation.”

The man closes his eyes.

His breathing becomes heavier.

---

### 🏥 LATER

The hospital is quiet.

The bitten man lies motionless.

The heart monitor continues.

**BEEP... BEEP... BEEP...**

The doctor watches the screen.

The heartbeat begins slowing.

**BEEP...**

**DOCTOR:**
“His heart rate is dropping.”

**BEEP...**

The nurse looks at the monitor.

**BEEP...**

Then—

**FLATLINE.**

Everyone freezes.

**NURSE:**
“Cardiac arrest!”

The doctors rush toward him.

---

### 🧬 FIVE MINUTES LATER

The monitor suddenly changes.

**BEEP.**

The doctors stop.

**BEEP.**

The man's fingers twitch.

His eyes slowly open.

They are—

**RED.**

The doctor takes a step backwards.

**DOCTOR:**
“Everyone, move away.”

The man suddenly sits upright.

**NURSE:**
“Doctor—”

He lunges forward.

**DOCTOR:**
“RUN!”

The medical staff scatter.

The infected crashes into a medical cart.

He gets back up.

Then charges toward the door.

---

### 🚨 LONDON HOSPITAL — CORRIDOR

The infected bursts into the corridor.

Patients scream.

A nurse runs away.

Two security officers arrive.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“Stay back!”

The infected charges.

One officer tries to restrain him.

The infected grabs his arm.

**BITE.**

The officer screams.

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“AAAH!”

The second officer pulls him away.

The infected escapes through an emergency exit.

---

### 🇬🇧 LONDON — STREET

The infected runs into the city.

People stare.

A cyclist notices him.

**CYCLIST:**
“What's happening?”

The infected suddenly changes direction.

He charges toward the cyclist.

The cyclist drops his bicycle and runs.

**CYCLIST:**
“HELP!”

The infected catches up.

**BITE.**

The cyclist screams.

People begin running.

Cars stop.

A driver immediately calls the police.

---

### 🚨 LONDON — POLICE CONTROL ROOM

Phones are ringing.

**DISPATCHER:**
“Emergency services. What's your location?”

**CALLER:**
“Someone is attacking people!”

Another line rings.

**DISPATCHER 2:**
“Emergency services.”

**CALLER 2:**
“There was another attack near the hospital!”

The two dispatchers look at each other.

Another call comes through.

Then another.

But there are still only a handful of incidents.

The city hasn't collapsed.

Not yet.

---

### 🏥 LONDON — HOSPITAL

The bitten security officer sits inside an emergency room.

A doctor examines him.

**DOCTOR:**
“Your temperature is normal.”

**SECURITY OFFICER:**
“So I'm okay?”

The doctor pauses.

**DOCTOR:**
“We'll keep monitoring you.”

The officer looks at his wound.

A tiny dark mark begins spreading around it.

---

### 🌃 LONDON — NIGHT

The infected continues moving through the streets.

The cyclist lies in an ambulance.

His eyes are closed.

His breathing becomes heavier.

Meanwhile, the first bitten man inside the hospital is already gone.

Now there are new victims.

But the chain is still small.

**One infected.**

**One bite.**

**One new infected.**

Then another bite.

---

### 📺 LONDON — NEWS STATION

A reporter watches footage from the hospital.

Police vehicles.

Ambulances.

People running.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“Authorities are investigating several violent incidents across London tonight.”

A producer speaks from behind the cameras.

**PRODUCER:**
“Do we know if they're connected?”

The reporter looks at the footage again.

**NEWS ANCHOR:**
“Not yet.”

She turns toward the screen.

A frame shows the infected man's pale face.

Red eyes.

An infection mark.

The reporter's expression changes.

---

### 🧬 UNKNOWN LOCATION — SARAH

Sarah watches her computer.

A new location appears on the map.

> **LONDON — CASES DETECTED**

She studies the information.

Another marker appears.

Then another.

Sarah remains silent.

---

### 🇬🇧 LONDON — FINAL MOMENTS

The city is still functioning.

Buses are still moving.

Lights are still on.

People are still walking home.

Most of London has no idea what is happening.

But somewhere inside the city...

A bitten officer begins sweating.

A cyclist lies unconscious.

Another victim begins showing symptoms.

And an infected person is still moving through the streets.

The outbreak has not taken London.

**It has only entered it.**

### CUT TO BLACK.
